### [1] [Operator Algebras and Their Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12478)
*Vitalii Shpakivskyi*

Main category: math.RA

TL;DR: 该论文引入一类新的交换结合代数（commutative associative algebras），其结构常数（structural constants）为作用于线性拓扑空间（linear topological space）的线性算子，并在此基础上建立单演函数（monogenic functions）理论的类比框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原文摘要未明确阐述研究动机或背景问题，仅以"引入新一类代数"作为出发点，缺乏对既有理论缺口或应用需求的直接说明。

Method: 核心方法是将代数的结构常数推广为线性拓扑空间上的线性算子，并为此类代数发展单演函数理论的类比版本；通过分析单演函数的各分量，导出其满足的二阶算子微分方程（second-order operator-differential equations）。

Result: 证明单演函数的各个分量满足二阶算子微分方程，并表明该框架为求解多种偏微分方程（partial differential equations）与积分微分方程（integro-differential equations）提供了构造性方法（constructive method）。

Conclusion: 该代数与单演函数类比构成求解偏微分及积分微分方程的构造性工具；文中给出了若干示例以作说明，但摘要未提供具体结论性成果或适用范围限制。

Abstract: We introduce a new class of commutative associative algebras whose structural constants are linear operators acting in a linear topological space. An analogue of the theory of monogenic functions is developed for such algebras. It is shown that the components of monogenic functions satisfy second-order operator-differential equations. This approach provides a constructive method for obtaining solutions of various partial differential and integro-differential equations. Several illustrative examples are presented.

</details>

### [2] [The Stone-Wales trefoil: a new topological defect in graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13410)
*M. V. Kondrin,Y. B. Lebed,V. V. Brazhkin*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文指出石墨（graphite）高温比热（specific heat）显著偏离"3R"杜隆-珀蒂定律（Dulong-Petite law），并尝试以低形成能缺陷的累积加以解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨高温比热明显偏离经典的"3R"杜隆-珀蒂定律（Dulong-Petite law），该反常现象需要机制层面的解释。

Method: 基于密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算，提出一种可能拓扑缺陷（topological defects）候选结构，并考察其能量。

Result: 该反常可由形成能（formation energy）低至1.45 eV的缺陷累积来解释；所提出的拓扑缺陷候选其能量与生长激活能（growth activation energy）相当。

Conclusion: 缺陷累积可能是石墨高温比热偏离"3R"杜隆-珀蒂定律的成因，且所提拓扑缺陷在能量上具备作为候选的合理性。

Abstract: Specific heat of graphite at high temperatures demonstrates significant deviation from the ``3R'' Dulong-Petite law. It can be explained by accumulation of defects with formation energy as low as 1.45 eV. Based on the density functional theory calculations, we propose a possible candidate of topological defects, whose energy is comparable to the growth activation energy.

</details>

### [3] [Ultrafast laser-stimulated field emission from a metal: a non-equilibrium Green's functions approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13251)
*Luciano Jacopo D'Onofrio,Adolfo Avella,Martin Eckstein*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文提出基于非平衡格林函数（non-equilibrium Green's function）的理论框架，在一维模型中模拟强驱动金属纳米针尖-真空界面的电子动力学，揭示多体关联效应对远场发射谱的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超快激光辅助金属纳米针尖场发射（field emission）是产生相干电子脉冲的重要途径，但现有理论模型常忽略发射体电子结构中的多体强关联效应及其对光场的响应。

Method: 采用非平衡格林函数形式，在一维模型中处理强驱动物质-真空界面；将针尖-真空耦合作微扰处理，通过场缀饰真空传播子（field-dressed vacuum propagators）描述发射体占据态局域态密度的过滤；以第二玻恩近似（second-Born approximation）引入电子-电子相互作用；并自洽求解界面处的戴森方程（Dyson equation）。

Result: 强场发射与多光子发射（multi-photon emission）区间的相互作用可描述为发射体占据态局域态密度经场缀饰真空传播子的过滤；关联导致的电子结构修正直接传递至远场谱；自洽求解戴森方程揭示出由光场驱动的动态反作用（dynamic back-action）区，其特征为发射体的周期性极化，并重塑远场谱。

Conclusion: 非平衡格林函数方法为探索和分析关联电子的场发射提供了一个通用框架，能够统一刻画强场发射、多光子发射、关联效应与动态反作用对远场谱的影响。

Abstract: Ultrafast laser-assisted field emission from metallic nanotips is a promising route for generating coherent electron pulses. However, existing theoretical models often neglect many-body strong correlation effects in the emitter's electronic structure and its response to the optical field. In this work, we propose a theoretical framework based on the non-equilibrium Green's function formalism to simulate the out-of-equilibrium electron dynamics across a strongly driven matter-vacuum interface, in a one-dimensional model. Treating the tip-vacuum coupling perturbatively, we find that the interplay between strong-field emission and multi-photon emission regimes can be described in terms of a filtering of the emitter's local density of occupied states through field-dressed vacuum propagators. By including electron-electron interactions via the second-Born approximation, we show that correlation-induced modifications in the electronic structure are directly transferred to the far-field spectrum. Finally, by self-consistently solving the Dyson equation at the interface, we uncover a dynamic back-action regime driven by the optical field, characterized by a periodic polarization of the emitter, which reshapes the far-field spectrum. This shows that the non-equilibrium Green's functions approach provides a versatile framework for exploring and analyzing field emission of correlated electrons.

</details>

### [4] [Predicting the Final Stationary State of Sheared 2D Euler Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13615)
*Renato Pakter,Yan Levin*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一个非平衡统计力学（non-equilibrium statistical mechanics）框架，用于预测剪切二维欧拉流体（sheared 2D Euler fluid）的渐近涡度分布，并从理论上导出“核-晕”（core-halo）结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开尔文-亥姆霍兹不稳定性（Kelvin-Helmholtz instability）的线性动力学已较为清楚，但其流体最终弛豫到的定常状态仍属难题。作者旨在回答剪切流体弛豫终态这一未决问题。

Method: 通过为弗拉索夫方程（Vlasov equation）的解构造自洽拟设（self-consistent ansatz），在不显式求解含时欧拉方程（time-dependent Euler equations）的前提下确定定常流函数（stationary stream function）。

Result: 弛豫导致出现粒子数反转的核（population-inverted core），其中高能态被完全占据；其外围是位于位形空间（configuration space）低能区的稀疏涡旋晕（tenuous halo of vortices）。

Conclusion: 核-晕结构（core-halo structure）是该系统演化的稳健统计结果，为理解无粘二维流体（inviscid 2D fluids）中剪切诱导湍流（shear-induced turbulence）的饱和提供了普适框架。

Abstract: The Kelvin-Helmholtz (KH) instability is a fundamental signature of the natural world, manifesting in phenomena ranging from atmospheric clouds to the iconic turbulence of Jupiter's Great Red Spot. While the linear dynamics of this instability is well-understood, the ultimate fate of the fluid --- the final stationary state to which it relaxes --- remains a formidable challenge. In this paper, we present a general non-equilibrium statistical mechanics framework that allows us to predict the asymptotic vorticity distribution of a sheared 2D Euler fluid. By constructing a self-consistent ansatz for the solution of the Vlasov equation, we determine the stationary stream function without explicitly solving the time-dependent Euler equations. We demonstrate that the relaxation leads to a population-inverted core, where high-energy states are fully occupied, surrounded by a tenuous halo of vortices in the lower-energy regions of configuration space. This result establishes the core-halo structure as a robust statistical consequence of the system's evolution, offering a universal framework for understanding the saturation of shear-induced turbulence in inviscid 2D fluids.

</details>

### [5] [On the notion of kinetic energy in a spacetime of maximal proper acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12510)
*Ricardo Gallego Torromé*

Main category: physics.gen-ph

TL;DR: 本文表明，在具有最大固有加速度（maximal proper acceleration）的时空中，粒子可在减速的同时增加动能；作者以一维电腔类比模型阐释该效应，并据此提出速度与加速度在能量意义上的等价性，以及一个超光速动力学扇区的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察时空存在最大固有加速度（maximal proper acceleration）这一设定下的粒子动力学后果，特别是能量学层面速度与加速度之间的关系，并重塑经典层面光速与最大固有加速度的角色。

Method: 方法上采用经典一维电腔式方案（1-dimensional electric cavity-like scheme）进行说明，并在高阶射流时空几何（higher order jet spacetime geometry）框架中表述所提出的等价关系，其形式类似于玻恩互反原理（Born reciprocity principle）。

Result: 结果显示：粒子可以减速并同时增加动能；由此暗示速度与加速度在能量视角下等价；理论中还存在一个动力学超光速扇区（dynamical superluminal sector），对应正质量点粒子，具有超光速速度且固有加速度大于最大固有加速度。

Conclusion: 结论是：在经典层面，光速与最大固有加速度仅充当两个动力学扇区的分离条件（separation conditions），而非绝对界限。

Abstract: It is shown in this work that in spacetimes of maximal proper acceleration there are situations where a particle decelerates and simultaneously increases its kinetic energy. To illustrate the effect, a classical $1$-dimensional electric cavity-like scheme is discussed. The gain in kinetic energy by deceleration suggests an equivalence between velocity and acceleration from the energetic point of view. Such an equivalence is stated in a form resembling Born reciprocity principle but formulated in the framework of higher order jet spacetime geometry. We also show the existence in the theory of a dynamical superluminal sector for point particles with positive mass and characterized by superluminal speed and a proper acceleration larger than the maximal proper acceleration. Therefore, the speed of light and the maximal proper acceleration become only {\it separation conditions} at the classical level of the two dynamical sectors.

</details>

### [6] [Learning Schwarzschild Geodesic Motion with Lagrangian Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13234)
*Sukru Caglar,Sena Kalabalik,Abdullah Umut Hamzaogullari,Arkadas Ozakin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作首次成功将拉格朗日神经网络（Lagrangian Neural Networks, LNNs）训练于史瓦西测地线（Schwarzschild geodesics）任务，通过解耦的位置—速度归一化（decoupled position--velocity normalization）与基于最内稳定圆轨道（innermost stable circular orbit, ISCO）结构的能量约束对数均匀采样策略，实现对弯曲时空中测地线动力学的可靠学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LNNs 通过参数化标量拉格朗日量并经由欧拉—拉格朗日方程（Euler--Lagrange equation）导出加速度，具备变分归纳偏置（variational inductive bias），物理一致性优于纯数据驱动模型；然而其在广义相对论（general relativistic）设定中的扩展仍将史瓦西测地线留为未解难题。训练复杂相对论系统面临动力学多样性、数值不稳定性以及难以充分采样物理相关且多样的轨道区域等障碍。

Method: 提出两项协同改进：其一为解耦的位置—速度归一化方案，对每个坐标及其时间导数独立重缩放，并重加权加速度目标以平衡各通道贡献；其二为受最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）结构启发的能量约束、对数均匀采样数据集生成策略。

Result: 模型准确复现近日点进动（perihelion precession）与相对论时间膨胀（relativistic time dilation）；在围绕中心质量完成多圈轨道后，能量与角动量守恒误差仍在百分之几以内；并在近场、中场与远场三类区域的八种轨道场景中预测出轨道轨迹。

Conclusion: 结果表明，辅以物理动机的归一化与采样策略后，LNNs 能够可靠学习弯曲时空中的测地线动力学；该工作首次实现 LNNs 在史瓦西测地线上的成功训练，为复杂相对论系统的数据驱动建模提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Lagrangian Neural Networks (LNNs) learn dynamics from trajectory data by parameterizing a scalar Lagrangian and deriving accelerations through the Euler--Lagrange equation, providing a variational inductive bias that improves physical consistency over purely data-driven models. LNNs have recently been extended to general relativistic settings, leaving Schwarzschild geodesics as an open challenge. Training on complex relativistic systems is hindered by the diversity of the dynamics, as well as numerical instabilities and the difficulty of adequately sampling physically relevant, diverse orbital regimes. We introduce two coordinated improvements: a decoupled position--velocity normalization scheme that independently rescales each coordinate and its time derivatives, reweighting acceleration targets to balance contributions across all channels; and an energy-constrained log-uniformly sampled dataset generation strategy informed by the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) structure. With these improvements, our model accurately reproduces perihelion precession and relativistic time dilation, conserves energy and angular momentum to within a few percent even after completing multiple orbits around the central mass and predicts orbital trajectories across eight orbital scenarios in close, medium, and far-field regimes. These results establish that LNNs, augmented with physically motivated normalization and sampling, can reliably learn geodesic dynamics in curved spacetime. Our work also represents the first successful training of LNNs on Schwarzschild geodesics and offers a promising direction for data-driven modeling of complex relativistic systems.

</details>


### [7] [An all-transistor physical-learning transformer can be trained to perform in-context learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12571)
*Michael C. Abbott,Benjamin B. Machta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作通过引入额外电路元件恢复双向信息流，构建了基于亚阈值 MOSFET 指数电流律的注意力机制电路，并在仿真中实现了简单的上下文学习任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数基于平衡传播（equilibrium propagation）或耦合学习（coupled learning）训练的系统与多层感知机（multi-layer perceptron）同属一类，难以学习 transformer 擅长的联想任务。其根源在于平衡传播依赖输入与输出间的双向信息流，而更复杂的非线性系统（如执行乘法以表达点积注意力者）会破坏反向流。

Method: 在双联（dyadic）电路中显式引入额外的电路元件以恢复双向流，并利用亚阈值 MOSFET（sub-threshold MOSFET）器件的指数电流律构建注意力机制。使用一种基于 softmax 所用同一 MOSFET 子电路的新型浮动电路元件，生成适配交叉熵损失（cross-entropy loss）的 nudge；电阻参数则用 Lion 优化器（Lion optimiser）训练。

Result: 仿真显示，尽管所用反向元件只能传递近似梯度（approximate gradient），该电路仍可被训练完成一个简单的上下文学习（in-context learning）任务。训练成功依赖交叉熵损失而非均方误差（mean squared error）。

Conclusion: 在双联电路中恢复双向信息流，并结合交叉熵损失与浮动电路元件，可使物理注意力电路完成上下文学习。Lion 优化器可通过每参数一个电容存储过往梯度估计的局部记忆来实现。

Abstract: Most systems trained by equilibrium propagation or coupled learning are in the same class as multi-layer perceptrons: while they may be universal function approximators, they struggle to learn associative tasks which are easy for transformers. Equilibrium propagation relies on a bidirectional flow of information through the physical system between input and output. The reverse flow is broken by more complex nonlinear systems, such as those performing multiplication which is needed to express dot-product attention. Here we explicitly restore this bidirectional flow by introducing extra circuit elements for this purpose into a double or dyadic circuit. We then use these ideas to construct an attention mechanism exploiting the exponential current law of sub-threshold MOSFET devices. We show that in simulations this circuit can be trained to perform a simple in-context learning task, despite using realistic reverse components which transmit only an approximate gradient. For this training to succeed we need cross-entropy loss (not mean squared error). We generate nudges appropriate for this loss using a novel floating circuit element, based on the same MOSFET sub-circuit used for softmax. We train the resistors in this circuit using the Lion optimiser, which can be implemented using one capacitor per parameter to store local memory of past gradient estimates.

</details>

### [8] [Aubry-André transition in photonic time quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13538)
*Shuhang Chen,Qi Zhu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究证明光子时间准晶（photonic time quasicrystals）中存在 Aubry-André 转变的时间对应物，其转变发生在动量空间的迁移率边（mobility edges）而非能量空间，从而将 Aubry-André 转变从空间晶格拓展至时间域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维中无序势导致的 Anderson 局域化（Anderson localization）在任何强度下均发生，而 Aubry-André 模型中准周期势导致的局域化则需在临界强度处发生转变。光子时间晶体（photonic time crystals）已展现 Anderson 局域化的时间对应物（光停止传播并在时间上增长），但光子时间准晶中是否存在 Aubry-André 转变的时间对应物此前仍为开放问题。

Method: 研究考察了脉冲调制（impulsive modulation）情形，其中场精确满足自对偶的 almost-Mathieu 方程（self-dual almost-Mathieu equation）；对于一般解析调制（general analytic modulations），则借助 Avila 全局理论（Avila's global theory）揭示迁移率边形成的条件。

Result: 光子时间准晶中确实存在 Aubry-André 转变，且该转变发生在动量（momentum）空间中的迁移率边，而非能量空间。这些边缘即使在脉冲调制下也会出现，其形成条件可由 Avila 全局理论给出。

Conclusion: Aubry-André 转变由空间晶格延伸至时间域，在时间域中依据波数（wave number）将光区分为传播的分量与停止并增长的分量。

Abstract: In one dimension, Anderson localization by a disordered potential sets in at any strength, whereas localization by a quasiperiodic potential in the Aubry-André model occurs through a transition at a critical strength. Disordered photonic time crystals exhibit a temporal counterpart of this Anderson localization, in which light, instead of decaying in space, stops propagating and grows in time. Whether a counterpart of the Aubry-André transition exists in photonic time quasicrystals remains an open question. Here we show that it does, and that the transition occurs at mobility edges in momentum rather than in energy. These edges arise even under impulsive modulation, where the field obeys the self-dual almost-Mathieu equation exactly, and for general analytic modulations Avila's global theory reveals the condition under which they form. The Aubry-André transition thus extends from spatial lattices to the time domain, where it sorts light by wave number into components that propagate and components that stop and grow.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [9] [On the Cohomology Classes of Certain Eisenstein-Kronecker Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13581)
*Zhixuan Sheng,Jie Zhou*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文引入模权分级（modular weight grading），研究一类 Eisenstein-Kronecker 形式的 de Rham 上同调（de Rham cohomology）与 Deligne 分解（Deligne splitting），并借此计算给出椭圆曲线 Gromov-Witten 不变量生成级数的一个特定上同调类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅指出目标对象为一类 Eisenstein-Kronecker 形式，并暗示该类形式的上同调结构与椭圆曲线 Gromov-Witten 不变量的生成级数存在联系。属推断受限，故不作扩展。

The Schwarzschild model is excluded for GRO J1655-40 and XTE J1550-564, with a difference in the Akaike information criterion of about 110.

Abstract: Universal horizons trap even arbitrarily fast signals, providing a test of black-hole thermodynamics in theories with a preferred time direction. In Einstein--aether theory, this direction is defined by a dynamical field, the aether, that can remain non-aligned relative to stationary time at infinity. Here, we resolve this issue for an exact family of static, spherically symmetric solutions. Using the covariant phase-space formalism, we identify the asymptotic tilt and the decaying part of the aether field as a boundary source and its conjugate response. Allowing both to vary independently obstructs the definition of an energy that generates time translations. Fixing either quantity, or imposing a relation between them, restores such an energy, while externally varying the boundary data introduces work into the first law. The energies obtained by fixing the source or its response are related by a Legendre transformation. At the universal horizon, heat includes both the metric area contribution and an irreducible aether energy flux. The resulting balance separates work at infinity from horizon heat and explains why the physical temperature cannot generally be inferred from the coefficient of an area variation in the black-hole mass.

</details>

Abstract: The Boltzmann flux reflectivity used in quantum-horizon models does not by itself define a causal black-hole boundary response. We construct causal completions that preserve the modulus on any prescribed finite band. Second, we prove a low-frequency outer-phase expansion for a class of completions with fixed profiles and a common Boltzmann slope, including an exact-band completion and a smooth crossover completion. The logarithmic coefficient is common to this class, while the linear constant is an explicit functional of the completion. When the fixed proper-frame cutoff is redshifted to the matching surface and the local temperature is the Tolman redshift of the Hawking temperature, the outer phase cancels the leading logarithmic dependence of the propagation phase on the matching distance. The geometric remainder is quadratic in the proper distance, while a separate dispersive remainder depends on the completion. This is a low-energy comparison of specified response families, not an exact matching invariance or a phase fixed by causality alone. In an axial Schwarzschild example at a fixed matching plane, accounting for the predicted linear-delay difference reduces the maximum relative cavity-factor difference between two cutoffs from about $21\%$ to $0.11\%$ on the sampled frequency band. A passive quantum completion supplies the associated absorption-noise relation and checks the consistency of the asymptotic output.

</details>

The horizon then loses 28% of its area as it swallows the phantom field, and by $t=100$ lies within 1.4% of the Schwarzschild radius of the pair's mass, still shrinking. Method: 该学派面向具备广义相对论（general relativity）背景的高年级本科生与研究生，通过系列讲座形式覆盖引力波物理的多类基础专题；讲义以书面形式整理并对外发布。

Result: 讲座专题涵盖运动理论（theory of motion）、量子物理（quantum physics）带来的新挑战，以及黑洞天体物理（astrophysics of black holes）与面向引力波探测器的波源建模（source modeling）。

Conclusion: 讲义意在传承尼尔斯·玻尔研究所A报告厅所象征的量子革命精神，鼓励学生在引力物理中挑战既有范式，并将这些新思想开放给整个科学界。

Abstract: Understanding the gravitational interaction is one of the great scientific endeavors of the 21st century. The Nobel Prize-winning discovery of gravitational waves in 2015, and subsequent detections by the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA Collaboration, have marked a revolutionary step forward in physics and astrophysics by opening an entirely new window for exploring the Universe. Future upgrades to these detectors, along with upcoming facilities such as LISA, the Einstein Telescope, and radio and optical instruments, promise other breakthrough discoveries that the next generation of scientists will need to interpret. The School on Gravity (22--26 June 2026, Copenhagen) aimed to introduce junior scientists--graduate students and advanced undergraduates with a background in general relativity--to a wide range of foundational topics in the exciting new field of gravitational-wave physics. Topics spanned from the theory of motion, to new challenges introduced by quantum physics, to the astrophysics of black holes and source modeling for gravitational-wave detectors. The School on Gravity took place at the legendary Auditorium A of the Niels Bohr Institute, inviting students and lecturers to challenge current paradigms in gravitational physics in the spirit of the quantum revolution that unfolded in this very room. These lecture notes aim at opening up those new ideas presented in Auditorium A to the rest of the scientific community.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 尽管准粒子与两体哈密顿量具有完全相同的 SSG 对称性，电子能带结构中受对称性保护的简并可在激子谱的特定动量处被内禀解除；该劈裂仅源于激子的多体本质，无需自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）或非共线磁序。由此预言了一类新的自旋劈裂激子准粒子——alter-exciton，并给出其在交替磁体系中的特征色散。

Conclusion: 激子可作为探测隐藏自旋空间对称性的灵敏探针，并为共线磁体中对称性工程化的多体现象开辟新途径。

Abstract: Magnetism provides a fertile platform for discovering emergent quantum phenomena governed by symmetry. Recent developments of spin-space-group (SSG) theory have revealed altermagnets as a distinct class of nonrelativistic collinear magnets with unconventional spin-dependent electronic structures. Here, we demonstrate that altermagnetic symmetry imposes fundamentally different constraints on electronic and excitonic excitations. Although the quasiparticle and two-body Hamiltonians possess identical SSG symmetries, we find that symmetry-protected degeneracies present in the electronic band structure can be intrinsically lifted in the exciton spectrum at specific momenta. This splitting arises solely from the many-body nature of excitons and does not require spin-orbit coupling or noncollinear magnetic order. Combining symmetry analysis with many-body perturbation theory, we predict a novel class of spin-split excitonic quasiparticles, termed alter-excitons, and reveal their characteristic dispersions in altermagnetic systems.   <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 非微扰地确认了德西特空间轻标量场红外发散被随机形式正确复求和；该方法将德西特空间开放给格点蒙特卡洛方法。

Abstract: We report the first Monte Carlo simulation of quantum field theory on Euclidean de Sitter space, $S^4$. Rather than discretizing space, we use a spectral lattice based on spherical harmonics, keeping all symmetries exact at finite cutoff. For a massless $λφ^4$ theory, the zero-mode distribution reproduces the Starobinsky-Yokoyama equilibrium at weak coupling, and deviations closely follow the one-loop renormalization of $λ$. At strong bare coupling, the renormalized quartic coupling saturates near the ceiling of the one-loop renormalization-group flow. This saturation is the de Sitter counterpart of the triviality mechanism that bounds the Higgs mass in flat space. Our results confirm nonperturbatively that the infrared divergences of light scalars in de Sitter are correctly resummed by the stochastic formalism. More broadly, this work opens up de Sitter space to lattice Monte Carlo methods.

### [24] [GLSMs for non-polynomial deformations and generalized complete intersection Calabi-Yau manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12554)

The finite-angle comparison further suggests a projection-covariant identification of island and brane endpoints at linear order. ### [53] [Local Diagnostics of Learned Event Representations for Anomaly Detection at the HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13437)

Main category: cs.AI

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型强子对撞机（LHC）的新物理搜索需要模型无关（model-agnostic）策略，而基于学习到的低维表示的异常检测（anomaly detection）虽无需预先设定手工观测量，却牺牲了直接的物理解释性。为使异常检测进入发现流程，被标记事件必须被本地诊断，以区分潜在新物理与模型失配或探测器效应，并识别用于针对性后续搜索的观测量。

Method: 在 COLLIDE-2V（750 百万事件，模拟于真实高亮度 LHC 条件）上评估监督与自监督神经嵌入策略，异常分数取自在学习隐空间中训练的自编码器（autoencoder）的重构误差；并提出本地诊断流程，对标准模型（SM）隐空间拟合高斯混合模型（Gaussian Mixture Model），在无过程标签条件下通过高维观测量刻画各分量、定位被标记事件并按本地判别力对观测量排序。

Result: 在所考虑的配置与基准信号中，利用模拟过程标签的监督目标总体优于自监督对应方法；其最佳配置相较直接在运动学特征上训练的自编码器有所提升，在所有留出过程上以 10% 本底接受度下的信号效率均得到提高。观测量排序能够恢复留出过程的决定性运动学特征，而将异常分数与相同观测量相关联则量化了分数对其的追踪程度，揭示出最具判别力的观测量并非分数最紧密追踪的那一个。

Conclusion: 监督式神经嵌入配合基于高斯混合模型的本地诊断流程，可在保持模型无关搜索框架的同时恢复物理可解释性，为异常检测进入发现流程提供可行路径，并提示判别力与异常分数追踪度之间存在不完全一致。

Abstract: Anomaly detection, the identification of collision events that deviate from Standard Model expectations, is one of the most promising model-agnostic strategies for new-physics searches at the Large Hadron Collider. Searching for rare events in learned low-dimensional representations removes the need to commit to hand-crafted observables a priori, but sacrifices the immediate physical interpretation those observables provide. For anomaly detection to enter a discovery pipeline, flagged events must therefore be diagnosed locally, both to distinguish potential new physics from mismodeling or detector effects and to identify observables for targeted follow-up searches. We evaluate supervised and self-supervised neural embedding strategies on COLLIDE-2V, a 750-million-event dataset simulated under realistic High-Luminosity LHC conditions, using the reconstruction error of an autoencoder trained in the learned latent space as the anomaly score. Across the configurations and benchmark signals considered, supervised objectives, which exploit simulation process labels during training, generally outperform their self-supervised counterparts and, in their best configurations, improve on an autoencoder trained directly on kinematic features, raising the signal efficiency at 10% background acceptance on all held-out processes. We further introduce a local diagnostic pipeline that fits a Gaussian Mixture Model to the SM latent space, profiles each component through high-level observables without process labels, localizes the flagged events, and ranks observables by their local discriminating power. The ranking recovers the defining kinematics of the held-out processes, while correlating the anomaly score with the same observables quantifies how closely the score tracks them, revealing cases where the most discriminating observable is not the one the score follows most closely.

</details>

### [54] [Finiteness of planar central configurations of five bodies for all positive masses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13100)
*Tejasvi Singh Tomar*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该论文证明平面五体问题（planar five-body problem）对任意五个正质量在相似意义下仅有有限多个中心构型（central configurations），从而在平面五体情形下解决了 Smale 第六问题（Smale's sixth problem）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Smale 第六问题关注 n 体问题中中心构型数量的有限性；Albouy 与 Kaloshin 已证明在余维二（codimension two）的闭代数子集之外的质量取值下结论成立，本文旨在补齐任意正质量这一缺口，尤其是平面五体情形。

Method: 沿用 Albouy–Kaloshin 的方法：假设存在无穷多中心构型则会得到复解的代数曲线（algebraic curve），在曲线无穷远处互距的赋值（valuation）必须满足平衡条件。作者确定所有正质量下可能的赋值向量及相应质量条件；对四体形成零势能团簇（cluster of zero potential energy）的极限，通过在某一处展开势函数加以处理，从而排除相应图示；余下情形用计算机代数（computer algebra）精确分析可同时成立的质量条件集合，并以精确有理证书（exact rational certificates）验证平衡失效。

Result: 展开与证书排除了绝大多数图示，仅剩少数单参数质量族需单独处理。在过 $(1,4,4,4,4)$ 与 $(1,1,4,4,4)$ 的射线上证书不适用：第一条射线上复方程存在连续解（与 Albouy–Kaloshin 的观察一致）。作者在这两条射线上计数实中心构型，在保定向相似（orientation-preserving similarity）意义下分别为 306 个与 294 个。

Conclusion: 结论为平面五体问题对任意正质量仅有有限多个中心构型，Smale 第六问题在平面五体情形下得证；证书、校验程序及在 Lean 证明助手（Lean proof assistant）中的定理证明均已公开。

Abstract: We prove that for every choice of five positive masses the planar five-body problem has finitely many central configurations up to similarity. This settles Smale's sixth problem for five bodies in the plane. Albouy and Kaloshin proved it for masses outside a closed algebraic subset of codimension two. We follow their method. Infinitely many central configurations would give an algebraic curve of complex solutions. At the places at infinity of the curve the valuations of the mutual distances must balance. We determine the possible valuation vectors at all positive masses, together with the mass conditions under which each can occur. We treat limits in which four bodies form a cluster of zero potential energy by expanding the potential, which is constant along the curve, at a place. This excludes, at every positive mass, one of the two diagrams in which such clusters occur, and part of the other. The same expansion excludes one further diagram at every positive mass. What remains is an exact analysis, by computer algebra, of the sets of mass conditions that can hold together at positive masses. Exact rational certificates show that the balance fails under each of them, except on a few one-parameter families of masses, which we treat separately. On the rays through $(1,4,4,4,4)$ and $(1,1,4,4,4)$ the certificates do not apply. On the first ray they cannot apply: there the complex equations have a continuum of solutions, as Albouy and Kaloshin observed. On these rays we count the real central configurations: there are 306 and 294 up to orientation-preserving similarity. The certificates, the programs that check them and a proof of the theorem in the Lean proof assistant are available.

</details>

### [55] [Enhanced Coulomb pseudopotentials in high-pressure hydrides from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12563)
*Kieran Bozier,Chris J. Pickard*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作以第一性原理方法计算超导氢化物中的库仑参数 μ 与库仑赝势 μ*_ME，发现其远超经验范围，并归因于费米能级处的高态密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Eliashberg 超导理论（Eliashberg theory of superconductivity）中，库仑相互作用（Coulomb interactions）抑制声子介导的吸引并压低临界温度 Tc，但多数第一性原理预测仍沿用经验库仑赝势（Coulomb pseudopotential）μ*_ME（0.1–0.15），缺乏对 μ 与 μ*_ME 的从头计算。

Result: H3S、YSbH6、Mg2IrH6 等体系呈现强库仑排斥，由费米能级处大态密度 N_F 驱动；Mg2IrH6 得到 μ=0.62、μ*_ME≈0.39，相对标准 μ*_ME=0.16 使 Tc 被压低约 40 K。所获 μ*_ME 远高于常规范围，并在计入态密度能量依赖后消除了此前研究 Tc 估计中两倍的差异。在所有研究体系与压强下，μ 与 N_F 密切相关。

Conclusion: 常用 μ*_ME 取值会在费米能级处态密度较大的材料中高估 Tc，μ 随 N_F 变化的规律提示应重新评估此类体系的库仑赝势处理方式。

Abstract: In the Eliashberg theory of superconductivity, Coulomb interactions oppose the phonon-mediated attraction and suppress the superconducting critical temperature $T_{\mathrm{c}}$. However, most first-principles predictions continue to treat this repulsion with an empirical Coulomb pseudopotential $μ^*_{\text{ME}}$ in the range 0.1-0.15. Here we present first-principles calculations of the Coulomb parameter $μ$ and pseudopotential $μ^*_{\text{ME}}$ in superconducting hydrides across a range of pressures. The screened Coulomb interaction is evaluated at the random phase approximation level with the Mantel code. We demonstrate that several hydride systems, including H$_3$S, YSbH$_6$ and Mg$_2$IrH$_6$, have strong Coulomb repulsions driven by a large density of states at the Fermi energy, $N_{\mathrm{F}}$. For Mg$_2$IrH$_6$ we obtain $μ=0.62$ and $μ^*_{\text{ME}}\approx0.39$, leading to a 40 K suppression in $T_{\mathrm{c}}$ relative to a standard $μ^*_{\text{ME}}=0.16$. Our obtained $μ^*_{\text{ME}}$ is far above the conventional range and, accounting for the energy dependence of the density of states, resolves the factor-of-two discrepancy between $T_{\mathrm{c}}$ estimates of previous studies. Across all systems and pressures studied, we find that $μ$ closely tracks $N_{\mathrm{F}}$, suggesting that commonly used $μ^*_{\text{ME}}$ values lead to overestimated $T_{\mathrm{c}}$ in materials where the density of states at the Fermi energy is large.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维超导体系是凝聚态物理（condensed matter physics）的前沿方向，其独特物理性质与潜在应用驱动了对新型二维超导平台及高 T_c 机制的探索。该研究旨在揭示单层 ZrX₂ 作为二维电子化合物（electride）是否具备双极性超导电性（bipolar superconductivity）并阐明其调控因素。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），对单层 ZrX₂（X = Cl, Br, I）在电子掺杂与空穴掺杂条件下开展研究，评估其声子介导超导性；并通过与 InSe 和 LaBr₂ 的对比，分析决定空穴掺杂超导强度及竞争序（competing orders）性质的关键因素。

Result: 电子掺杂下，ZrBr₂ 在 0.01 e/f.u. 时 T_c = 30.0 K，ZrCl₂ 在 0.05 e/f.u. 时为 22.5 K，ZrI₂ 在 0.01 e/f.u. 时为 18.1 K；高 T_c 源于平带导带底（flat conduction band minimum）及其附近 Van Hove 奇点（Van Hove singularities）增强的态密度（density of states）。空穴掺杂需 0.2–0.25 h/f.u. 的较高浓度才能激活超导，T_c 约 2 K，并在更高掺杂水平趋于磁性失稳。对比 InSe 与 LaBr₂ 表明，多余电子的空间位置与轨道特征（orbital character）是决定空穴掺杂超导强度与竞争序性质的关键因素。

Conclusion: 单层 ZrX₂ 被确立为二维电子化合物中实现双极性超导的平台，并为高 T_c 超导体的平带工程（flat-band engineering）提供了见解。

Abstract: Two-dimensional (2D) superconducting systems have recently emerged as a frontier in condensed matter physics, driven by their unique physical properties and potential applications. Using first-principles calculations, we show that monolayer ZrX$_2$ (X = Cl, Br, I), which is a family of 2D semiconducting electrides, hosts phonon-mediated superconductivity under both electron and hole dopings. Electron doping in ZrX$_{2}$ can achieve high superconducting transition temperatures at very low concentrations, with ZrBr$_2$ reaching $T_c = 30.0$ K at 0.01 \textit{e}/f.u., followed by ZrCl$_2$ (22.5 K at 0.05 \textit{e}/f.u.) and ZrI$_2$ (18.1 K at 0.01 \textit{e}/f.u.), for which the high $T_c$ originates from the flat conduction band minimum, with Van Hove singularities nearby enhancing the density of states. In contrast, hole doping requires higher concentrations (0.2--0.25 \textit{h}/f.u.) to activate superconductivity, yielding $T_c \sim 2$ K, and approaches a magnetic instability at elevated doping levels. By comparing with InSe and LaBr$_2$, we identify the spatial location and orbital character of excess electrons as key factors determining the strength of hole-doped superconductivity and the nature of competing orders. These findings establish monolayer ZrX$_2$ as a platform for bipolar superconductivity in a 2D electride and provide insights into flat-band engineering for high-$T_c$ superconductors.

</details>

Conclusion: 交错磁性（altermagnetism）可作为在常规与复合超导相干性之间进行选择的微观机制，确立了独立电荷-4e相的理论实现。

Tuning the altermagnetic splitting suppresses the leading single-pair tunneling harmonic while retaining strong double-pair coupling, producing a finite charge-4e region between staggered and uniform charge-2e states. Using the complete temperature-dependent microscopic Josephson spectrum, we find short-range ordinary pair correlations alongside algebraic coherence of pairs of Cooper pairs. Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless scaling and a half-flux response further identify the charge-4e phase. Our results establish altermagnetism as a microscopic means to select between ordinary and composite superconducting coherence.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Practical ship design requires localized refinement and performance evaluation. This paper presents a reward-directed diffusion framework for localized hull optimization. Parametric masking during dataset generation restricts geometric changes to selected variables, including the bulbous bow and forebody, while preserving full-hull continuity. Surrogate-assisted sequential Monte Carlo sampling targets reduced predicted calm-water resistance, with stability retained through training data and displacement matched during screening. An automated viscous free-surface CFD pipeline, validated against measurements for two benchmark container ships, evaluates a generated variant and GenHull E, a hull refined by a naval architect within the same representation. At matched KRISO Container Ship (KCS) displacement, the generated variant has comparable total resistance at $F_n=0.195$ but 45-50% higher resistance at $F_n=0.250$ and $0.260$, missed by empirical screening. GenHull E achieves 6-8% lower total resistance up to $F_n=0.240$ but higher resistance above $F_n=0.250$ as opposing friction and pressure trends reverse the gains. Aggregating resistance across this crossover can conceal tradeoffs that emerge when friction and pressure components are evaluated separately. The assessment reveals an inability to represent a faired bulbous bow and a reward lacking viscous pressure and stability terms. These findings show how high-fidelity assessment informs improved representations and objectives for physically realizable generative designs.

</details>

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究非线性色散方程驻波（standing waves）对应椭圆型 ODE Δu+f(u)=0 的径向束缚态（radial bound states），在球对称流形与非线性项上证明指定节点面数目的存在性与模型非线性下的唯一性，并覆盖原点奇异的流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性 Schrödinger 与 Klein–Gordon 方程的驻波轮廓满足非线性椭圆 ODE，因此刻画该 ODE 的径向束缚态是理解此类方程驻波结构的基础问题，尤其在一般球对称流形及带奇点流形上尚需系统性结果。

Method: 作者针对形如 Δu+f(u)=0 的非线性椭圆 ODE，在一大类球对称流形（spherically symmetric manifolds）与非线性项 f 上，通过构造并分析具有指定节点面（nodal surfaces）数目的径向解来证明存在性；对模型非线性 f(u)=|u|^{p-1}u-u 在温和假设下进一步证明唯一性。

Result: 证明了径向束缚态的存在性，且其节点面数目可预先指定；对模型非线性 f(u)=|u|^{p-1}u-u，在温和假设下证明了此类束缚态的唯一性。存在性与唯一性结果均适用于部分在原点带奇点（singularity at the origin）的流形。

Conclusion: 该结果为非线性色散方程驻波在一般球对称流形（含原点奇异情形）上的径向束缚态提供了存在性与唯一性刻画，将节点结构纳入分类框架，并适用于广泛的非线性项。

Abstract: Standing waves $e^{iωt}u(x)$ of nonlinear dispersive equations, such as the nonlinear Schrödinger and Klein--Gordon equations, have a profile $u$ that solves a nonlinear elliptic ODE of the form $Δu+f(u)=0$. We prove the existence of radial bound states with a prescribed number of nodal surfaces for this equation, on a large class of spherically symmetric manifolds and nonlinearities $f$. Under mild assumptions, we prove uniqueness of such bound states for the model nonlinearity $f(u)=|u|^{p-1}u-u$. Both existence and uniqueness results apply to some manifolds with a singularity at the origin.

</details>

*Glal Bacho,Christof Melcher*

Main category: math.AP

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建结合竞争的二阶与一阶交换相互作用（exchange interactions）、并以外加磁场（external magnetic field）稳定化的能量泛函，将其化为从三维空间到二维球面（two-sphere）的映射变分问题，采用变分方法与同伦类分析。

Conclusion: 该模型在严格意义上支持具有非平凡 Hopf 指数的稳定场构型，其大场行为由一极限变分问题主导，集中尺度刻画了构型的核心结构。

Abstract: We study a continuum model of frustrated magnetism that supports particle-like field configurations with nontrivial Hopf index. The energy functional combines competing second and first order exchange interactions with a stabilizing external magnetic field and gives rise to a variational problem for maps from three-dimensional space into the two-sphere. We prove the existence of energy minimizers in infinitely many nontrivial homotopy classes. We further establish the existence of a unit-charge Hopfion in the regime of strong external fields. Finally, we analyze the corresponding large-field limit, identify the natural concentration scale, and prove strong convergence of suitably rescaled minimizers to a minimizer of a limiting variational problem.

</details>

*Bernadette Lessel*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文主张广义相对论（general relativity）教科书是重建其“复兴期”（Renaissance，1955年至1970年代中期）思想史的重要史料，其价值不亚于研究论文；并指出Lalli等人所识别的研究中心负责人几乎都各自撰写了代表其学派观点的教科书，论文还梳理了从1915—16年理论创立到1980年代的教科书简史以支撑该论点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有对广义相对论复兴期的历史研究多依赖研究论文，可能忽略教科书所提供的独特叙事视角；不同作者的教科书叙述可能存在显著差异，因而需要重新评估教科书的史料价值。

Method: 论证性（史学方法）研究：以广义相对论复兴期及其前夕出版的教科书为史料，比较其与同期研究论文叙事方式的差异；援引Lalli等人识别的研究中心及其负责人撰写教科书的现象作为经验观察；并梳理1915—16年至1980年代的教科书发展简史，将该现象置于领域整体演进脉络中。

Result: 教科书比研究论文更能提供关于该领域的叙事性叙述，构成见证塑造广义相对论之思想力量的独特证言；一个关键观察是，Lalli等人所识别的、作为广义相对论复兴期思想中心的各研究中心，其几乎所有主要研究者（principal investigators）最终都出版了与自身学派相关的教科书。

Conclusion: 教科书可作为重建广义相对论复兴期思想动态的史料来源，这一路径由教科书简史加以例示；该研究为理解1955年至1970年代中期该领域的思想力量提供了新的史学工具。

Abstract: This paper argues for the historical utility of general relativity textbooks written during, or shortly before, the Renaissance of general relativity. More so than research papers, textbooks offer a narrative account of the field--one that may differ substantially from the accounts of other authors addressing the same subject. Textbooks on general relativity therefore constitute a distinctive form of testimony to the intellectual forces that shaped general relativity, particularly during its reinvention period from 1955 to the mid-1970s. A second pillar of this argument is the observation that nearly all principal investigators at the research centers previously identified by Lalli et al.--centers that functioned as intellectual epicenters of the Renaissance of general relativity--eventually produced a textbook associated with their own school of thought. To situate this observation within the broader development of the field, the paper presents a concise history of general relativity textbooks from the theory's inception in 1915-16 to the 1980s. This history illustrates one way in which textbooks can serve as sources for reconstructing the intellectual dynamics of the Renaissance of general relativity.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 集成 hBN 的谐振器维持低介电损耗，低功率下内损耗角正切量级为 $10^{-6}$；结果显示 hBN 可在品质因子几乎不退化的情况下被加入常规超导量子比特叠层。

Conclusion: 该研究是迈向材料驱动策略的第一步，目标是通过二维材料的热学性质阻止衬底中激发的非热声子（athermal phonons）跨越超导体-衬底界面传输，从而消除固态量子系统中声子介导的相关误差。同一技术若应用于量子传感器，则可通过阻止声子进入无源金属薄膜来最大化声子收集。

Abstract: The integration of low-loss dielectrics into superconducting circuits is of importance to the functionality of quantum computers and sensors. In this work, we fabricate and measure niobium resonators atop few-layer, epitaxial hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) grown by Molecular Beam Epitaxy on a sapphire substrate and compare against a control counterpart without hBN. We conduct radiofrequency measurements which probe the responses to power, temperature, and temporal fluctuations of two-level systems. The resonators integrating hBN maintain low dielectric loss with a low-power internal loss tangent on the order of $10^{-6}$, and demonstrate hBN can be added to the conventional superconducting qubit stack with minimal degradation of the quality factor. This research represents a first step towards a materials-driven strategy to eliminate phonon-mediated correlated errors in solid-state quantum systems, which leverages the thermal properties of 2D materials to prevent athermal phonons excited in the device substrate from transmitting across the superconductor-substrate interface. The same technique applied to quantum sensors would maximize phonon collection by blocking phonons from entering passive metal films.

</details>


### [63] [Quantum-assisted rendezvous on hypercubes: a quantum Bernoulli noise approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12949)
*Ce Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在 d 维超立方体（hypercube）上研究单步双智能体量子行走（quantum-walk）框架下的量子辅助平衡会合（balanced rendezvous），以投影 t-球面分解（projective t-sphere resolution）为核心结构刻画有限维量子可达性，并给出最优策略的刚性定理（rigidity theorem）。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解量子辅助会合中有限维量子策略能否达到无信号基准（no-signalling benchmark）的值，并揭示其与经典刻画之间的关系。核心切入点是投影 t-球面分解这一结构，其标量类比可还原相应的经典刻画。

Method: 方法上，采用单步双智能体量子行走框架，两个智能体移动相同汉明距离（Hamming distance）并在共同中点相遇；通过投影 t-球面分解构造量子策略，并对无信号基准的有限维量子可达性进行刻画，进而建立最优有限维策略的刚性定理。

Result: 结果为：刻画了无信号基准的有限维量子可达性，基于投影球面分解构造了量子策略，并证明了最优有限维策略的刚性定理。同时发现最近邻（nearest-neighbor）更新与更高基数（higher-cardinality）更新之间存在显著差异：前者可能出现量子优势（quantum advantage），而后者中相应汉明球面几何排除了对无信号值的精确有限维可达性。

Conclusion: 结论指出，上述结果将量子行走动力学、汉明球面几何与量子会合联系起来，并在量子伯努利噪声（quantum Bernoulli noises, QBN）框架内统一理解。

Abstract: We study quantum-assisted balanced rendezvous on the $d$-dimensional hypercube in a one-step two-agent quantum-walk framework, where the two agents move the same Hamming distance and meet at a common midpoint. The key structure is a projective $t$-sphere resolution, which governs exact finite-dimensional quantum attainment. Its scalar analogue recovers the corresponding classical characterization. We characterize finite-dimensional quantum attainment of the no-signalling benchmark, construct quantum strategies from projective sphere resolutions, and establish a rigidity theorem for optimal finite-dimensional strategies. A marked difference appears between nearest-neighbor and higher-cardinality updates: quantum advantage can occur in the former, whereas in the latter the geometry of the relevant Hamming spheres rules out exact finite-dimensional attainment of the no-signalling value. Together, these results connect quantum-walk dynamics, Hamming-sphere geometry, and quantum rendezvous within the quantum Bernoulli noises (QBN) framework.

### [64] [Dissipative Majorana Chain---Anomalies and Non-Invertible Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12607)

For odd length, constructing mutually commuting ket- and bra-sector spin operators instead yields an Ising Lindbladian with a Kramers--Wannier duality defect. Abstract: Quantum error-correcting codes on graph geometries beyond regular euclidean lattices have emerged as a promising route toward robust quantum information processing. A major challenge in quantum error correction is the trade-off between favorable properties of codes constructed in high dimensionality and the need for locality in practical implementations on near-term hardware. Sparse intermediate geometries may combine the advantages of both, but how their structure governs quantum information dynamics and code performance remains unclear. Here we show that the spectral dimension $d_s$, defined from the low-energy spectrum of the graph Laplacian, is the universal parameter connecting these properties. Using Floquet random Clifford circuits on two distinct sparse graph architectures with tunable spectral dimension, we find that $d_s$ governs both quantum information spreading and the scaling of the resulting quantum error-correcting code distance. We identify a geometry-induced entanglement phase transition from localized area-law to ergodic volume-law, whose universality is determined by $d_s$ and exhibits long-range percolation criticality. In the ergodic phase, the circuit acts as a CSS code encoder whose code distance is set by the competition between two logical sectors --- the cost of severing the graph and the length of its shortest winding cycle --- which connectivity pushes in opposite directions. Abstract: Frustrated magnets host rich emergent behavior because competing interactions generate dense manifolds of low-energy states. The Ising Shastry-Sutherland model is a paradigmatic setting, motivated experimentally by Ising-like rare-earth tetraborides such as TmB$_4$ and ErB$_4$, whose field-induced magnetization plateaus have motivated extensive microscopic modeling. Yet how tunable quantum fluctuations reorganize this material-motivated phase diagram remains largely unexplored. We map its quantum phase diagram using density matrix renormalization group calculations and probe corresponding signatures experimentally in arrays containing more than 110 atoms. Beyond the established classical phases, the calculations identify two regimes absent in the classical limit and stabilized at finite transverse field: a $2/5$ filling plateau whose finite stability region emerges from a classically degenerate phase boundary and a symmetry-preserving quantum-dimer regime replacing classically selected collinear order. Experiments on finite open arrays reveal signatures consistent with these regimes across three boundary-pinning conditions, with the resulting response maps agreeing qualitatively with the calculated phase diagram. ### [69] [An Instability of the Quantum Markov Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12929)
*David C. Newsom,Michael Levin*

TL;DR: 马尔可夫长度（Markov length）在弱局域信道（local channels）下并不普遍稳定，甚至在纯直积态即失效；该不稳定性对基于马尔可夫长度的混合态相（mixed-state phases）等价定义构成挑战。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马尔可夫长度被认为是刻画混合态物质相（mixed-state phases of matter）的有用工具，其隐含前提是状态在弱局域信道下应保持稳定。本文旨在检验这一稳定性假设是否普遍成立。

Conclusion: 马尔可夫长度在弱局域信道下缺乏稳定性，这一结果对近期以马尔可夫长度为基础的混合态相等价定义提出了质疑。

Abstract: The Markov length, the length scale governing the exponential decay of conditional mutual information (CMI) in a quantum many-body state, has proven useful for characterizing mixed-state phases of matter. We demonstrate this explicitly in a one-dimensional fermionic chain, where a fine-tuned depth-two circuit of nearest-neighbor Gaussian channels maps the vacuum to a state whose CMI decays \emph{algebraically} and whose Markov length is therefore infinite, even when the channels are taken arbitrarily close to the identity. The algebraic decay is rooted in the nonanalyticity of the von Neumann entropy at rank-deficient density operators. We exhibit an analogous instability on an ordinary qubit chain. More generally, we show that the Markov length is unstable in any translation-invariant state with even a single rank-deficient reduced density operator. These results pose a challenge to recent definitions of mixed-state phase equivalence based on the Markov length.

</details>

*Fan Zhong*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放系统与有限温度环境耦合下量子相变的实时动力学是凝聚态物理（condensed-matter physics）与量子信息科学（quantum-information science）的核心问题。近期数值研究观察到反常的 Kibble-Zurek 标度（Kibble-Zurek scaling）与弛豫行为，其背后的标度机制尚需统一解释。

Method: 采用 Schwinger-Keldysh 形式（Schwinger-Keldysh formalism），并针对同一开放量子 Ising 链（open quantum Ising chain）分别构造离散化（discretized）与对角化（diagonalized）两种表述进行比较分析。

By contrast, dissipation-dominated dynamics behaves qualitatively differently. There, furthermore, we show that the discretized and diagonalized formulations of the same open quantum Ising chain realize distinct universality classes.

</details>

Motivation: 研究动机在于理解神经网络在训练推进及新增未见奇偶校验（parity）数据时，其已学函数如何重新组织。作者希望以布尔函数与量子信息式的度量，定量刻画"数据集结构—所学函数—预测表现"之间的关系，而非仅依赖传统损失或准确率指标。

Method: 将训练模型视为有限长度二进制串上的布尔函数，在训练集内外全部数据上求值，读取完整符号表；其 ±1 元素构成归一化态的振幅，即函数态，其逆子系统纯度 K_f=1/Tr(ρ_A²) 作为有效 Schmidt 秩。同时定义训练集给出的约束图（constraint graph）连通性，并追踪 Schmidt 谱、目标对齐（target alignment）与预测精度的演化次序。

Result: 小幅数据增加会拓宽 Schmidt 谱，称为分化（differentiation）；较大增加则将其重新集中到目标模式，称为目标选择性再相干（target-selective recoherence）。随数据集增长，约束图连通性单调上升，而学到的逆纯度非单调变化。在固定连通数据集上，学习推进时 Schmidt 谱先重组，随后目标对齐提升，最后预测精度改善。固定数据规模下，已学函数可发生"退缩"（retreat）并再次分化，且图连通性不变；恢复过程以类似目标选择性模式重新集中谱，但不精确重现原布尔态。

Conclusion: 数据约束的渗流（percolation）固定了可辨识支撑集（identifiable support），而训练产生的约化态在该支撑集停止变化后仍持续重组。这一框架区分了学习的三个层面——约束可辨识性、函数态组织与预测精度，并揭示了二者在时间上的有序关系。

Abstract: We study how a neural network reorganizes its learned function as training proceeds and as previously unseen parity data accumulate. The trained model is itself a Boolean function on binary strings of finite length. We then evaluate it on all data, inside and outside the training set, and read off its complete sign table. Its $\pm1$ entries are the amplitudes of a normalized state, dubbed the function-state, whose inverse subsystem purity $K_f=1/\textrm{Tr}(ρ_A^2)$ is an effective Schmidt rank. We find that small data additions broaden the Schmidt spectrum, whereas larger additions reconcentrate it in the target mode. The former is called differentiation because the model distinguishes inputs more sharply. The latter is called target-selective recoherence, where the model settles on the single rule shared by the added data. As the dataset grows, the connectivity of the constraint graph defined by the training set increases monotonically, whereas the learned inverse purity changes nonmonotonically. On a fixed connected dataset, as learning proceeds, the Schmidt spectrum begins to reorganize, followed by gains in target alignment and later by improvement in predictive accuracy. This ordering distinguishes three aspects of learning: constraint identifiability, function-state organization, and predictive accuracy. At fixed data size, a function that has already been learned can re-differentiate, an event we call retreat, without changing graph connectivity. Recovery reconcentrates the Schmidt spectrum in a similar target-selective pattern, without exact recurrence of the Boolean state. Thus the percolation of data constraints fixes the identifiable support, while the reduced state produced by training continues to reorganize even after that support stops changing.

</details>


### [72] [Zak Phase Quantization without Global Symmetry through Destructive Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13250)
*Geonwoo Hwang,Jun-Won Rhim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种无需全局对称性即可精确量子化 Zak 相位（Zak phase）的机制，其基础是局域支撑对称性（local support symmetry）与破坏性干涉（destructive interference）。当对称破缺自由度与拓扑母系统（topological parent system）耦合方式使其无法进入相关 Bloch 态时，被保留态继承母波函数并获得精确量子化 Zak 相位。该机制在破缺反演（inversion）的最小模型与破缺反射（reflection）的扶手椅纳米带（armchair nanoribbon）中得到验证，并给出扰动导致退量子化的相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zak 相位对一维能带结构提供基本几何刻画，其量子化通常被视为体极化与边界现象的体指标（bulk indicator），而传统上其精确量子化依赖 Bloch 哈密顿量（Bloch Hamiltonian）的全局对称性（如反演或反射）。本文旨在突破这一传统认识，寻找在保护对称性于全哈密顿量层面被显式破缺时仍保持 Zak 相位精确量子化的新机制。

Method: 机制基于局域支撑对称性与破坏性干涉：将对称破缺自由度以特定方式耦合到拓扑母系统，使相关 Bloch 态无法泄漏至这些自由度。作者对一般一维绝缘体（one-dimensional insulators）建立该机制，并构造一个破缺反演的最小模型和一个破缺反射的扶手椅纳米带进行演示。此外通过引入违反精确破坏性干涉条件的微扰，考察退量子化行为。

Result: 在破缺反演最小模型与破缺反射扶手椅纳米带中，未受保护的保留态仍保持母波函数，从而精确继承量子化的 Zak 相位；具体表现为量子化的 π Zak 相位及伴随的边界局域态（boundary-localized states）持续存在。当微扰破坏精确的破坏性干涉条件时，Zak 相位连续退量子化（dequantize），但在有限参数区间内仍可保留接近 π 的 Zak 相位及边界响应。

Conclusion: 结果表明，波函数支撑（wave-function support）本身即可作为保护一维系统量子化量子几何（quantized quantum geometry）的机制，而不仅依赖全局对称性。这为在缺乏保护性全局对称性的体系中识别与设计量子化 Zak 相位及边界现象提供了新的理论依据。

Abstract: The Zak phase provides a fundamental geometric characterization of one-dimensional band structures and, when quantized by symmetry, serves as a bulk indicator of polarization and boundary phenomena. Conventionally, its exact quantization is tied to a global symmetry of the Bloch Hamiltonian, such as inversion or reflection symmetry. Here, we demonstrate a distinct mechanism by which the Zak phase remains exactly quantized even when the corresponding protecting symmetry is explicitly broken at the level of the full Hamiltonian. The mechanism is based on local support symmetry and destructive interference: symmetry-breaking degrees of freedom are coupled to a topological parent system in such a way that the relevant Bloch states cannot leak into them. The preserved states therefore retain their parent wave functions and inherit their quantized Zak phase exactly, despite the absence of the protecting global symmetry. We establish this mechanism for general one-dimensional insulators and demonstrate it in an inversion-broken minimal model and a reflection-broken armchair nanoribbon, where a quantized $π$ Zak phase and associated boundary-localized states persist. We further show that perturbations violating the exact destructive-interference condition continuously dequantize the Zak phase, while a finite regime with a near-$π$ Zak phase and boundary response can survive. Our results establish wave-function support, rather than global symmetry alone, as a mechanism for protecting quantized quantum geometry in one-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [73] [Modelling Josephson Junctions with Boundary Conditions: Towards High-Fidelity Models for Superconducting Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13417)
*Luis Bueno Blanco,Juan Manuel Pérez-Pardo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种直接在希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）中表述的约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction）纯量子力学模型：带电粒子在一维环上运动，结点由自伴边界条件（self-adjoint boundary conditions）刻画，参数为耦合强度 λ 与相位跳变 δ。求解单结能谱问题后，模型从第一性原理导出了两条约瑟夫森关系，并预测了对纯 sinδ 律的偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准约瑟夫森结描述依赖一系列近似，缺乏直接从量子力学出发的表述。作者希望建立不依赖这些近似的模型，为超导量子比特（superconducting qubits）提供更高保真度的理论描述。

Method: 模型设定为：带电粒子在一维环（one-dimensional loop）上运动，结视为一个点，波函数在该点满足自伴边界条件，由耦合强度 λ（控制结的透明度）与相位跳变 δ（充当超导相位差）标记。对含单结的环求解能谱问题（spectral problem），并将基态动量期望值等同于超电流。相位随时间变化时，通过规范变换（gauge transformation）考察电压降与 δ̇ 的关系。

Result: 基态动量期望严格因子化为 ⟨p̂₀⟩ = I_c(δ,λ)sinδ。不透明结（opaque junction）下 I_c 与 δ 无关，第一约瑟夫森关系 I=I_c sinδ 得以还原；强吸引耦合下基态束缚于结处，sinδ 律同样成立但振幅被指数压制。更透明的结则预测对纯 sinδ 律的偏离：I_c 在 λ⁻² 阶即出现对 δ 的依赖，与近期为描述超导量子比特谱而引入的高阶约瑟夫森谐波（Josephson harmonics）相容；完全透明接触给出正比于 δ 的电流。规范变换表明电压降正比于 δ̇，从而无需外加假设即可导出第二约瑟夫森关系。

Conclusion: 边界条件方法从第一性原理同时再现了两条约瑟夫森关系，并进一步预测其修正项，为构建更高保真度的超导量子比特模型提供了可能路径。

Abstract: Josephson junctions are the central nonlinear element of superconducting qubits, yet their standard description is reached through a chain of approximations. We propose a purely quantum-mechanical model of the junction, formulated directly in Hilbert space: a charged particle moves on a one-dimensional loop, and the junction is a point at which the wave function obeys self-adjoint boundary conditions, labelled by a coupling strength $λ$, which controls the transparency of the junction, and a phase jump $δ$, which plays the role of the superconducting phase difference. For a loop with a single junction we solve the spectral problem and show that the ground-state momentum expectation, identified with the supercurrent, factorises exactly as $\langle\hat{p}_0\rangle = I_c(δ,λ)\sinδ$. For opaque junctions $I_c$ becomes independent of $δ$ and the first Josephson relation $I=I_c\sinδ$ is recovered; for strongly attractive coupling, where the ground state is bound to the junction, the $\sinδ$ law is also recovered, but with an exponentially suppressed amplitude. For more transparent junctions, however, the model \emph{predicts} deviations from the pure $\sinδ$ law: $I_c$ acquires a dependence on $δ$ already at order $λ^{-2}$, compatible with the higher Josephson harmonics recently found necessary to describe superconducting qubit spectra, and a fully transparent contact yields a current linear in $δ$. When the phase varies in time, a gauge transformation shows that the voltage drop across the junction is proportional to $\dotδ$, so that the second Josephson relation emerges without being imposed. The boundary-condition approach thus reproduces both Josephson relations from first principles and predicts corrections to them, offering a route towards higher-fidelity models of superconducting qubits.

</details>


### [74] [Encoding Matters: A Qubit-Efficient Reformulation for Quantum Resilient Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13621)
*Emanuele G. Dalla Torre,Bat-El Friedman,Daniel Porat*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种结构感知的编码约简（structure-aware encoding reduction）方法，将物流与路由问题高效映射到量子比特，并在双链路故障下的时延韧性路由基准上验证，使量子比特数与编译电路复杂度显著下降，成功率提升 100 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子比特数量决定可访问状态空间规模，但也带来电路规模增长、约束开销与硬件噪声暴露等问题；对近期量子计算机（near-term quantum computers）而言，问题所需量子比特数是核心的实际关切。

Method: 提出通过结构感知编码约简将物流与路由问题映射到量子比特的方法，并在双链路故障下的时延韧性路由（latency-resilient routing under dual-link failures）基准问题上进行演示。

Result: 约简后的表述在保持原优化任务不变的前提下，大幅降低量子比特数与编译电路复杂度；在同一台离子阱量子计算机（trapped-ion quantum computer）上运行，成功率获得 100 倍提升。

Conclusion: 精细的问题表述（problem formulation）可显著提升当前量子硬件在实际优化任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Qubits are powerful computational resources because each additional qubit doubles the size of the accessible state space. This expressive power comes at a cost: adding qubits also increases circuit size, constraint overhead, and exposure to hardware noise. These limitations are especially critical for near-term quantum computers, making the number of qubits required to formulate a problem a central practical concern. In this work, we present a method for efficiently mapping logistics and routing problems onto qubits through structure-aware encoding reduction. We demonstrate the method on a benchmark problem of latency-resilient routing under dual-link failures. For this benchmark, the reduced formulation substantially lowers both the qubit count and the compiled circuit complexity while preserving the original optimization task. By running the reduced model on the same trapped-ion quantum computer as the benchmark, we obtain a 100X improvement in the success probability. This finding shows how careful problem formulation can make current quantum hardware substantially more effective for practical optimization.

</details>


### [75] [Locality-Preparation Time Tradeoffs in Dissipative Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13784)
*Philipp Westhoff,Sara Murciano,Mattia Moroder*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作为二次费米子系统建立了严格的“局域性—制备时间”关系，并提出在保持目标态的前提下优化耗散动力学局域性的构造方法，揭示了Kitaev链在临界与能隙区制下截然不同的局域性-时间权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耗散态制备（dissipative state preparation）将量子多体态设计为开放系统动力学的稳态，所需耗散的空间范围决定模拟实现的可行性与数字实现的连通性和控制复杂度，但局域性如何定量约束制备时间此前基本未知。

Method: 针对二次费米子系统（quadratic fermionic systems）建立精确的局域性—制备时间关系，并发展一种构造方法，在保持目标态的同时优化耗散动力学的局域性，随后应用于Kitaev链。

Result: 在临界区（critical regime），抑制跳跃算符（jump operators）的代数尾部必然使制备时间随系统尺寸代数增长；而在平庸与拓扑能隙相（gapped phases）中，均可实现指数局域化的耗散，并在热力学极限下具有有限制备时间。

Conclusion: 研究界定了快速耗散制备何时与局域控制相容，以及临界性何时带来不可避免的动力学代价。

Abstract: Dissipative state preparation engineers quantum many-body states as steady states of an open-system dynamics. The spatial range of the required dissipation sets both the feasibility of analog implementations and the connectivity and control complexity of digital ones. Yet how this locality quantitatively constrains preparation times remains largely unknown. Here, we establish an exact locality-preparation time relation for quadratic fermionic systems and develop a construction that optimizes the locality of the dissipative dynamics while preserving the target state. Applied to the Kitaev chain, our framework reveals sharply different locality-preparation time tradeoffs in critical and gapped regimes. At criticality, suppressing the algebraic tails of the jump operators necessarily forces the preparation time to grow algebraically with system size. By contrast, both trivial and topological gapped phases admit exponentially localized dissipation with a finite preparation time in the thermodynamic limit. Our results identify when fast dissipative preparation is compatible with local control and when criticality imposes an unavoidable dynamical cost.

</details>


### [76] [A Warped Born Rule Turns an Observer into a Maxwell's Demon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13936)
*Olumakinde Ogunnaike*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文探讨将玻恩定则（Born rule）$p(m)\propto\|P_m|\psi\rangle\|^2$ 替换为扭曲定则（warped rule）$p_b(m)\propto\|P_m|\psi\rangle\|^{2b}$ 后，在粒子数守恒的多体系统中的后果。测量历史的概率偏置方向由 $b-1$ 的符号决定：$b>1$ 使气体趋向冻结构型（类似麦克斯韦妖抽取熵），$b<1$ 则清除长波涨落形成超均匀气体。该效应在一维量子气体中被解析推导并经矩阵乘积态（matrix product state）模拟验证，并分析了其在“复制技巧”（replica trick）中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学结构极为精细，对其中几乎任何部分的微小改动都可能导致巨大的定性差异与严重非物理的预测。作者由此出发，考察修改玻恩定则（Born rule）这一基本概率规则会带来何种多体后果。

Method: 在粒子数守恒系统中，研究由重复局域粒子观测所打断的演化，其中测量历史按扭曲定则 $p_b(m)\propto\|P_m|\psi\rangle\|^{2b}$ 赋权；针对特定测量方案对一维量子气体进行解析推导，并用基于矩阵乘积态（matrix product state）模拟的离散时间投影测量电路模型对更一般的测量过程进行数值验证。

Result: 概率偏置由 $b-1$ 的符号控制：$b>1$ 时偏置通常驱动气体聚集成近乎冻结的构型，观测行为如同麦克斯韦妖（Maxwell's demon）从封闭系统中抽取熵；$b<1$ 时偏置反转，通常清除长波涨落，产生超均匀气体（hyperuniform gas）。一维量子气体中的这两个相被解析导出并获数值佐证。

Conclusion: 观测行为通过扭曲的玻恩定则可在封闭系统中产生类麦克斯韦妖的熵抽取或反偏置的超均匀化效应，且该偏置在“复制技巧”（replica trick）这一常使用扭曲定则的常用技术中同样显现，意味着有限复制数（finite-replica）计算与复制极限（replica-limit）计算之间存在定性差异。

Abstract: Quantum mechanics seems to be an incredibly delicate theory. Slight alterations to nearly any part of its structure can lead to huge qualitative differences and wildly unphysical predictions. Here, we explore the many-body consequences of replacing the Born rule $p(m)\propto\|P_m|ψ\rangle|^2$ by a warped rule $p_b(m)\propto\|P_m|ψ\rangle\|^{2b}$. Specifically, in systems with conserved particle number, evolution punctuated by repeated local particle observations acquires a probabilistic bias over measurement histories with an effect controlled by the sign of $b-1$. For $b>1$, this bias generically drives the gas to collect into nearly frozen configurations. Similar to the action of Maxwell's demon, the simple act of observation seems to extract entropy from a closed system. For $b<1$, the bias is reversed, generically scouring out long-wavelength fluctuations to produce a hyperuniform gas. We study this effect in a one-dimensional quantum gas, where these two phases are derived analytically for a particular measurement scheme and corroborated numerically for more general procedures with discrete-time projective-measurement circuit models using matrix product state simulations. We additionally discuss how this bias manifests in a common technique that often employs this warped rule, the ``replica trick,'' implying qualitative differences between finite-replica and replica-limit calculations.

</details>


### [77] [An entangling Hubbard quasiparticle collider on a digital quantum processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13946)
*Chan Bin Bark,Hanbyul Kim,Yoonil Koo,SeungBeom Hong,Seik Pak,Kwang Jun Ahn,Sangmo Cheon,Youngseok Kim,Moon Jip Park*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在数字量子处理器上实现费米-哈伯德模型的准粒子碰撞实验，首次测得准粒子透射概率并与Bethe-ansatz散射矩阵无自由参数一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准粒子散射（quasiparticle scattering）可直接探测强关联体系（strongly correlated systems）中主导相互作用的机制，因此需要可控的实验平台来验证相关理论预言。

Method: 在半填充21格点链（编码于ibm_boston处理器的42个量子比特）上，将一个doublon与一个holon进行碰撞，实现费米-哈伯德（Fermi-Hubbard）模型的准粒子散射实验。

Result: 首次在量子硬件上测得哈伯德模型准粒子的透射概率（transmission probability），与Bethe-ansatz散射矩阵无自由参数符合；碰撞通过叠加透射与反射分量产生纠缠，由粒子数涨落（particle-number fluctuation）推断得出。对照组两个doublon的碰撞未显示相互作用依赖或粒子数涨落。

Conclusion: 按需制备特定类型的准粒子并编程其运动与相互作用，使数字量子处理器成为研究准粒子散射所生成纠缠结构（entanglement structure）的新型实验工具。

Abstract: Scattering of quasiparticles provides direct access to the interactions that govern strongly correlated systems. In this work, we realize the quasiparticle scattering experiment of the Fermi-Hubbard model on a digital quantum processor by colliding a doublon and a holon in the half-filled chain of 21 sites encoded on 42 qubits of the ibm_boston processor. For the first time, we measure the transmission probability of quasiparticles of the Hubbard model on quantum hardware, which agrees with the Bethe-ansatz scattering matrix without free parameters. Beyond the scattering probabilities, the collision generates entanglement by superposing the transmitted and reflected components, which we infer from the particle-number fluctuation. In sharp contrast, a control collision between two doublons shows neither the interaction dependence nor the number fluctuation, as expected for indistinguishable particles. Our work demonstrates that the ability to create quasiparticles of a chosen flavor on demand and program their motion and interactions makes a digital quantum processor a new experimental toolset for probing the entanglement structure generated by quasiparticle scattering.

</details>
