### [78] [Ionic liquid/PDMS coated nanostructured ZnO surfaces for superior antibacterial activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12966)
*Anushika Rohilla,Ajit Seth,Mayank Bharadwaj,Rumal Singh,Sajal K Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究采用绿色环保的热水处理（Hot Water Treatment, HWT）法在锌基底上制备致密氧化锌（ZnO）纳米棒阵列，并复合咪唑基离子液体（imidazolium-based ionic liquid）与聚二甲基硅氧烷（polydimethylsiloxane, PDMS）构建耐久混合涂层，实现对大肠杆菌（Escherichia coli）的协同抗菌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 食品、医疗与海洋领域亟需应对细菌定植与生物污损（biofouling），而传统抗菌涂层存在操作稳定性差、细胞毒性（cytotoxicity）及抗菌耐药性（antimicrobial resistance）等局限，因此需要开发新型纳米结构抗菌表面。

Method: 以氧化锌（ZnO）纳米结构为基础，利用其形貌丰富、理化性质优良及固有杀菌（bactericidal）特性；采用绿色热水处理（HWT）法在锌基底上生长ZnO纳米棒阵列；进一步以咪唑基离子液体经聚二甲基硅氧烷（PDMS）稳定化，形成耐久混合涂层。

Result: 混合涂层修饰的纳米结构表面通过形貌诱导的机械应力（topography-induced mechanical strain）与膜破坏（membrane disruption）的协同作用，对大肠杆菌（Escherichia coli）表现出优异的抗菌效能。

Conclusion: 该绿色制备的ZnO纳米棒阵列复合涂层兼具耐久性与高效抗菌性能，为克服传统抗菌涂层的稳定性、毒性与耐药性挑战提供了一种可行策略。

Abstract: The development of nanostructured antibacterial surfaces has gained significant attention due to the urgent need to combat bacterial colonization and biofouling across food, healthcare, and marine sectors. Among functional metal oxides, zinc oxide (ZnO) nanostructures are particularly promising owing to their rich morphology, favourable physicochemical attributes, and intrinsic bactericidal properties. Here, we utilize a green, eco-friendly Hot Water Treatment (HWT) method to fabricate dense ZnO nanorod arrays on zinc substrates. To overcome the practical limitations and challenges of conventional antibacterial coatings, such as poor operational stability, cytotoxicity, and the risk of antimicrobial resistance, we developed a durable hybrid coating composed of an imidazolium-based ionic liquid stabilized with polydimethylsiloxane. The resulting hybrid-coated nanostructured surfaces exhibit superior antibacterial efficacy against Escherichia coli through a combination of topography-induced mechanical strain and membrane disruption.

</details>


### [79] [Imbibition dynamics under dissolution-driven fluid thickening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13169)
*H. W. Almeida,A. F. V. Matias,R. C. V. Coelho,N. A. M. Araujo*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究针对液体侵入多孔介质时溶解固体组分并改变自身流变性的耦合过程，以牛奶浸渗饼干为实例，建立了溶解驱动流体增稠的定量描述。研究推导了广义Washburn定律（generalized Washburn law），提出以累积逆黏度定义的重新标度时间，预测了不同溶解速率下浸渗动力学的坍缩，并通过数值模拟验证；在扩散较慢及无序多孔介质中进一步分析空间非均匀性与孔隙几何的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液体侵入多孔介质并溶解固体组分、进而改变自身流变性的耦合过程广泛存在于食品加工、过滤和反应性多孔介质（reactive porous media）中，但流体黏度演变如何定量决定浸渗动力学尚缺乏描述。研究旨在弥补这一缺口，聚焦溶解驱动的流体增稠（dissolution-driven fluid thickening）情形。

Method: 在充分混合极限（well-mixed limit）下针对毛细管（capillary tube）推导广义Washburn定律，将演变的黏度通过累积逆黏度给出的重新标度时间（rescaled time）纳入描述；采用耦合自由表面与溶质输运的数值模拟（coupled free-surface and solute-transport simulations）进行验证，并进一步考察扩散较慢情形及无序多孔介质（disordered porous media）。

Result: 广义Washburn定律预测不同溶解速率下浸渗动力学发生坍缩（collapse），数值模拟予以确认。当扩散较慢时，浓度与黏度呈空间非均匀（spatially heterogeneous）分布，导致推进液体的阻滞弱于充分混合理论的预测。在无序多孔介质中，研究量化了溶解驱动增稠与孔隙几何变化对动力学的影响。

Conclusion: 该结果将经典Washburn描述扩展至流体性质演变条件下的浸渗过程，为理解固体与侵入液体相互作用逐步改变其流变学（rheology）时的流体吸收提供了定量基础。

Abstract: The imbibition of milk into a biscuit provides an example of a broader class of processes in which a liquid invades a porous material, dissolving components of the solid and thereby modifying its own rheology. This coupling between imbibition, dissolution, and evolving fluid rheology also occurs in food processing, filtration, and reactive porous media. While the resulting changes in fluid viscosity are known to affect imbibition, a quantitative description of how this evolving rheology determines the imbibition dynamics is still lacking. Here, we establish such a description for dissolution-driven fluid thickening. For a capillary tube in the well-mixed limit, we derive a generalized Washburn law showing that the evolving viscosity can be incorporated through a rescaled time given by the accumulated inverse viscosity. This predicts a collapse of the imbibition dynamics for different dissolution rates, which we confirm numerically using coupled free-surface and solute-transport simulations. When diffusion is slower, concentration and viscosity become spatially heterogeneous, resulting in a weaker retardation of the advancing liquid than predicted by the well-mixed theory. We then consider disordered porous media and quantify how dissolution-driven thickening and variations in pore geometry affect the dynamics. Our results extend the classical Washburn description to imbibition with evolving fluid properties and provide a quantitative basis for understanding fluid uptake when interactions with the solid progressively modify the rheology of the invading liquid.

</details>


### [80] [A new device for live cell imaging of biological samples under in-plane shear](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13496)
*Sidharth Kumar Das,Lyova Mamoyan,Eberhard Bodenschatz,Stefano Villa*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文介绍一种新型装置，可在商用光学显微镜下对细胞单层施加均匀的面内剪切（in-plane shear），并展示其可对单细胞及汇合细胞单层实现可重复、可控且均匀的剪切。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械应力与形变对细胞及组织行为的影响是生物物理学（biophysics）的核心议题，其理解对胚胎发生（embryogenesis）到肿瘤发展等领域均具意义。为探究细胞对基本机械转变的响应，需开发能施加简单形变的装置；然而现有拉伸装置较多，面内剪切形变研究相对不足。

Method: 设计并引入一种新装置，在细胞单层上施加均匀的面内剪切，同时借助商用光学显微镜（optical microscope）对样品进行成像。文中报告了所面临的实验挑战，并对该装置进行了完整表征。

Result: 装置能够对单细胞和汇合细胞单层（confluent cellular monolayers）施加可重复、可控且均匀的剪切。

Conclusion: 该装置弥补了面内剪切形变研究工具的不足，为在成像条件下研究细胞对剪切响应的机械生物学问题提供了可行手段。

Abstract: The effect of mechanical stresses and deformations upon the behavior and properties of cells and tissues is a topic of paramount interest in the field of biophysics. Its understanding is essential for deepening our comprehension of cellular responses to environmental stimuli, with implications in fields ranging from embryogenesis to tumor development. For boosting the understanding in this field, it is important to develop devices for the application of simple deformations in order to probe the cellular response to the basic mechanical transformations. While a number of stretching devices have been developed, in-plane shearing deformation has been little investigated. Here, a new device is introduced that allows the application of homogeneous in-plane shear upon a cell monolayer while imaging the sample with a commercial optical microscope. A description of the faced experimental challenges is reported and a full characterization is presented. It is demonstrated the capability of the device to apply reproducible, controlled and homogeneous shear of both single cells and confluent cellular monolayers.

</details>


### [81] [How Fluctuations Control Chiral Symmetry Breaking in Reacting Mixtures of Chiral Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13523)
*Leonardo Silva-Dias,Christoph A. Weber*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究探讨在手性分子自催化（autocatalysis）与相互拮抗（mutual antagonism）驱动的化学系统中，有限粒子数涨落（finite-population fluctuations）如何影响手性对称破缺（chiral symmetry breaking）的态选择、特征时间与随机切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物分子的手性不对称（chiral asymmetry）是生命系统的显著特征，但其起源机制尚未阐明。作者认为手性对称破缺可能发生在分子数目足够小、本征涨落起重要作用的生命前（prebiotic）条件系统中，因此需研究有限粒子数涨落的作用。

Method: 采用由自催化（autocatalysis）与相互拮抗（mutual antagonism）驱动的化学系统模型，分析有限粒子数涨落对手性态选择、对称破缺特征时间及手性态间随机切换的影响，推导相关动力学过程随粒子数的标度关系及解析表达式。

Result: 推导出选择任一对手性态的概率作为初始对映体偏差（initial enantiomeric bias）函数的解析表达式；计算了不同初始偏差下的平均选择时间（mean selection time），并表明在弱相互拮抗（weak mutual antagonism）条件下，涨落可诱导相反手性态之间的转变。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了有限粒子数涨落如何同时控制自催化化学系统中分子手性的出现与稳定性，为理解生命前手性对称破缺提供了动力学层面的定量描述。

Abstract: The pronounced chiral asymmetry of biomolecules is a defining feature of living systems, yet the mechanisms by which such asymmetry emerged remain unresolved. Chiral symmetry breaking may have occurred under prebiotic conditions in systems with sufficiently small molecular populations for intrinsic fluctuations to play an important role. Here, we investigate how finite-population fluctuations affect chiral-state selection, the characteristic time for symmetry breaking, and stochastic switching between chiral states in a system driven by autocatalysis and mutual antagonism. We determine how these dynamical processes scale with particle number and derive an analytical expression for the probability of selecting either chiral state as a function of the initial enantiomeric bias. We further calculate the mean selection time for different initial biases and show that, for weak mutual antagonism, fluctuations can induce transitions between opposite chiral states. These results characterize how finite-population fluctuations control both the emergence and stability of molecular chirality in autocatalytic chemical systems.

</details>


### [82] [Shear-driven arrest of dip-coating film entrainment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13729)
*Theo Witkamp,Stéphane Zaleski,Federico Toschi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 通过两相不可混溶 Navier-Stokes 模拟，研究发现与平板运动方向相反的层流气流可在液-气界面施加切向剪切应力，从而显著提高液膜卷吸的临界速度，并在剪切足够大时完全抑制卷吸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从部分润湿液体槽中提拉平板时，超过临界速度会夹带液膜。此研究旨在探究外加层流气流对液-气界面施加切向剪切应力时，该临界卷吸阈值能否被显著提高。

Method: 采用两相不可混溶 Navier-Stokes 模拟（two-phase immiscible Navier-Stokes simulations），将气体限制在狭窄通道内，推导界面应力的闭式表达式，并结合润滑理论（lubrication theory）建立膜厚、接触线速度与外加剪切应力之间的关系。

Result: 随着气流速度增大，液膜变薄、接触线速度减慢，直至外加剪切克服运动平板的阻力、卷吸被阻止。平板与接触线之间的速度失配与平板速度无关，仅由外加剪切决定（与静止情形一致）。一组恒定临界平板速度下的变气流模拟即可确定每个更小平板速度对应的临界气流。

Conclusion: 反向层流气流通过界面剪切应力可有效调控并抑制液膜卷吸，其临界条件满足标度关系，使得单组模拟结果可外推至其他平板速度情形。

Abstract: A plate withdrawn from a bath of partially wetting liquid entrains a film above a critical velocity. Here, we show that this critical threshold can be substantially increased by the presence of a laminar gas flow opposed to the plate velocity that exerts a tangential shear stress on the liquid-gas interface. Using two-phase immiscible Navier-Stokes simulations and confining the gas to a narrow channel, we derive a closed-form expression for the interfacial stress, which combined with lubrication theory relates the thickness of the film and the contact line velocity to the applied shear stress. As the gas velocity is increased, the film thins and the contact line velocity slows down, until the imposed shear overcomes the drag of the moving plate and entrainment is arrested. As in the quiescent case, the velocity mismatch between plate and contact line is independent of the plate velocity and is set by the applied shear alone. We find that one set of varying gas flow simulations, with constant critical plate velocity, therefore determines the critical gas flow for every smaller plate velocity.

</details>


### [83] [Arrested phase separation and amorphous patterns in charged binary fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13748)
*Logan H. Grant,Ana Bensabat,Eva Pospivsilova,Joost de Graaf,Alexander Morozov,Giuseppe Negro,Ciprian G. Pruteanu,Tyler N. Shendruk,Davide Marenduzzo*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究探讨带电二元流体中相分离趋势与被屏蔽静电排斥之间的竞争，揭示了一种具有有限波长不稳定性的受阻相分离（arrested phase separation）机制，可产生可调尺度的液滴、迷宫、同心壳与管状等结构，其中部分为无序非晶形貌（amorphous morphologies）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞争性相互作用产生的受阻（frustration）能够稳定具有可调尺度的图案，从而避免无限粗化与宏观相分离。研究旨在探究相分离倾向与被屏蔽静电排斥（screened electrostatic repulsion）相对抗时所产生的结构形态及其稳定性。

Method: 结合解析分析（analytics）与数值模拟（numerical simulations），对带电二元流体（charged binary fluid）进行研究，识别出以有限波长不稳定性（finite-wavelength instability）为特征的受阻相分离区间。

Result: 所得状态在二维中包括液滴相与迷宫相（labyrinthine phases），在三维中包括液滴、同心壳（concentric shells）与管状结构（tubular structures）。其中部分结构为可从不同初始条件可重复出现的非晶形貌。自由能估算（free-energy estimates）表明这些无序结构可能由受阻在热力学上稳定，而非被动力学俘获（kinetically trapped）。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一种简单机制：相分离与被屏蔽静电之间的竞争可生成具有玻璃态特征（glassy features）的平衡非晶微相（equilibrium amorphous microphases）。

Abstract: Frustration generated by competing interactions can stabilise patterns with tunable lengthscales which evade indefinite coarsening and macroscopic phase separation. Here we investigate a charged binary fluid in which the tendency toward phase separation is opposed by screened electrostatic repulsion. Combining analytics and numerical simulations, we identify a regime of arrested phase separation characterised by finite-wavelength instability. The resulting states include droplet and labyrinthine phases in two dimensions, and droplets, concentric shells and tubular structures in three dimensions. Surprisingly, some of these structures are amorphous morphologies that emerge reproducibly from different initial conditions. Free-energy estimates suggest that these disordered structures may be thermodynamically stabilised by frustration, rather than being kinetically trapped. Our results identify a simple mechanism through which the competition between phase separation and screened electrostatics can generate equilibrium amorphous microphases with glassy features.

</details>


### [84] [Potential energy release rate for solids with bounded domains during evolutions involving changes to boundaries and boundary data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13874)
*Mrityunjay Kothari,Sayaka Kochiyama,Haneesh Kesari*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文研究非线性弹性固体（nonlinear elastic solids）在几何、材料属性、外力与位移约束随演化变化时，平衡势能的释放率（potential-energy release rate），给出仅依赖当前状态平衡解与局部演化量的闭式表达，并证明其在二维缺口扩展问题中退化为经典 $J$-积分（$J$-integral）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程与材料演化过程（如缺口扩展、材料属性空间变化）中，势能随状态变化的速率具有重要物理意义，但势能在各演化状态下的恰当表述及释放率的良定义性（well defined）尚需系统刻画。作者旨在建立可涵盖几何、材料、载荷与边界数据同时演化的统一变分框架。

Method: 将演化表示为一参数族变分问题（one-parameter family of variational problems），识别势能释放率良定义的族类；强调每个演化状态的势能泛函须给出正确的平衡态，其平衡值须正确反映状态间物理势能变化。在此框架下，针对有界域（bounded domains）固体推导释放率的闭式表达，允许材料空间非均匀演化、尖锐材料界面（sharp material interfaces）以及边界几何与边界数据的演化。

Result: 得到势能释放率的闭式表达式，该表达式完全由所考虑状态的平衡解及规定局部演化的量决定，无需再求解邻近的平衡问题（neighboring equilibrium problems）。将其应用于二维弹性体缺口（notch）的扩展，所得释放率退化为经典 $J$-积分。

Conclusion: 所提变分框架为非线性弹性固体演化中的能量释放率提供了良定义且计算上局部的刻画；结果在缺口扩展算例中与经典 $J$-积分一致，表明该表述具有与断裂力学既有理论相衔接的普适性。

Abstract: We investigate the rate of change of equilibrium potential energy for nonlinear elastic solids undergoing evolutions that may involve changes in body geometry, material properties, applied tractions, and prescribed displacements. We represent such an evolution by a one-parameter family of variational problems and identify a class of these families for which the potential-energy release rate is well defined. Particular attention is given to the formulation of the potential energy at each evolution state: each constituent variational problem must yield the appropriate equilibrium state, while its equilibrium value must also correctly represent the physical change in potential energy between states. Within this framework, we derive a closed-form expression for the potential-energy release rate for solids with bounded domains, allowing for spatially nonuniform material evolution, sharp material interfaces, and evolving boundary geometry and prescribed boundary data. The expression is determined entirely by the equilibrium solution at the state under consideration and by the quantities prescribing the local evolution, so neighboring equilibrium problems need not be solved. Finally, we apply the result to the extension of a notch in a two-dimensional elastic body and show that the resulting potential-energy release rate reduces to the classical $J$-integral.

</details>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [Frustrated Topological Orbital Magnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12557)
*Sergio Garrido Bourkhaeva,Claudio Castelnovo,Wojciech J. Jankowski*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作研究三维体系中几何阻挫（geometric frustration）与阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆通量（Aharonov-Bohm fluxes）的相互作用，揭示一类具有轨道磁偶极、四极与铁环矩序（ferrotoroidic orders）的拓扑半金属基态，并将其命名为"阻挫拓扑轨道磁性"（frustrated topological orbital magnetism）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索几何阻挫与规范场（Aharonov-Bohm 通量）耦合时能否产生类似经典自旋冰（spin ices）的自旋排布，从而拓展阻挫物理与拓扑物态交叉领域的新现象。

Method: 通过研究三维中几何阻挫与 Aharonov-Bohm 通量的相互作用，构建并分析阻挫轨道电流图样，并将其与非阿贝尔拓扑节线（non-Abelian topological nodal lines）及拓扑 Weyl 节点荷相联系。

Result: 发现阻挫拓扑轨道磁基态具备轨道磁偶极、四极和铁环矩序；由非阿贝尔拓扑节线支撑的基态环矩化（toroidizations）可诱导可测量的电流致霍尔现象；不同的 2-in-2-out 与 3-in-1-out 阻挫轨道电流图样对应不同拓扑 Weyl 节点荷，并伴随各向异性的线性与非线性霍尔效应。

Conclusion: 该研究提出可在候选材料中实验验证不同阻挫拓扑轨道磁序的特征信号，为阻挫拓扑轨道磁性这一新现象类别提供理论与实验检验路径。

Abstract: By studying the interplay of geometric frustration with Aharonov-Bohm fluxes in three dimensions, we unveil a rich playground exhibiting topological semimetallic ground states with orbital magnetic dipole, quadrupole and ferrotoroidic orders, akin to spin patterns in classical spin ices - a class of phenomena which we dub frustrated topological orbital magnetism. We demonstrate that the ground-state toroidizations underpinned by non-Abelian topological nodal lines induce measurable current-induced Hall phenomena. We further find that distinct 2-in-2-out and 3-in-1-out frustrated orbital current patterns can be associated with topological Weyl node charges, and corresponding anisotropic linear and nonlinear Hall effects. We propose signatures for experimental validation of distinct frustrated topological orbital magnetic orders in candidate materials.

</details>


### [86] [Entropy Production in Mesoscopic Electronic Transport Beyond Weak-Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12652)
*Ivana R. A. C. Lucena,Caio H. Lewenkopf,Alexandre R. Rocha*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究在超越弱耦合（weak-coupling）的范围内考察相干介观导体（coherent mesoscopic conductors）中熵产生（entropy production）的全统计性质，基于内外视角与 Landauer-Büttiker 散射框架，揭示涨落对称性（fluctuation symmetry）不依赖耦合强度并给出弱、强耦合极限下的解析累积生成函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多局限于弱耦合近似，对超出弱耦合情形下熵产生的完整统计规律认识不足。本文旨在突破该限制，刻画耦合强度对熵产生分布与高阶累积量的影响。

Method: 采用内外视角（inside-outside perspective）并结合 Landauer-Büttiker 散射框架（scattering framework），将熵产生与粒子输运的全计数统计（full counting statistics）建立关联，并推导弱耦合与强耦合极限下累积生成函数（cumulant-generating function）的闭式解析表达式。

Result: 累积生成函数满足与耦合强度无关的涨落对称性；高阶累积量揭示共振输运（resonant transport）与相干费米子分配（coherent fermionic partitioning）的独特特征；耦合诱导的展宽（coupling-induced broadening）显著改变输运统计，但在长时间极限下涨落对称性依然保持。

Conclusion: 熵产生的涨落对称性由微观可逆性（microscopic reversibility）决定，在长时间极限下不受耦合强度及耦合诱导展宽影响；所获解析结果可用于表征相干介观导体中熵产生的分布与高阶统计。

Abstract: We investigate the full statistics of entropy production in coherent mesoscopic conductors beyond the weak-coupling regime. Using the inside-outside perspective and the Landauer-Büttiker scattering framework, we relate entropy production to the full counting statistics of particle transport and show that the corresponding cumulant-generating function satisfies the fluctuation symmetry independently of the coupling strength. We obtain closed analytical expressions for the cumulant-generating function in the weak- and strong-coupling limits, enabling a detailed characterization of entropy production distributions and higher-order cumulants. These cumulants reveal distinct signatures of resonant transport and coherent fermionic partitioning. While coupling-induced broadening substantially modifies the transport statistics, the fluctuation symmetry imposed by microscopic reversibility remains preserved in the long-time limit.

</details>


### [87] [Electron binding by charge disorder surrounding gapped bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12714)
*Jude McClay,Angelika Knothe,Igor Rozhansky,Vladimir Fal'ko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究基于连续介质介电响应框架，分析双层石墨烯（bilayer graphene, BLG）在介电环境电荷无序下带边态（band-edge states）的库仑束缚行为；结果表明，无论 hBN 封装还是双电层离子门控，远程电荷均不产生深能隙中态（deep midgap states），束缚态始终靠近导带与价带边缘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层石墨烯的带隙可通过静电方式调控，但其介电环境中的电荷无序（charge disorder）如何影响带边态尚不明确，尤其在面向栅定义量子点（gate-defined quantum dots）与离子门控（ionic gating）两类应用场景时。

Method: 建立描述电子与空穴被库仑中心（Coulomb centres）束缚的理论框架，纳入 Bernal 双层石墨烯的面内与面外介电响应（in- and out-of-plane dielectric response），并分别应用于 hBN 封装的介质带隙 BLG 与双 n/p 离子门控 BLG 两种体系。

Result: 在 hBN/BLG/hBN 中，单个远程电荷仅形成弱束缚态，束缚能普遍远小于 BLG 带隙；在离子门控下，束缚态尺度过大而无法被单个离子俘获，局域化（localisation）源于离子密度涨落，其幅度受离子构型冻结温度 $T_f$ 下的库仑充电能（Coulomb charging energy）限制。

Conclusion: 在两种情形中，电荷无序均不产生深能隙中态，束缚态保持在导带与价带边缘附近，说明介电环境无序对 BLG 带边态的影响相对温和。

Abstract: Bilayer graphene (BLG) is a unique electronic material offering electrostatic tunability of its band gap. Here, we study how charge disorder in the dielectric environment modifies band-edge states by binding electrons and holes to Coulomb centres, taking into account the in- and out-of-plane dielectric response of Bernal bilayers. We apply this framework to (a) hBN-encapsulated medium-size-gap BLG used for gate-defined quantum dots, and (b) BLG under double n/p ionic gating. In hBN/BLG/hBN, we find that single remote charges produce only weakly bound states, with binding energies generally small compared with the BLG gap. Under ionic gating, we find that the bound states are too large to be trapped by individual ions -- localisation arises from fluctuations in the ion density, whose magnitude is limited by Coulomb charging energy at the freezing temperature $T_f$ of the ionic configuration. Thus, in both regimes, charge disorder does not produce deep midgap states, with the bound states remaining closer to the conduction and valence band edges.

</details>


### [88] [Nonlocal Josephson coupling and current redistribution in a large-area graphene multiterminal Josephson junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12753)
*Luojia Zhang,Kushagra Aggarwal,Jay D. Sau,Ethan G. Arnault,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Valla Fatemi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在微米尺度石墨烯霍尔条（graphene Hall bar）中实现多端子约瑟夫森网络（multiterminal Josephson network），观察到四探针响应中显著的负微分纵向电阻（negative differential longitudinal resistance, NDLR），并用以揭示两种非局域性的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多端子约瑟夫森结（multiterminal Josephson junctions, MTJJs）的超导输运常由相邻引线间的局域耦合主导，掩盖了超越简单连通性的物理；需构建具有显著非局域耦合的多端子体系以探索更丰富的现象。

Method: 采用微米尺度石墨烯霍尔条几何结构构建多端子约瑟夫森网络，实现远距离端子间的实质性非局域耦合；通过四探针与两端测量对比，并结合包含两种非局域性相互作用的网络模型复现实验结果。

Result: 在开关电流（switching currents）附近，四探针响应中出现显著的负微分纵向电阻（NDLR），而两端测量中无此特征；该响应可由包含非局域约瑟夫森耦合（非局域 Josephson coupling，决定网络开关序列）与非局域正常态电导（nonlocal normal-state conductance，在区域切换后重新分配电流）相互作用的网络模型复现。

Conclusion: 涌现的 NDLR 有望用于表征超导—半导体混合系统（superconductor–semiconductor hybrid systems）及构建非线性电路；该约瑟夫森霍尔条几何为研究石墨烯基异质结构中介观超导（mesoscopic superconductivity）与多体基态（many-body ground states）的相互作用提供了平台。

Abstract: Multiterminal Josephson junctions (MTJJs) extend conventional two-terminal Josephson physics to devices defined by multiple superconducting phases, where rich phenomena have been predicted. However, in conventional MTJJs, superconducting transport is often dominated by local coupling between neighboring leads, obscuring physics that goes beyond simple connectivity. Here, we realize a multiterminal Josephson network in a graphene Hall bar geometry with micron-scale dimensions, enabling substantial nonlocal coupling between distant terminals. Our key observation is a pronounced negative differential longitudinal resistance (NDLR) in the four-probe response near switching currents, while no such feature appears in two-terminal measurements. We reproduce the NDLR response with a network containing the interplay between two distinct forms of nonlocality: nonlocal Josephson coupling, which determines the switching sequence of the superconducting network, and nonlocal normal-state conductance, which redistributes current after different regions switch. Our results highlight the potential of the emergent NDLR for characterizing superconductor--semiconductor hybrid systems and for nonlinear circuits. Further, this Josephson Hall bar geometry provides a platform for exploring the interplay between mesoscopic superconductivity and many-body ground states in graphene-based heterostructures.

</details>


### [89] [Vacuum-field-mediated Landau-level reconstruction in a cavity-coupled two-dimensional electron gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12956)
*Zi-Heng Zhu,Yu Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在静态非共振极限下，研究整数量子霍尔（integer quantum Hall）二维电子气与单一空间调制腔模耦合；虚光子交换产生周期性能量重整化与抗磁标量势，形成动量转移为 2k₀ 的有限 k₀ 动量阶梯，重构朗道能级与边缘态但保持整数拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究空间局域、有限动量（finite-k₀）的真空场机制如何影响量子霍尔谱与输运，并与腔诱导的全对全跃迁（all-to-all hopping）机制形成互补认识。

Method: 同时保留顺磁（paramagnetic）与抗磁（diamagnetic）贡献，推导规范完备的单粒子哈密顿量；对角化条带（strip）哈密顿量，并计算占据子空间陈数（Chern number）与条带 Kubo 响应。

Result: 阶梯重构朗道能级带结构并重新分配（而非倍增）手性边缘态（chiral edge states）；ν=1,2,3 时陈数保持 C_ν=ν（0≤λ_cav≤0.5），体隙减小但不闭合；霍尔台阶能量发生位移，有限宽度下平台高度非整数并随 L_x 增大趋近整数；阶梯在 k₀ℓ_B=0.5 时谱分辨、在 k₀ℓ_B=0.1 时坍缩。

Conclusion: 空间局域、有限 k₀ 的动量阶梯可作为清洁系统中结构化真空场重构量子霍尔谱与条带输运的机制，且不改变整数拓扑，与腔介导的全对全跃迁形成互补。

Abstract: We study a two-dimensional electron gas in the integer quantum Hall regime coupled to a single spatially modulated cavity mode in the static off-resonant limit. Retaining both paramagnetic and diamagnetic contributions on equal footing, we derive a gauge complete single-particle Hamiltonian in which virtual photon exchange generates a periodic kinetic renormalization and a diamagnetic scalar potential. Both terms transfer momentum by $2k_0$ and displace guiding centers by $ΔX=2k_0\ell_B^2$, forming a finite-$k_0$ momentum ladder. Diagonalizing the strip Hamiltonian, we find that the ladder reconstructs the Landau-level band structure and redistributes, rather than multiplies, the associated chiral edge states. Occupied-subspace Chern numbers remain $C_ν=ν$ for $ν=1,2,3$ throughout $0\leλ_{\mathrm{cav}}\le 0.5$, while the bulk gaps decrease without closing. Strip Kubo responses show cavity-induced displacements of the Hall-step energies; at finite width the plateau heights are noninteger and approach the integers as $L_x$ increases. The ladder is spectrally resolved at $k_0\ell_B=0.5$ and collapses at $k_0\ell_B=0.1$. These results isolate a spatially local, finite-$k_0$ momentum ladder as a clean-system mechanism by which a structured vacuum field reconstructs quantum Hall spectra and strip transport without changing the integer topology, complementary to cavity-mediated all-to-all hopping.

</details>


### [90] [Parity-Anomalous Hall Response of Chiral Lifshitz Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13017)
*Yanmiao Han,Rundong Zhao,Yu-Hao Wan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究二维手性 Lifshitz 费米子（chiral Lifshitz fermions）的宇称反常（parity anomaly）响应，指出低能耦合为 (qx+iqy)^N 的节点其费米环缠绕数 W = N 给出 σxy^anom = (W/2)e²/h，并在手性多层 Haldane 模型中验证该机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个无质量狄拉克费米子（massless Dirac fermion）的宇称反常表现为半量子化霍尔响应，但该响应在更一般的高阶色散节点中的推广尚不明确。本文旨在考察二维手性 Lifshitz 费米子对应的反常响应机制。

Method: 理论分析节点低能耦合 (qx+iqy)^N 与费米环缠绕数 W = N 的关系；在调至谷选择性临界线（valley-selective critical line）的手性多层 Haldane 模型（AB、ABC、ABCA 堆叠）中计算投影赝自旋织构（pseudospin textures）、Kubo 计算、平衡边界电流积分以及带退相干的六端输运模拟。

Result: 投影赝自旋织构给出 AB、ABC、ABCA 堆叠的 W = 2、3、4，Kubo 计算得到 σxy = (1, 3/2, 2)e²/h，平衡边界电流积分复现同一 W/2 序列；六端退相干模拟显示霍尔电导在 W/2 处量子化，而纵向电导保持有限。

Conclusion: 无质量手性 Lifshitz 费米子构成宇称反常霍尔响应的一种由缠绕数控制的高阶推广；即使 W/2 为整数，其性质也不同于量子反常霍尔绝缘态（quantum anomalous Hall insulating state）。

Abstract: The parity anomaly of a single massless Dirac fermion is manifested as a half-quantized Hall response once the required parity-breaking regularization is supplied. We study the corresponding response for two-dimensional chiral Lifshitz fermions. For a node with low-energy coupling (qx +iqy)^N, the Fermi-loop winding number W = N gives an anomalous Hall response σxy^anom = (W/2)e^2/h. We examine this mechanism in chiral multilayer Haldane models tuned to a valley-selective critical line, where a single gapless chiral Lifshitz fermion remains at the K valley. For the AB, ABC, and ABCA stacks, the projected pseudospin textures give W = 2, 3, 4, while Kubo calculations yield σxy = (1, 3/2, 2)e^2/h; integrated equilibrium boundary currents reproduce the same W/2 sequence. Six-terminal simulations with dephasing further show that the Hall conductivity is quantized at W/2 while the longitudinal conductivity remains finite. These results identify gapless chiral Lifshitz fermions as a higher-order, winding-controlled generalization of the parity-anomalous Hall response, distinct from a quantum anomalous Hall insulating state even when W/2 is an integer.

</details>


### [91] [Orbital transfer reveals material-selective structure in Rydberg excitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13149)
*Mengli Wang,Peng Chen,Wenqi Xue,Haonan Xia,Hong Zhang,Benniu Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作以 1s、2p、2s 能级确定标量 Rytova-Keldysh（RK）哈密顿量，并检验其对被排除的 3p 态的迁移能力，发现 MoSe2 与 WS2 中迁移偏差符号相反，并据此给出可检验的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子级薄半导体中的 Rydberg 激子（Rydberg excitons）可敏感反映介电屏蔽（dielectric screening）与电子结构。随着光谱分辨率提高，核心问题是：有效哈密顿量除能重现用于拟合的能级外，能否预测更多材料信息。

Method: 以 1s、2p、2s 三个能级确定标量 Rytova-Keldysh 哈密顿量，将其迁移至未参与拟合的 3p 态进行检验；并设置独立的高场标定基准，与纯二维库仑（two-dimensional Coulomb）参考比较，随后分离带隙（gap）与共同尺度（common-scale）贡献以分析残差。

Result: 迁移偏差符号相反：MoSe2 中观测 3p 比预测低 7.38 meV，WS2 中则高 5.58 meV，且在已报道的完整 ±2σ 峰误差范围内符号保持一致。在高场标定基准中，RK 消除了纯二维库仑参考 66.8% 的轨道误差（orbital error）。分离带隙与共同尺度贡献后，残差显示材料相关的轨道模式。对 WS2，由测得的 2p/3p 曲率给出参数固定的预测 Q4p=+4.08 meV。

Conclusion: 结果表明，性能良好的光谱模型可被检验其可迁移的物理信息，而下一个未测量态提供了独立的实验验证途径，并对简单的多通道（multichannel）描述构成约束。

Abstract: Rydberg excitons in atomically thin semiconductors offer a sensitive view of dielectric screening and electronic structure. As their spectra become increasingly well resolved, a central question is how much material information an effective Hamiltonian can predict beyond the states used to determine it. Using the 1s, 2p and 2s levels to determine a scalar Rytova-Keldysh Hamiltonian, we test its transfer to the excluded 3p state. The transfer deviations have opposite signs: the observed 3p lies 7.38 meV below the prediction in MoSe2 and 5.58 meV above it in WS2; the opposite signs persist across the complete reported +/-2-sigma peak-error boxes. In a separate high-field-calibrated benchmark, RK removes 66.8% of the orbital error of a pure two-dimensional Coulomb reference, making the remaining state-selective deviations particularly informative. After separating gap and common-scale contributions, the residuals reveal distinct material-dependent orbital patterns and constrain simple multichannel descriptions. For WS2, the measured 2p/3p curvatures yield a parameter-fixed prediction, Q4p=+4.08 meV, for a future same-platform measurement. Together these results show how a well-performing spectral model can be tested for transferable physical information, with the next unmeasured state providing an independent experimental check.

</details>


### [92] [Equal-Spin local and nonlocal Andreev reflection in altermagnet/superconductor/altermagnet junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13193)
*Kai-Yi Lyu,Xing Wang,Yu-Xian Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究在交错磁体/超导体/交错磁体（AM/SC/AM）结中考察等自旋安德烈夫反射（equal-spin Andreev reflection），指出奈尔矢量（Néel vector）的旋转是其直接成因，晶向角与奈尔矢量可共同调控自旋相关的超导输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清AM/SC/AM结中等自旋安德烈夫反射的产生机制与条件，并区分晶向角（实空间）与奈尔矢量（自旋空间）在其中的不同作用。

Method: 采用AM/SC/AM结的理论输运分析，重点考察晶体取向角（crystal orientation angle）与奈尔矢量对电子、空穴自旋相关输运通道的影响，并计算局域与交叉安德烈夫反射（local and crossed Andreev reflection）系数。

Result: 晶向角决定自旋相关的电子与空穴输运通道，导致取向依赖的局域与交叉安德烈夫反射；旋转奈尔矢量显著增加电子与空穴的自旋翻转（spin-flip）通道，从而产生等自旋安德烈夫反射。当θ=π/2时，α=0与α=π/2的自旋相关透射通道完全相同，但安德烈夫反射系数仍不同，反映奈尔矢量与晶向角的不同角色。

Conclusion: 通过调控晶向角与奈尔矢量可实现取向依赖的常规与等自旋安德烈夫反射；奈尔矢量旋转是AM/SC/AM结中等自旋安德烈夫反射的直接原因，为在AM异质结中实现自旋相关超导输运现象提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We investigated the equal-spin Andreev reflection phenomenon in an altermagnet/super\-conductor/altermagnet (AM/SC/AM) junction, focusing on the roles of the crystal orientation angle and the Néel vector. The crystal orientation angle in the AM effectively determines the spin-dependent transport channels for both electrons and holes, leading to orientation-dependent local and crossed Andreev reflection in the AM/SC/AM junction. We studied how and under what conditions this equal-spin Andreev reflection occurs. Rotating the Néel vector in the AM substantially increases spin-flip channels for both electrons and holes, giving rise to equal-spin Andreev reflection. When the Néel vector $θ=π/2$, the spin-dependent transmission channels for crystal orientations $α=0$ and $α=π/2$ are completely the same, the Andreev reflection coefficients remain different, reflecting the distinct roles of the Néel vector in spin space and the crystal orientation in real space. By tuning the crystal orientation angle and the Néel vector, orientation-dependent conventional and equal-spin Andreev reflection can be realized. This study reveals that Néel vector rotation is the direct cause of equal-spin Andreev reflection in AM/SC/AM junctions and provides a viable pathway for realizing spin-dependent superconducting transport phenomena in AM heterojunctions.

</details>


### [93] [Graphene double quantum dot as a solid-state two-level system in the THz range](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13196)
*Alexis Wietzke,Solen Coeymans,Benjamin Hue,Simon Messelot,Aurelien Schmitt,Michael Rosticher,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Jerome Tignon,Sukhdeep Dhillon,Rebeca Ribeiro,Audrey Cottet,Matthieu Delbecq,Takis Kontos,Juliette Mangeney*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作基于六方氮化硼（hBN）封装的石墨烯双量子点（double quantum dot），实现了跃迁频率位于太赫兹（THz）波段的二能级系统。通过低温输运谱与基于非平衡格林函数（nonequilibrium Green's function, NEGF）的建模，观测到键态与反键态分离达 0.6 meV（对应 0.145 THz）的人工分子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物质基二能级系统（如里德堡原子、离子阱、超导电路、分子与量子点）的跃迁频率多处于微波或光学波段，而将其拓展至太赫兹（THz）波段可借助 THz 辐射的独特性质带来诸多优势。因此需要一种可在 THz 频段工作的二能级平台。

Method: 采用 hBN 封装的石墨烯双量子点作为器件，在暗场条件下进行低温输运谱（low-temperature transport spectroscopy）测量，并结合基于非平衡格林函数（nonequilibrium Green's function formalism）的理论建模进行分析。

Result: 实验与建模共同证实了人工分子（artificial molecule）的形成，其键态（bonding state）与反键态（antibonding state）能量间隔最高达 0.6 meV，对应共振频率 0.145 THz。该方法可直接获取开放双量子点系统的重要参数（如隧穿率），为在 THz 频率下难以实现的传统技术（如光子辅助隧穿 photon-assisted tunneling）提供了实用替代方案。

Conclusion: 所实现的二维材料基 THz 二能级系统兼容片上直接集成，可作为未来 THz 量子技术的关键构建单元。此外，借助单电子跃迁，将该器件与 THz 谐振腔耦合，有望在单跃迁与单腔耦合的基本极限下探索超强光-物质相互作用（ultra-strong light-matter interactions），超越现有基于集体跃迁的 THz 量子系统所能达到的范围。

Abstract: Matter-based two-level quantum systems are fundamental building blocks of quantum technologies, underpinning applications in quantum computing, simulation, communication, and sensing. Today, most of these two-level quantum systems, including Rydberg atoms, trapped ions, superconducting circuits, molecules, and quantum dots, involve transition frequencies in microwave or optical domains. However, extending their transition frequency to the THz spectral range could provide numerous benefits, leveraging the unique properties of THz radiation. Here, we show that an hBN-encapsulated graphene double quantum dot realizes a two-level system with a transition frequency in the THz spectral range. Using low-temperature transport spectroscopy in the dark combined with modeling based on nonequilibrium Green's function formalism, we demonstrate the formation of an artificial molecule, featuring bonding and antibonding states separated by an energy of up to 0.6 meV, corresponding to a resonance frequency of 0.145 THz. This approach provides direct access to important parameters of open double quantum dot systems, including tunneling rates, offering a practical alternative to conventional techniques such as photon-assisted tunneling, which are challenging to implement at THz frequencies. The realized 2D-material-based THz two-level system, compatible with direct on-chip integration, could serve as a key building block for future THz quantum technologies. Furthermore, by involving a single-electron transition, coupling these devices to a THz resonator could enable the exploration of ultra-strong light-matter interactions at the fundamental limit of a single transition coupled to a single cavity, beyond the reach of existing THz quantum systems based on collective transitions.

</details>


### [94] [Theory of exciton-exciton interactions in crystalline systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13246)
*L. Maisel Licerán,H. T. C. Stoof*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一个普适的激子-激子相互作用（exciton-exciton interactions）微观理论，适用于几乎任意晶体半导体体系，并可处理能带拓扑（band topology）、量子几何（quantum geometry）与莫尔效应（moiré effects）显著的体系，从而为双激子（biexciton）谱与激子多体相（excitonic many-body phases）的定量计算提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子（exciton）相互作用影响半导体的光学性质及其集体行为与关联相，但现有研究缺乏考虑激子作为复合准粒子（composite quasiparticle）完整性质的微观理论。作者此前的理论（Physical Review B 113, 155440 (2026)）仅适用于能带杂化较弱的传统半导体，因此需要推广到更广泛的体系。

Method: 作者构建了一个激子-激子相互作用的普适解析理论（analytical theory），该理论建立在此前工作的基础上，但推广至几乎任意晶体半导体体系；此外，还给出了在具有非平庸量子度规（nontrivial quantum metric）的平带（flat bands）简单体系中的计算结果。

Result: 在具有非平庸量子度规的平带体系中，双激子谱（biexcitonic spectrum）与无量子几何情形相比存在强烈差异。

Conclusion: 该理论可应用于能带拓扑、量子几何与莫尔效应起重要作用体系中的双激子形成与激子关联相研究，为现代凝聚态体系中双激子谱与激子多体相的精确定量计算开辟了道路。

Abstract: Excitons are bound electron-hole pairs in semiconductors whose interactions influence optical properties and determine their collective behavior and emergent correlated phases. Despite the theoretical and experimental significance of these phenomena, a microscopic theory of exciton-exciton interactions that takes into account the full composite nature of these quasiparticles is lacking. Here, we present a general theory of exciton-exciton interactions that builds upon our previous publication [Physical Review B 113, 155440 (2026)], which applied only to conventional semiconductors with weak band hybridization. By contrast, the theory presented here is valid in virtually any crystalline semiconducting system and can be applied to the study of biexciton formation and correlated excitonic phases in systems where band topology, quantum geometry, and moiré effects play a significant role. In addition to the analytical theory, we also present results in simple systems with flat bands with a nontrivial quantum metric and show that the biexcitonic spectrum differs strongly from that in the case without quantum geometry. Our theory paves the way for accurate quantitative calculations of biexciton spectra and excitonic many-body phases in modern condensed-matter systems.

</details>


### [95] [Atomic Edge Resonators Flatten Phonon Bands in Biphenylene Nanoribbons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13342)
*V. L. Katkov,N. M. Chtchelkatchev,V. A. Osipov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 双苯烯纳米带（biphenylene nanoribbons）中的 H–F 边缘配合物（H–F edge complexes）充当本征原子谐振器（intrinsic atomic resonators），使低频声子支（phonon branches）趋于平坦，并通过弱色散模式显著影响热致带隙收窄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在阐明边缘化学（edge chemistry）如何调控纳米带的低频晶格动力学，并建立局域原子共振与整条纳米带电子响应之间的联系。此前对边缘结构如何产生近平坦声子带（nearly flat phonon bands）及其对电子性质的影响尚缺乏明确机制说明。

Method: 结合机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials）、冻结声子计算（frozen-phonon calculations）与密度泛函紧束缚（density-functional tight binding），并辅以位移剖面分析与边缘质量调谐来识别共振机制，同时考察六方氮化硼（hexagonal boron nitride）衬底的影响。

Result: 随波矢（wave vector）增大，弯曲与扭转振动（flexural and twisting vibrations）聚集于边缘，而碳骨架运动被抑制；位移剖面与边缘质量调谐确证了类似调谐质量阻尼器（tuned mass damper）的局域共振机制。弱色散模式对热致带隙收窄（thermal band-gap narrowing）贡献显著，零点运动（zero-point motion）使计算所得带隙闭合向实验观测的纳米带宽度偏移。六方氮化硼衬底保留主要谱特征，同时减弱热重正化（thermal renormalization）。

Conclusion: 边缘化学可作为获得近平坦声子带的途径，并将局域原子共振与整条纳米带的电子响应相关联；衬底可在保持主要声子谱特征的同时调节热效应。

Abstract: We show that H--F edge complexes in biphenylene nanoribbons act as intrinsic atomic resonators, flattening low-frequency phonon branches. With increasing wave vector, flexural and twisting vibrations concentrate at the edges while motion of the carbon backbone is suppressed. Displacement profiles and edge-mass tuning identify a local-resonance mechanism, an atomic counterpart of a tuned mass damper. Combining machine-learned interatomic potentials, frozen-phonon calculations, and density-functional tight binding, we find that weakly dispersive modes contribute substantially to thermal band-gap narrowing. Zero-point motion shifts the calculated gap closure toward the experimentally observed ribbon widths. A hexagonal boron nitride substrate preserves the principal spectral features while reducing thermal renormalization. These results establish edge chemistry as a route to nearly flat phonon bands and connect a local atomic resonance to the electronic response of an entire nanoribbon.

</details>


### [96] [Pulse-by-pulse imaging of reversible, collective reshaping in laser-excited gold nanorods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13383)
*Mees Dieperink,Loriane Monin,Ichiro Ohnishi,Takeo Sasaki,Yu Jimbo,Erik Garnett,Wiebke Albrecht*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究利用脉冲光激发下透射电子显微镜（transmission electron microscope, TEM）原位观测金纳米棒（gold nanorods），逐帧量化其结构转变，揭示了一个此前未被发现的动态重塑区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米材料（nanomaterials）性质由原子结构决定，若能按需驱动结构变化即可实现性质可编程；脉冲光因波长、注量、偏振与脉冲时序可调，是最可控的途径，但其转变的时空动力学此前只能通过转变前后的快照间接推断。

Method: 在透射电子显微镜下对金纳米棒施加脉冲光激发（pulsed optical excitation），逐帧（frame by frame）追踪并量化其结构转变过程。

Result: 纳米棒进入意想不到的动态区间：材料在颗粒内部与颗粒之间重新分布，孪晶界（twin boundaries）形核并传播，相距数百纳米的金纳米棒协同重塑，单根纳米棒几乎消失后又恢复原状，表明存在结构记忆（structural memory）。

Conclusion: 以脉冲同步的原位电子显微镜（pulse-synchronized in situ electron microscopy）驱动并追踪这些转变，有望将光致重塑（light-induced reshaping）转变为按需设定结构乃至性质的工具。

Abstract: Nanomaterial properties are governed by atomic structure, and driving structural changes on demand could enable materials with programmable properties. Pulsed light is the most controllable route: wavelength, fluence, polarization and pulse timing can each be tuned to inject energy with nanometre and picosecond precision and mould atomic positions. Yet the spatiotemporal dynamics of these transformations have remained hidden, inferred only from before-and-after snapshots. Here we track gold nanorods in a transmission electron microscope under pulsed optical excitation, quantifying the structural transition frame by frame. The rods enter an unexpectedly dynamic regime: material redistributes within and between particles, twin boundaries nucleate and propagate, rods hundreds of nanometres apart reshape in concert, and individual rods almost vanish before recovering their original shape, indicating a structural memory. Driving and following these transformations with pulse-synchronized in situ electron microscopy could turn light-induced reshaping into a tool for setting structure, and consequently properties, on demand.

</details>


### [97] [Controlling the Atomic Reconstruction in Twisted MoSe$_2$/WSe$_2$ with a Monolayer hBN Spacer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13493)
*Johannes Figueiredo,Kaiyuan Chen,Aubrey Penn,Mirco Troue,Sebastian Loy,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Frances M. Ross,Julian Klein,Alexander W. Holleitner*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在近对齐（near-aligned）的 MoSe$_2$/WSe$_2$ 异质双层中插入单层或双层 hBN 间隔层（spacer），可抑制原子重构（atomic reconstruction）并显著提升晶格均匀性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转范德瓦尔斯异质双层（twisted van der Waals heterobilayer）是研究集体激子（collective exciton）多体现象的重要平台，但近对齐结构常出现局部各向异性原子重构，破坏其横向均匀性，从而制约相关研究。

Method: 以 hBN 封装的近对齐 MoSe$_2$/WSe$_2$ 异质双层为对象，对比单层与双层 hBN 间隔层对相邻区域原子级重排的调控作用；采用高角环形暗场 STEM（high-angle annular dark-field STEM）直接分辨两层原子对齐，并以 4D-STEM 定量映射局域旋转、膨胀与异质应变（heterostrain）。

Result: STEM 结果显示，间隔层可抑制重构并大幅提升结构均匀性；4D-STEM 定量表明，有间隔层的区域与刚性莫尔超晶格（rigid moiré superlattice）模拟一致，而无间隔层时结构参数分布展宽达两到三倍。

Conclusion: 通过抑制原子重构，超薄 hBN 间隔层为提升 MoSe$_2$/WSe$_2$ 异质双层的晶格均匀性提供了有效途径，有助于面向集体激子与光电子现象的应用。

Abstract: Twisted van der Waals heterobilayers are a promising material platform for studying collective exciton many-body phenomena, but near-aligned structures often exhibit a local, anisotropic atomic reconstruction that compromises lateral homogeneity. The present study describes how atomic-scale rearrangement in adjacent regions of hBN-encapsulated, near-aligned MoSe$_2$/WSe$_2$ heterobilayers is controlled by the presence of a mono- or bilayer hBN spacer between the two monolayers. High-angle annular dark-field STEM directly resolves the atomic alignment in both layers and shows that the spacer suppresses the reconstruction and strongly increases the structural uniformity of the heterobilayers. 4D-STEM quantitatively maps local rotation, dilation, and heterostrain, revealing that the separated regions remain consistent with rigid moiré superlattice simulations, whereas without the spacer the structural parameters have two to three times broader distributions. By suppressing reconstruction, ultrathin hBN spacers therefore provide an effective route to enhance lattice homogeneity in MoSe$_2$/WSe$_2$ heterobilayers targeting collective excitonic and optoelectronic phenomena.

</details>


### [98] [Giant geometric nonlinear transport in inhomogeneously strained Bi$_2$Se$_3$ single crystalline slabs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13542)
*Igor Veremchuk,Pavlo Makushko,Anita Guarino,Muhammad Waqee Ur Rehman,Rosalba Tatiana Fittipaldi,Tobias Kosub,Alexej Pashkin,Fabian Ganss,Oleg A. Petrenko,Proloy T. Das,Rui Xu,Rhonald Burgos Atencia,Debottam Mandal,Antonio Vecchione,Carmine Ortix,Denys Makarov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在拓扑绝缘体 Bi₂Se₃ 的宏观非均匀应变块体中，观测到创纪录的 10⁴ μm/V 非线性响应度，较薄膜对应物提升六个数量级，归因于体内复杂应变梯度驱动的经典几何非线性输运效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非中心对称材料（non-centrosymmetric materials）中的非线性电导可产生二次谐波电压，是零阈值电磁能量收集器与宽带频率上转换器的关键；上述应用均要求最大化非线性响应度（nonlinear responsivity）。

Method: 采用宏观尺度、非均匀应变的拓扑绝缘体（topological insulator）Bi₂Se₃ 块体样品，测量其非线性输运响应。

Result: 获得 10⁴ μm/V 的创纪录非线性响应度，相比薄膜对应物提升六个数量级；该增强被归因于材料内部复杂应变梯度（strain gradients）所驱动的经典几何非线性输运效应（geometric nonlinear transport）。

Conclusion: 所揭示的几何机制（类似此前仅在微尺度图案化结构中观测到的几何非线性霍尔效应 geometric nonlinear Hall）表明，宏观应变工程（macroscopic strain engineering）是增强非线性输运的有效途径，并可直接推广至过渡金属硫族化合物（transition metal dichalcogenides）与莫尔异质结构（moiré heterostructures）等层状体系。

Abstract: Electrical transport in non-centrosymmetric materials exhibits a nonlinear conductivity tensor component that generates second-harmonic voltages in response to applied electric fields. Harnessing this effect is essential for developing zero-threshold electromagnetic energy harvesters and broadband frequency upconverters, both of which require maximizing the nonlinear responsivity. Here, we demonstrate that inhomogeneously strained macroscopic slabs of the topological insulator Bi$_2$Se$_3$ achieve a record-high nonlinear responsivity of $10^4\,μ$m/V: a six orders of magnitude boost compared to its thin-film counterparts. We attribute this giant enhancement to a classical geometric nonlinear transport effect, which is driven by complex strain gradients within the bulk material. Notably, a similar geometric mechanism known as geometric nonlinear Hall was previously observed only in microscale-patterned structures. Our findings establish macroscopic strain engineering as a powerful route to enhance nonlinear transport, with immediate relevance to a broad class of layered systems, including transition metal dichalcogenides and moiré heterostructures.

</details>


### [99] [Parametric Glassiness in Graphene-based Electronic Lévy Glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13638)
*Caue N. da Silva,Diego B. Fonseca,Adauto J. F. de Souza,Antônio M. S. Macêdo,Luiz Felipe C. Pereira,Anderson L. R. Barbosa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究在石墨烯基电子莱维玻璃（electronic Lévy glass）中观测到类复制对称破缺（replica symmetry breaking, RSB）的重叠分布现象，揭示无热力学稳定玻璃相时仍可出现参数敏感的介观玻璃性（mesoscopic glassiness）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂无序导体即使在缺乏热力学稳定玻璃相时，也可能表现出高度结构化且对参数敏感的输运行为，因此需要一种诊断工具来刻画这种组织化。原为复制对称破缺（RSB）发展的重叠分布（overlap distributions）具备此潜力，但须将其解释与平衡态 Parisi 构造相区分。

Method: 在石墨烯基电子莱维玻璃中调控费米能（Fermi energy），以此控制传播波模式数目，并测量重叠分布随参数的变化；同时对比石墨烯基与常规二维电子气（two-dimensional electron gas, 2DEG）电子莱维玻璃。此外，分析样品分辨的重叠分布与归一化电导方差。

Result: 通过调节费米能可观察到重叠分布从窄中心化向宽分布或双峰分布的可重入（reentrant）变化。该现象在石墨烯基与常规 2DEG 电子莱维玻璃中均出现，显示其普适性。样品分辨结果显示该效应源于每种无序构型（disorder landscape）的內禀特性；归一化电导方差表明绝对方差的增长主要源于平均电导的增加。

Conclusion: 重叠可被解释为层次化组织输运路径之间相关性的度量，而可重入行为则被视为钉扎（pinning）、参数诱导重组以及相干输运中介观混沌（mesoscopic chaos）的证据。

Abstract: Complex disordered conductors can display highly structured and parameter-sensitive transport even in the absence of a thermodynamically stable glass phase. Overlap distributions originally developed for replica symmetry breaking (RSB) provide a useful diagnostic of this organization, provided that their interpretation is distinguished from the equilibrium Parisi construction. In this study, we report RSB-like overlap phenomenology and parametric mesoscopic glassiness in a graphene-based electronic Lévy glass, a device that mimics optical phenomena via electronic processes. By tuning the Fermi energy, we can control the number of propagating wave modes and observe reentrant changes from narrowly centered to broad or bimodal overlap distributions. The phenomenology is observed in both graphene-based and conventional two-dimensional electron-gas electronic Lévy glasses, indicating its generality. Sample-resolved overlap distributions show that the effect is intrinsic to each disorder landscape, whereas the normalized conductance variance shows that the growth of the absolute variance is mainly caused by the increasing mean conductance. We therefore interpret the overlap as a measure of correlation among hierarchically organized transport pathways and the reentrant behavior as evidence of pinning, parameter-induced reorganizations, and mesoscopic chaos in coherent transport.

</details>

### [100] [Some remarks on the connection between generative diffusion and optimal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13614)
*Giulio Biroli,Marc Mézard*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文讨论生成扩散（generative diffusion）与最优传输（optimal transport）之间的三个简单联系：前向加噪的联合分布是熵正则最优传输问题的解；扩散与流匹配（flow matching）中模式分离的动力学不稳定性在最优传输中表现为指数薄的边界层；基于得分（score）的反向过程满足漂移型变分原理，其最小代价为初始与最终分布的熵差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示生成扩散与最优传输之间的深层理论关联，并厘清扩散、流匹配与最优传输在生成过程中模式涌现机制上的差异。

Method: 通过理论分析建立三个联系：将Ornstein-Uhlenbeck加噪的联合律刻画为熵正则最优传输解；以高斯混合（Gaussian mixtures）作为高维例子比较扩散、流匹配与最优传输的模式分离方式；对一般约束势下的前向动力学推导反向过程的漂移型变分原理。

Result: 数据与其Ornstein-Uhlenbeck加噪的联合律在任意时刻均为熵最优传输问题的解，温度由噪声水平设定，反向扩散可视为逐步降低熵正则化的退火过程；扩散与流匹配中分离模式的动力学不稳定性，在最优传输中变为生成初期指数薄的边界层，并被熵正则化圆滑；反向过程满足变分原理，其最小代价等于初始与最终分布的熵差。

Conclusion: 生成扩散与最优传输之间存在熵正则化退火、模式分离机制对应以及熵差变分原理三方面的联系，为理解扩散模型的生成动力学提供了最优传输视角。

Abstract: We discuss three simple connections between generative diffusion and optimal transport. First, the joint law of the data and of their Ornstein-Uhlenbeck noising is, at every time, the solution of an entropic optimal-transport problem, at a temperature set by the noise level. Backward diffusion can consequently be read as an annealing which gradually decreases the entropic regularization. Second, we study the speciation transition--how the modes of the data distribution emerge during generation--and compare how it happens in diffusion and flow matching with respect to optimal transport. Using Gaussian mixtures as high-dimensional example, we show that the dynamical instability through which diffusion and flow matching separate the modes becomes, for optimal transport, an exponentially thin boundary layer at the beginning of generation, which is rounded by entropic regularization. Third, considering a generic forward dynamics with a general confining potential, we show that the backward process driven by the score also satisfies a drift-based variational principle whose minimal cost is the entropy difference between initial and final distributions.

</details>

### [101] [Non-resonant angles in a Robin eigenvalue problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13601)
*Konstantin Pankrashkin*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文确定了多边形中带强吸收 Robin 边界条件的 Laplacian 特征值的非共振角（non-resonant angles）的完整范围，证明非共振条件仅对形如 π/(2n+1)（n=1,2,3,…）的开角失效，并以此解决了 Dauge 与 Lafranche 提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期研究已表明，在多边形中若每个角为凹角或非共振角，则单个特征值的渐近行为由边界上的一维有效算子（effective one-dimensional operator）决定，且角点处满足 Dirichlet 边界条件；但当时仅证明直角与钝角满足该非共振条件，非共振角的完整范围尚不明确。

Method: 通过分析截断扇形（truncated sectors）上的特征值界，确定非共振条件成立与失效的角度范围，并将该临界条件等价地刻画为特征值计数函数（eigenvalue counting function）的跳变以及有界广义特征函数（bounded generalized eigenfunctions）的存在性。

Result: 非共振条件仅在开角为 π/(2n+1)（n=1,2,3,…）时失效；这些临界角可用特征值计数函数的跳变与有界广义特征函数的存在性等价刻画，从而给出非共振角的完整范围。

Conclusion: 研究完整确定了非共振角的取值范围，解决了 Dauge 与 Lafranche 曾提出并由数值结果支持的猜想。

Abstract: In an earlier study of the Laplacian eigenvalues with strongly attractive Robin boundary conditions in polygons it was found that if each corner is either concave or non-resonant, then the asymptotic behavior of the individual eigenvalues is determined by an effective one-dimensional operator on the boundary with Dirichlet boundary conditions at the corners. The non-resonance condition was formulated in terms of an eigenvalue bound on truncated sectors, and it was only shown to be satisfied by the right and obtuse opening angles. In the present work, we determine the full range of non-resonant angles: it turns out that the non-resonance condition fails only for the opening angles $\fracπ{2n+1}$ with $n=1,2,3,\dots$, and these critical values are equivalently characterized in terms of the jumps of the eigenvalue counting function and the existence of bounded generalized eigenfunctions. This resolves the numerically supported conjecture stated by Dauge and Lafranche.

</details>


<div id='hep-ex'></div>

# hep-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [\textbf{\textsc{HepUKE}}: Uncertainty-aware Knowledge Evolution for High Energy Physics Analysis Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12862)
*Zijie Shang,Ke Li,Junkun Jiao,Shengchun Wang,Changzheng Yuan,Xiongfei Wang,Beijiang Liu,Weimin Song,Xiaoshen Kang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: HepUKE 是一个面向高能物理（HEP）的不确定性感知知识演化系统，利用信息熵统一 LLM 生成的统计不确定性与 HEP 方法选择的系统不确定性，构建自演化 DSL 知识库并减少检索轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识库系统将检索到的证据视为同等可信，且无法从所辅助的分析中积累知识，因而难以对新颖分析提供战略性指导。该工作借鉴 HEP 中标准的不确定性处理方式，旨在解决证据可信度区分与知识持续演化的问题。

Method: 提出 HepUKE，将信息熵（information entropy）用于统一 LLM 生成的统计不确定性（statistical uncertainty）与 HEP 方法选择的系统不确定性（systematic uncertainty），据此构建自演化的领域特定语言（DSL）知识库并发布专家标注基准。通过采样 DSL 生成的信息熵量化逐条证据置信度，高置信先例约束程序合成，缺乏近似先例的分析经验证后写回；并引入早停控制器（early-stopping controller），融合答案后验的后向平稳性检验与前向边际信息增益估计。

Result: 在 1000 对 BESIII PhysicsQA 基准上，HepUKE 将检索轮次减少 43.0%，同时保持大部分答案质量；在域外 HotpotQA 基准上检索轮次减少 14.5%，呈现类似的质量—效率权衡。代码与数据集已公开。

Conclusion: HepUKE 通过不确定性感知的证据置信度量化与知识写回机制，实现知识库的持续演化，并在域内与域外基准上取得检索效率与答案质量的良好平衡。

Abstract: Knowledge bases are essential to agents for high energy physics (HEP), supplying few-shot evidence for previously unseen tasks. Their principal utility lies in strategic guidance for novel analyses rather than ready-made answers or reusable code. Prevailing systems, however, treat all retrieved evidence as equally trustworthy and fail to accrue knowledge from the analyses under assistance. In this paper, inspired by the standard HEP treatment of uncertainty, we propose \textbf{\textsc{\underline{HepUKE}}}: an \textbf{\underline{U}}ncertainty-aware \textbf{\underline{K}}nowledge \textbf{\underline{E}}volution system for \textbf{\underline{HEP}} that repurposes information entropy to unify statistical uncertainty from LLM generation and systematic uncertainty from HEP methodological choices. Upon it, we construct a self-evolving domain-specific-language (DSL) knowledge base and release a large-scale expert-annotated HEP physics-analysis benchmark. Per-evidence confidence is quantified via the information entropy of sampled DSL generations: high-confidence precedents constrain program synthesis, whereas analyses lacking close precedents are validated and written back, sustaining continual knowledge evolution. To handle the highly redundant yet logically stringent nature of HEP knowledge, we introduce an early-stopping controller that fuses a backward stationarity test on the answer posterior with a forward marginal information gain estimate. On the 1,000-pair BESIII PhysicsQA benchmark, HepUKE reduces retrieval rounds by 43.0\% while preserving most answer quality, and on the out-of-domain HotpotQA benchmark, it reduces retrieval rounds by 14.5\% with a similar quality--efficiency trade-off. The code and datasets used in this work are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/HepUKE-32FF.

</details>

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器采集的(2712.4±14.4)×10⁶个ψ(3686)事件，对ψ(3686)→π⁰π⁰J/ψ衰变中的π⁰π⁰质量谱（mass spectrum）进行了高精度研究。在ππ质量阈值附近确认了此前在π⁺π⁻道观测到的增强结构，统计显著性超过10σ，Breit-Wigner拟合给出质量M=274.3±2.1_stat.±0.6_syst. MeV/c²、宽度Γ=17.1±1.5_stat.±0.5_syst. MeV，与π⁺π⁻道结果一致。在包含末态相互作用（final-state interactions）的分析中，手征微扰理论（chiral perturbation theory, ChPT）能描述0.3 GeV/c²以上的高质量区却无法重现阈值增强，而包含ψ(3686)的S波与D波混合结构的QCD多极展开（QCD multipole expansion, QCDME）模型能更好地描述完整的π⁰π⁰质量谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前在π⁺π⁻道中观测到ππ质量阈值附近的增强现象，需在π⁰π⁰道进行独立验证并提升测量精度。同时，需要借助包含末态相互作用的理论模型，判别该阈值增强的物理机制。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器采集的大统计量ψ(3686)数据，重建ψ(3686)→π⁰π⁰J/ψ衰变并对π⁰π⁰质量谱进行分析；采用Breit-Wigner函数拟合共振参数，并引入末态相互作用，分别使用手征微扰理论（ChPT）与QCD多极展开（QCDME）模型对数据进行比较分析。

Result: 在π⁰π⁰道确认了ππ质量阈值附近的增强，统计显著性超过10σ；拟合得到质量M=274.3±2.1_stat.±0.6_syst. MeV/c²、宽度Γ=17.1±1.5_stat.±0.5_syst. MeV，与π⁺π⁻道一致。ChPT模型能较好描述0.3 GeV/c²以上的高质量区但无法重现阈值增强；包含ψ(3686)的S波与D波混合结构的QCDME模型则能更好地描述完整的π⁰π⁰质量谱。

Conclusion: π⁰π⁰道中阈值增强的确认及其参数与π⁺π⁻道的一致性，支持该增强为真实物理效应；模型比较表明，需引入ψ(3686)的S波与D波混合结构方能解释完整的质量谱，QCDME框架优于ChPT对阈值增强的描述。

Abstract: Using a data sample of $(2712.4\pm14.4)\times 10^{6}$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector, we report a high-precision study of the $π^{0}π^{0}$ mass spectrum from $ψ(3686)\rightarrowπ^{0}π^{0}J/ψ$ decays. The enhancement previously observed near the $ππ$ mass threshold in the $π^+π^-$ channel is confirmed, with a statistical significance exceeding 10$σ$. A fit with a Breit-Wigner function yields a mass of $M = 274.3\pm 2.1_{\rm stat.}\pm0.6_{\rm syst.}~{\rm MeV}/c^2$ and a width of $Γ= 17.1\pm 1.5_{\rm stat.}\pm0.5_{\rm syst.}~{\rm MeV}$, consistent with those from the $π^{+}π^{-}$ channel. By including final-state interactions, we analyze the data using both chiral perturbation theory (ChPT) and QCD multipole expansion (QCDME). While the ChPT model describes the higher-mass region above 0.3 GeV/$c^2$ well, it fails to reproduce the significant threshold enhancement. In contrast, the QCDME model, which incorporates the $S$- and $D$-wave admixture structure of $ψ(3686)$, provides a better description of the full $π^{0}π^{0}$ mass spectrum.

</details>

K. Zhou,X. R. Zhou,X. Y. Zhou,Y. X. Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于 BESIII 探测器在 3.773 GeV 采集的 20.3 fb⁻¹ 正负电子对撞数据，对 D⁰→K_S⁰π⁺π⁻ 与 D⁰→K_L⁰π⁺π⁻ 衰变进行联合振幅分析（amplitude analysis），更新了 U 旋（U-spin）破缺参数 ρ̂ 的测量并给出强相位参数（strong-phase parameters）的模型预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前同类研究使用的数据量较小，U 旋破缺参数与强相位参数的精度受限；本研究利用显著更大的数据集，对该三体衰变（three-body decays）中的 CP 本征态共振模式（CP eigenstate resonant modes）进行更精确的检验。

Method: 使用 BESIII 探测器（BESIII detector）在质心能量（centre-of-mass energy）3.773 GeV 处采集的积分亮度（integrated luminosity）为 20.3 fb⁻¹ 的 e⁺e⁻ 对撞数据，对 D⁰→K_S⁰π⁺π⁻ 和 D⁰→K_L⁰π⁺π⁻ 两个衰变道同时进行振幅分析，提取复数 U 旋破缺参数 ρ̂。

Result: ρ̂ 参数的模长范围为 0.62 至 4.45，表明存在显著的 U 旋对称性破缺（U-spin symmetry breaking）；同时报告了共振拟合份额（resonant fit fraction），并给出两个衰变道在相空间（phase space）各区域上平均强相位参数的模型预言。

Conclusion: 该分析更新了 U 旋破缺参数的测量结果，证实 U 旋对称性破缺不可忽略，并为相关衰变的强相位参数提供了模型预言，可为后续测量（如 γ/φ₃ 角相关研究）提供输入。

Abstract: We perform a simultaneous amplitude analysis of the decays $D^0 \to K_{\text{S}}^{0}π^{+}π^{-}$ and $D^0 \to K_{\text{L}}^{0}π^{+}π^{-}$ using an $e^+e^-$ collision data sample, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $20.3\,\text{fb}^{-1}$, collected at the centre-of-mass energy of $3.773\,\text{GeV}$ with the BESIII detector. This study, based on a significantly larger dataset than that used in previous work, presents an updated measurement of the complex $U$-spin breaking parameters ($\hatρ$) associated with various $CP$ eigenstate resonant modes mediating the three-body decays. The magnitudes of the $\hatρ$ parameters range from $0.62$ to $4.45$, indicating substantial $U$-spin symmetry breaking. We also report the resonant fit fraction and obtain a model prediction for the average strong-phase parameters over regions of phase space for both decay modes.

</details>


### [105] [Recent results in $b \rightarrow s\,\ell\ell$ transitions at LHCb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13165)
*Juan Jose Juan Castella*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述LHCb合作组在B强子稀有衰变（rare decays of B hadrons）领域的最新研究进展，重点关注$b\rightarrow s\ell\ell$过程中新物理（new physics, NP）效应与标准模型（Standard Model, SM）预言的张力问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B强子稀有衰变在标准模型中受到强烈压低，因而对可能的新物理贡献高度敏感；近年实验测量与标准模型预言出现张力，但其来源尚存争议，故需更高精度测量与理论分析加以厘清。

Method: 基于LHCb合作组的实验数据，采用对$B^0 \rightarrow K^{*0}\, \mu^+\mu^-$、$B^+ \rightarrow K^+\, \mu^+\mu^-$等衰变的振幅分析（amplitude analyses），并开展$B^0 \rightarrow K^+\pi^-\mu^+\mu^-$大双强子质量区分支比（branching fraction）测量及高$q^2$区轻子味普适性比（lepton flavour universality ratio）的首次强子对撞机测量。

Result: 更高精度下，$B^0 \rightarrow K^{*0}\,\mu^+\mu^-$等过程中的张力依然存在；振幅分析为理论图像提供了进一步线索；并给出两项创新测量：$B^0 \rightarrow K^+\pi^-\mu^+\mu^-$在大双强子质量区的分支比，以及高$q^2$区$B^0 \rightarrow K^{*0}\,\ell^+\ell^-$轻子味普适性比的首个强子对撞机测量结果。

Conclusion: 稀有B衰变测量与标准模型预言的张力尚未消除，需结合高精度实验与振幅分析进一步判别其源于真实新物理还是理论理解的不完备；LHCb的最新测量为这一判别提供了关键实验输入。

Abstract: Rare decays of $B$ hadrons, such as $b\rightarrow s\ell \ell$ processes, provide great avenues to search for the influence of new physics (NP). They are highly suppressed in the Standard Model (SM) and therefore sensitive to NP contributions. In recent years, experimental measurements have shown tension with SM predictions. It is still up for debate whether these stem from genuine NP or an incomplete understanding of the theory. These proceedings focus on the recent efforts of the LHCb collaboration to advance this field. Some of the results covered show that tensions in these processes, like in $B^0 \rightarrow K^{*0}\, μ^+ μ^-$, still persist upon higher precision. Others aim to shed some light on the theoretical picture through amplitude analyses of decays like $B^0 \rightarrow K^{*0}\, μ^+ μ^-$ and $B^+ \rightarrow K^+ \, μ^+ μ^-$. We also present two innovative measurements: the branching fraction of $B^0 \rightarrow K^+ π^- μ^+ μ^-$ at large dihadron mass; and the first hadron collider measurement of the lepton flavour universality ratio of $B^0 \rightarrow K^{*0} \, \ell^+\ell^-$ at high $q^2$ (dilepton mass squared).

</details>


### [106] [A machine-learning method for the estimation of the background from jets misidentified as hadronic tau lepton decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13634)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 提出 MUFFIN 方法，用提升决策树重加权直接从观测数据学习逐对象修正因子，以估计喷流被误判为强子衰变 tau 轻子的本底。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夸克和胶子引发的喷流（jet）被误判为强子衰变 tau 轻子（hadronically decaying tau lepton）所构成的本底，是涉及 tau 轻子分析中的主要实验挑战，需直接从观测事件中估计该本底。

Method: 采用基于机器学习（machine learning）的 MUltivariate Fake-Factor INference（MUFFIN）方法，利用提升决策树重加权（boosted decision tree reweighting）从观测数据直接学习多维、逐对象（per-object）修正因子，在运动学与拓扑相空间中对喷流误判为强子 tau 的概率建模，避免依赖低维参数化（low-dimensional parametrisation）。

Result: 基于 CMS 实验在 CERN LHC 采集的质子-质子对撞数据（√s = 13.6 TeV，积分亮度 62.4 fb⁻¹）开展性能研究；相较低维参数化方法，关键运动学观测量（尤其在高 pT 区域）的建模得到改善，并使喷流误判为强子衰变 tau 的系统不确定度降低最多 70%。

Conclusion: MUFFIN 为涉及 tau 轻子末态（tau lepton final states）的精确测量与超出标准模型（beyond-the-standard-model）物理搜寻，提供了一种稳健且可扩展（robust and scalable）的本底估计方法。

Abstract: Backgrounds arising from quark- and gluon-initiated jets misidentified as hadronically decaying tau leptons constitute a major experimental challenge for analyses involving tau leptons. The MUltivariate Fake-Factor INference (MUFFIN) approach, a machine-learning-based method for estimating this background directly from observed events, is presented. Boosted decision tree reweighting is used to learn multidimensional, per-object correction factors directly from observed data, thereby modelling the jet to hadronic tau lepton misidentification probability across the kinematic and topological phase space without relying on low-dimensional parametrisations. Performance studies are presented using proton-proton collision data collected by the CMS experiment at the CERN LHC at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13.6 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 62.4 fb$^{-1}$. Compared to low-dimensional parametrisation methods, improved modelling of key kinematic observables is achieved, particularly in regions of high $p_\mathrm{T}$. The improved modelling leads to a reduction of up to 70% in the systematic uncertainty associated with jets being misidentified as hadronically decaying tau leptons. The MUFFIN method provides a robust and scalable method for background estimation in precision measurements and searches for beyond-the-standard-model physics involving tau lepton final states.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [Why has PET glycolysis remained difficult to industrialize despite decades of optimization?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12475)
*Xiaohui Liu*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对经典 PET 醇解（glycolysis）难以工业放大的问题，提出以“科学推理单元（Scientific Reasoning Units, SRUs）”与人类—AI 协同发现建立统一参考框架，并给出三步协同强化范式（Three-Step Synergistic Reinforcement Paradigm），在 10 分钟内将 BHET 收率提升至 80%–95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为经典 PET 醇解难以规模化的根源是研究范式缺陷：孤立地优化反应（uncoupled reaction optimization），忽视了系统层面的工程熵（engineering entropy）。传统体系存在三重失效模式——传质崩溃（mass-transfer collapse）、黏度死锁（viscosity deadlock）与副反应累积（side-reaction accumulation），需要从系统级视角重新构建优化框架。

Method: 通过建立统一的主导参考框架（governing reference frame），引入科学推理单元（SRUs）与人类—AI 协同发现机制，构建三步协同强化范式。具体集成双螺杆可控剪切（twin-screw controlled shearing）、固态微均质化（solid-state micro-homogenization）与深度解聚（deep depolymerization，EG/低聚物比例 2:1）三项手段。

Result: 该集成策略实现超快动力学区间（ultrafast kinetic regime），BHET 收率在 10 分钟内升至 80%–95%，消除了耗时数小时的热瓶颈（thermal bottlenecks）。

Conclusion: 该工作为闭环聚酯回收（closed-loop polyester recycling）提供了经济可行的框架，并表明以 SRUs 与人类—AI 协作为核心的系统级范式可突破传统醇解在工业放大中的局限。

Abstract: Classical PET glycolysis struggles with industrial scale-up due to a research paradigm defect: uncoupled reaction optimization that ignores system-level engineering entropy. By establishing a governing reference frame, Scientific Reasoning Units (SRUs) and human-AI collaborative discovery in this article, we present a Three-Step Synergistic Reinforcement Paradigm addressing the triple failure modes of traditional systems-mass-transfer collapse, viscosity deadlock, and side-reaction accumulation. By integrating twin-screw controlled shearing, solid-state micro-homogenization, and deep depolymerization (2:1 EG/Oligomer ratio), we achieve an ultrafast kinetic regime. BHET yields surge to 80% to 95% within 10 minutes, eliminating multi-hour thermal bottlenecks and delivering an economically viable framework for closed-loop polyester recycling.

</details>


### [108] [Predictive Synthesis of MOFs and COFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12739)
*Bin Feng,Reid A. Coyle,Shyam Chand Pal,Peter Walther,Saeun Park,Zhiling Zheng*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该展望（Perspective）讨论金属有机框架（MOFs）与共价有机框架（COFs）合成中从化学直觉驱动实验向人工智能（AI）与机器学习（ML）融合的演进，提出预测性合成应由静态配方推荐走向实时控制与可迁移的框架形成原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MOFs与COFs的合成本质上涉及由相互依赖变量构成的多维参数空间，实验化学家难以对其进行参数化与预测。合成决策中还存在先验知识、不确定性以及实验失败等因素，使"下一步该尝试什么"成为核心难题。

Method: 该文为展望性（Perspective）文章，通过讨论化学直觉驱动实验向实验室中AI与ML集成的发展脉络，围绕先验知识、不确定性与实验失败在决定后续尝试中的作用展开论述，未涉及具体实验或算法实现。

Result: 论文提出预测性合成（predictive synthesis）的演进路径：从静态配方推荐（static recipe recommendation）逐步发展到实时控制（real-time control）以及可迁移的框架形成原理（transferable principles of framework formation）。

Conclusion: 其意义在于将试错实践（trial-and-error practice）转变为可测量的化学推理手段，从而加速晶态材料发现并推动合成化学发展。

Abstract: Predictive synthesis concerns the prospective modeling of how a targeted structure can be realized. The synthesis of metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) and covalent organic frameworks (COFs) intrinsically involves a multidimensional space of interdependent variables, posing a challenge for bench chemists to parameterize and predict. In this Perspective, we discuss the evolution from chemical-intuition-driven experimentation toward the integration of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) in the laboratory. By virtue of prior knowledge, uncertainty, and experimental failure in determining what should be attempted next, we envision predictive synthesis progressing from static recipe recommendation toward real-time control and transferable principles of framework formation. In this context, the significance lies in transforming trial-and-error practice into a means of measurable chemical reasoning, thereby accelerating crystalline materials discovery and advancing synthetic chemistry.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Spinodal-controlled freeze-out and nucleation at the first-order superfluid-supersolid transition of a dipolar gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12511)
*Wai Kui Wong*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 通过三维扩展Gross–Pitaevskii方程（3D extended Gross–Pitaevskii equation）加截断Wigner噪声（truncated-Wigner noise），研究\(^{164}\)Dy无限管中超流–超固（superfluid–supersolid）一级相变扫掠动力学：纯量子噪声下体系滞后至旋节点（spinodal）后转变，延迟指数\(\zeta=0.36\pm0.02\)，接近连续相变与平均场值\(1/3\)；热噪声可在亚稳窗口内成核，反向扫掠出现动力学滞后（dynamical hysteresis）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶极量子气体（dipolar quantum gas）在长形势阱中可经相变形成超固，该相变在中等密度连续、在低/高密度为一级；连续点处散射长度（scattering length）扫掠遵循Kibble–Zurek标度，而一级相变处的扫掠动力学、尤其是旋节点延迟与噪声诱导成核尚缺乏统一描述。

Method: 采用三维扩展Gross–Pitaevskii方程并引入截断Wigner噪声，模拟\(^{164}\)Dy在无限管中跨一级超流–超固相变的散射长度扫掠；分别考察仅含量子噪声、以及含标称温度2.5–10 nK初始热噪声的保持（hold）与正向/反向扫掠协议。

Result: 仅量子噪声时，均匀超流穿越亚稳窗口并在旋节点（roton软化处）之后转变；线性扫掠下的延迟拟合指数\(\zeta=0.36\pm0.02\)（含统计与系统敏感性）。反向扫掠在晶体分支折叠（fold）后显示相当延迟，形成动力学滞后。初始热噪声在保持期间于亚稳窗口内产生晶区，越接近旋节点、初始温度越高成核越快；无热浴时有效速率依赖制备：同速率跨窗口的扫掠因起点不同，在32次中2次或16次中11次提前成核。低密度下较慢扫掠选择更大液滴间距，且反向扫掠中液滴可在远高于相变处存活。

Conclusion: 一级超固相变的扫掠动力学由旋节点延迟与噪声诱导成核共同支配，其延迟指数接近连续相变/平均场预测；由于无热浴时成核速率依赖制备历史，从保持速率推断的交叉时间（crossover time）仅为估计值，而非扫掠协议的实测边界。

Abstract: Dipolar quantum gases in elongated traps become supersolid through a transition that is continuous at intermediate density but first order at low and high density; at the continuous point, ramps of the scattering length follow Kibble--Zurek scaling. We study ramps across the first-order superfluid--supersolid transition of $^{164}$Dy in an infinite tube using the three-dimensional extended Gross--Pitaevskii equation with truncated-Wigner noise. With quantum noise only, the uniform superfluid survives the metastable window and transforms after its spinodal, where the roton softens. For onsets occurring during the linear ramp, the delay measured from this spinodal has a fitted exponent $ζ= 0.36 \pm 0.02$, close to the continuous-transition result and the mean-field value $1/3$. The quoted uncertainty combines statistical and systematic sensitivities. Reverse ramps show a comparable delay beyond the fold of the crystal branch, producing dynamical hysteresis. Initial thermal noise with nominal temperatures of 2.5--10 nK produces crystalline regions inside the metastable window during holds, with faster nucleation closer to the spinodal and at higher initial temperature. Without a heat bath these effective rates depend on preparation: ramps crossing the window at the same rate nucleate early in 2 of 32 or 11 of 16 realizations depending on their starting point. A crossover time inferred from hold rates is therefore an estimate, not a measured boundary for the ramp protocol. At low density, slower ramps select larger spacings in a crystal of separated droplets, and droplets survive far above the transition on reverse ramps.

</details>


### [110] [Flat-band quantum geometry from embedded quench dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12555)
*Ilia Komissarov,Daniel E. Sheehy,Ahana Chakraborty*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文提出一种在位淬火（on-site quench）方案，通过将一维平带晶格耦合至二维平带浴，利用局部占据测量提取平带量子几何（quantum geometry），并揭示初始密度图案的长期记忆与量子几何之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管量子几何（quantum geometry）是电子系统中诸多关联现象的根源，但实验上获取单能带几何仍具挑战性。量子模拟器（quantum simulator）的发展（如光晶格中的超冷原子）提供了局域态制备与格点分辨测量能力，从而自然引出能否从局部占据读出中提取能带分辨量子几何的问题。

Method: 提出在位淬火协议：将一维平带晶格（flat-band lattice）与二维平带浴（flat-band bath）耦合，以动力学方式隔离平带扇区。浴选择性移除色散激发（dispersive excitations），而紧致局域态（compact localized states）因与浴的平带模式存在重叠而被困住。

Result: 结果表明，晚期占据图案（late-time occupation pattern）直接编码了以初始密度图案波矢分隔的平带态之间的积分重叠，从而可获取以公度动量 δk = 2πs/r（s, r ∈ ℤ）分隔的态之间的积分量子距离（integrated quantum distance）以及积分平带量子度规（integrated flat-band quantum metric）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了平带系统中量子几何与初始空间图案长期记忆之间的直接联系，为基于局部占据测量的能带分辨量子几何探测提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Quantum geometry underlies a wide range of correlated phenomena in electronic systems, yet accessing the geometry of individual bands remains experimentally challenging. Advances in quantum simulators, such as ultracold atoms in optical lattices, have enabled local state preparation and site-resolved measurements, offering direct access to quantum dynamics beyond conventional transport probes. These developments naturally raise the question of whether band-resolved quantum geometry can be extracted from local occupation readouts. Here, we propose an on-site quench protocol to probe the flat-band quantum geometry via local occupation measurements in an atomic lattice. Our scheme dynamically isolates the flat-band sector by coupling a one-dimensional flat-band lattice to a two-dimensional flat-band bath. The bath selectively removes dispersive excitations, while compact localized states remain trapped due to their overlap with the flat-band modes of the bath. We show that the late-time occupation pattern directly encodes the integrated overlaps between flat-band states separated by the wavevector of the initial density pattern, thereby enabling access to integrated quantum distances between the states separated by the commensurate momenta $δk = 2 πs/r$, $s,r \in \mathbb Z$ and to the integrated flat-band quantum metric. Our results thereby establish a direct connection between quantum geometry and the long-time memory of an initial spatial pattern in flat-band systems.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [An elementary approach to the Szegő theorems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12724)
*Israel Klich,Ravisankar Rajagopal*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种初等图像解释强Szegő定理（strong Szegő theorem）中二次型与权重因子r的起源：强Szegő项来源于边界泄漏，即矩阵指数中离开有限区间并返回的路径，最短此类短途偏离的计数自然产生因子r。该框架给出有限程矩阵渐近行为的严格直观推导，并指出单一孤立边界恰贡献强Szegő修正的一半，而长区间的两个边界退耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强Szegő定理与Fisher-Hartwig公式描述平移不变算子限制在有限大区域上所得行列式的渐近行为，其有限修正项∑_{r>0} rV_rV_{-r}广泛出现于分析与数学物理中，但标准解析推导往往掩盖了该二次型及权重r的来源。作者希望给出一个更直观、初等的机制性解释。

Method: 构造以边界泄漏（boundary leakage）为核心的初等图像：考虑贡献于矩阵指数的路径离开有限区间后再返回，对最短短途偏离（shortest excursions）进行计数以导出因子r。对有限程矩阵给出严格的初等推导，并推广至衰减足够快的相互作用；另以自由费米子（free-fermion）论证提供同一抵消机制的物理解释。

Result: 最短短途偏离的计数直接产生权重r；单一孤立边界恰贡献强Szegő修正的一半，长区间的两个边界相互退耦。数值示例在可控区域内验证了渐近行为及Fisher-Hartwig对数修正，并进一步在超出Szegő公式形式适用范围的慢衰减与无序情形下进行数值考察，部分情形显示该方法具有一定稳健性。

Conclusion: 边界表述本身不要求平移不变性（translation invariance），因此自然指向向无序系统（disordered systems）、高维以及具有内部矩阵结构的符号的推广。数值结果表明，即便对慢衰减且无序的势，该途径在某些情形下仍具稳健性。

Abstract: The strong Szegő theorem and the Fisher-Hartwig formula describe the asymptotic behavior of determinants obtained by restricting translationally invariant operators to large finite regions. In particular, Szegő's theorem states that, beyond the leading extensive contribution to such a determinant, there is a universal finite correction, $\sum_{r>0} rV_rV_{-r}$ where $V_r$ are the Fourier coefficients of the logarithm of the symbol. Although this formula appears throughout analysis and mathematical physics, the origin of its quadratic form and of the weight $r$ is often obscured in its standard analytic derivations. We develop an elementary picture in which the strong Szegő term arises from boundary leakage: paths contributing to the matrix exponential leave the finite interval and subsequently return. Counting the shortest such excursions immediately produces the factor $r$. We then show that an isolated boundary contributes exactly one half of the strong Szegő correction, while the two boundaries of a long interval decouple. For finite-range matrices this gives a rigorous and intuitive elementary derivation of the asymptotics, with extensions to sufficiently rapidly decaying interactions. A complementary free-fermion argument provides a physical interpretation of the same cancellation mechanism. Numerical examples illustrate the asymtotics in the well controlled regime, the Fisher Hartwig logarithmic correction. The boundary formulation does not itself require translation invariance and therefore suggests natural extensions to disordered systems, higher dimensions, and symbols with internal matrix structure. To illustrate this we numerically go beyond the formal applicability region of the Szegő formula, considering slow decay and disordered cases, and showing in some cases a robustness of the approach even for slowly decaying but disordered potentials.

</details>


### [112] [Lieb-Robinson bounds for irreversible dynamics via adaptive Dyson expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12731)
*Carla Rubiliani,Marius Wesle,Tom Wessel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对有限与无限体积量子自旋系统的一类广泛的含时、不可逆动力学，提出一种自适应 Dyson 展开（adaptive Dyson expansion），以自回避集合路径展开局域算符对易子，并由此导出与哈密顿量及耗散项无关的 Lieb-Robinson 界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象为有限与无限体积量子自旋系统中的含时且不可逆动力学，核心难点在于此类演化不含逆向时间演化，难以用传统方法展开对易子并推导 Lieb-Robinson 界。

Method: 发展一种简单灵活的策略，将形如 $[A,\tau_{s,t}B]$ 的对易子按集合的自回避路径（self-avoiding paths of sets）展开，其中 $A,B$ 为局域算符，$\tau_{s,t}$ 为 Heisenberg 时间演化；该自适应 Dyson 展开（adaptive Dyson expansion）不需要逆向时间演化。

Result: 该展开可直接蕴含 Lieb-Robinson 界；所得界不依赖于生成元的任何哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）项和耗散在位项（dissipative on-site terms）。

Conclusion: 自适应 Dyson 展开适用于不可逆动力学，并为含时、不可逆量子自旋系统提供了一个与哈密顿量和耗散在位项无关的 Lieb-Robinson 界框架。

Abstract: In this note, we study a broad class of time-dependent and irreversible dynamics for quantum spin systems in both finite and infinite volume. We develop a simple and flexible strategy for expanding commutators of the form $[A,τ_{s,t} \, B]$ in terms of self-avoiding paths of sets, where $A$ and $B$ are local operators and $τ_{s,t}$ is the Heisenberg time evolution. This adaptive Dyson expansion requires no backwards time evolution and is thus also applicable to irreversible dynamics. Furthermore, we discuss how such an expansion directly implies Lieb-Robinson bounds. A central feature of our result is that the obtained Lieb-Robinson bounds are independent of any Hamiltonian and dissipative on-site terms of the generator.

</details>


### [113] [A Sklar-Type Decomposition for Positive Binary Distributions and a Gibbs Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12805)
*Yi-Yu Lin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过分离一阶边缘分布（one-body marginals）与依赖结构，以局部正重加权（local positive reweighting）定义"依赖核（dependence nucleus）"，并由高阶 Walsh 对数线性系数（Walsh log-linear coefficients）$J_S\ (|S|\ge2)$ 完全刻画，从而唯一确定严格正多元二元分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是对严格正多元二元分布（strictly positive multivariate binary distributions）进行结构化分解，将单变量边缘信息与变量间依赖区分开，以厘清依赖部分的独立表征方式。

Method: 引入局部正重加权以定义依赖核，并以高阶 Walsh 对数线性系数 $J_S$（$|S|\ge2$）对该核进行完全刻画；同时论证任一依赖核与任一严格正一阶边缘分布组合可唯一确定联合分布。

Result: 依赖核与一阶边缘分布构成一对相互独立且完备的表示要素：依赖核由 $|S|\ge2$ 的系数完全决定，二者共同唯一确定联合分布。

Conclusion: 在有限自旋吉布斯系统（finite-spin Gibbs systems）中，这些高阶系数即多体相互作用耦合（many-body interaction couplings），表明该分解为依赖结构与统计物理相互作用提供了统一表述。

Abstract: We study strictly positive multivariate binary distributions by separating one-body marginals from dependence. Local positive reweighting defines a dependence nucleus, completely characterized by the higher-order Walsh log-linear coefficients $J_S$, $|S|\ge2$. Any such nucleus and any strictly positive one-body marginals determine a unique joint distribution. In finite-spin Gibbs systems, these coefficients are the many-body interaction couplings.

</details>


### [114] [Critical Ising model as the $M(4,3)$ minimal model of conformal field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13705)
