*Dmitry Chelkak,Konstantin Izyurov,Kalle Kytölä*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文构造了从临界 Ising 模型格点自旋多项式空间到共形场论（conformal field theory）$M(4,3)$ 极小模型态空间的线性映射，其核恰为零场（null fields）。据此定义标度维数，并证明重新标度场在网格尺寸趋于零时具有非平凡标度极限，可用 $M(4,3)$ 原初场关联与 Virasoro 作用表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立格点临界 Ising 模型与连续共形场论 $M(4,3)$ 之间的精确对应，说明格点上自旋多项式的标度极限如何由 $M(4,3)$ 的原初场与 Virasoro 代数刻画。

Method: 构造从格点自旋多项式空间到 $M(4,3)$ 态空间的线性映射，并定义标度维数 $\Delta_{\Op}$ 为像分解中最低的 $\Loper{0}+\Loperbar{0}$ 本征值；证明中引入包含费米子与无序插入的更大格点场空间到两个费米子 Fock 空间直和的映射，关键技术为格点局域场空间上费米流作用的新版本与离散复分析（discrete complex analysis）的新进展。

Result: 映射的核恰为零场；非零场经 $\delta^{-\Delta_{\Op}}$ 重新标度后在关联函数中具有非平凡标度极限，且所有极限可用 $M(4,3)$ 原初场关联与连续 Virasoro 作用表达。$M(4,3)$ 的最高权向量（本征值 $0,1/16,1/2$）分别为常数 $1$、单格点自旋与单格边能量的像；结果对任意等径向图（isoradial graphs）成立。

Conclusion: 临界 Ising 模型的格点自旋多项式在连续极限下与 $M(4,3)$ 极小模型精确对应，标度维数取值于 $\Z_{\geq0}\cup(\Z_{\geq0}+1/8)$，且该结论在任意等径向图上有效，深化了格点模型与共形场论的联系。

Abstract: We construct a linear map from the space of spin polynomials in the
  critical Ising model on a lattice to the state space of the $M(4,3)$
  minimal model of conformal field theory. The kernel of the map consists
  precisely of null fields, i.e., spin polynomials that vanish in all
  correlations. For a polynomial $\Op$ which is not a null field, we
  define its scaling dimension $Δ_{\Op}\in\Z_{\geq0}\cup(\Z_{\geq0}+\frac{1}{8}),$
  to be the lowest $\Loper{0}+\Loperbar{0}$ eigenvalue in
  the decomposition of its image into homogeneous components. We show
  that as the mesh size $δ$ goes to zero, the rescaled field $δ^{-Δ_{\Op}}\Op$
  has a non-trivial scaling limit, when inserted in correlations with
  other such fields. Moreover, all these limits can be expressed in
  terms of correlations of primary fields of $M(4,3)$ and the Virasoro
  action in the continuum. The highest weight vectors of $M(4,3)$,
  the joint eigenvectors of $\Loper{0}$ and $\Loperbar{0}$ with the same eigenvalues $0,1/16,1/2$ for both, are the images of the
  constant $1$, the spin at a single lattice vertex, and the energy
  at a single lattice edge, respectively.
  For the proof, we construct a map from a larger lattice field space,
  accommodating also fermion and disorder insertions, to a direct sum
  of two fermionic Fock spaces. This direct sum contains contains the
  state spaces of both the $M(4,3)$ and the free fermion CFT. The main technical ingredient of the proof is a new version of the exact fermionic current action on the space of lattice local fields due to C. Hongler, K.K. and F. Viklund, combined with some new advances in discrete complex analysis. Our results are valid on arbitrary isoradial graphs.

</details>


### [115] [Perturbed isotropic oscillators with inversion symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13743)
*Antonella Marchesiello,Rita Mastroianni,Giuseppe Pucacco*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 1:1 失谐共振（1:1 detuned resonance）的正规形（normal form），通过双重平均（double averaging）将其与天体力学中的三体问题（three body problem）联系起来，并分析其分岔（bifurcation）结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星间引力耦合（gravitational coupling）具有对称性，使所得正规形呈现反演对称（inversion symmetry），因此需要借助一种系统方式刻画其在不同失谐与物理参数下的可能分岔行为。

Method: 采用双重平均（double averaging）方法建立正规形；基于失谐（detuning）等物理参数进行解析预测，并引入耦合参数（coupling）与非对称参数（asymmetry）以区分全部分岔序列。

Result: 分岔情景丰富：一种序列中，鞍结分岔（saddle-node bifurcation）后出现的不稳定轨道可经历反向叉形分岔（reversed pitchfork bifurcation），转为稳定并伴随两个不稳定轨道的产生，最终与两个准正规模（quasi-normal modes）之一重新连接；另一情景为标准叉形分岔（pitchfork），使一个准正规模失稳并产生一对稳定周期轨道（periodic orbits）。

Conclusion: 通过耦合与非对称参数的引入，可有效辨别所有可能的分岔序列，表明该正规形框架能够系统描述此类共振系统的分岔全貌。

Abstract: We consider the normal form of a 1:1 detuned resonance that can be related, via a double averaging process, to the three body problem of celestial mechanics. In view of the symmetry in the gravitational coupling between the planets, the obtained normal form exhibits inversion symmetry. We provide an analytical prediction of the possible bifurcations in terms of the detunings and the other physical parameters of the system. We show that the bifurcation scenario is rich. It includes bifurcation sequences where the unstable orbit appearing after a saddle-node bifurcation can have a reversed pitchfork bifurcation, where it turns into stability with the birth of two unstable orbits, which eventually reconnect with one of the two quasi-normal modes. Furthermore, another scenario shows a standard pitchfork in which one of the quasi-normal modes becomes unstable, giving rise to a pair of stable periodic orbits. By introducing coupling and asymmetry parameters, we provide an effective way to discern all the possible sequences of bifurcations.

</details>


### [116] [Unifying spherical convolutions through lifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13892)
*J. D. McEwen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过将球面卷积提升到旋转群，提出了球面卷积（spherical convolution）的统一描述。作者证明在固定输入输出自旋的映射中，方向性核（directional kernel）、球面输出与等变性（equivariance）无法同时满足，并提供谐波空间算法及开源实现 s2conv。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有多种球面卷积定义在核的轴对称性、输出所在空间、自旋（spin）及计算成本上各异，但缺乏统一处理框架。该工作旨在建立一个能涵盖所有已提出的旋转等变卷积（rotation-equivariant convolution）的统一表述。

Method: 将球面视为齐性空间（homogeneous space）$S^2 \cong \text{SO(3)}/\text{SO(2)}$，把自旋 $s$ 场提升到旋转群，在 $\text{SO(3)}$ 上卷积，再通过提升算子的伴随（adjoint）投影回球面（可具有不同的自旋 $s'$）。通过对核、自旋选择以及提升与投影的取舍施加约束，可复原所有已有卷积形式；并给出基于采样定理（sampling theorem）的谐波空间算法。

Result: 证明了对固定输入与输出自旋的场间映射，方向性核、球面输出与等变性三者不能同时实现。明确自旋场是相伴线丛（associated line bundle）的截面，并将自旋旋转相位表述为该截面扭曲（twist）的特征标（character）。所有算子实现于开源代码 s2conv，数值实验确认了各类归约关系及算子的等变性（达到机器精度）。

Conclusion: 该框架统一并连接了物理与线丛文献中的球面卷积表述，并在地球与天球（celestial sky）实测场上进行了示例展示，表明其在实际应用中的适用性。

Abstract: We develop a unified description of convolution on the sphere by lifting to the rotation group. A number of definitions of spherical convolution have been proposed, differing in whether the kernel is axisymmetric or directional, whether the output is on the sphere or the rotation group, whether the signals carry spin, and in their computational cost. No unified treatment exists. Regarding the sphere as the homogeneous space $S^2 \cong \text{SO(3)}/\text{SO(2)}$, we lift a spin $s$ field to the rotation group, convolve on $\text{SO(3)}$, and project back to the sphere through the adjoint of the lift for a potentially different spin $s'$. We recover every rotation-equivariant convolution considered previously through constraints on the kernel, spin choices and inclusion or omission of the lift and projection. For maps between fields of fixed input and output spin, we prove that directional kernels, output on the sphere and equivariance cannot be achieved simultaneously. We make explicit that spin fields are sections of associated line bundles, linking the physics and bundle literatures, and express the spin rotation phase as the character of the twist of the section. We present harmonic-space algorithms for the exact and efficient computation of lifted spherical convolutions of band-limited fields by appealing to sampling theorems on the sphere and rotation group. All operators are implemented in the open-source s2conv code, and numerical experiments confirm the reductions and the equivariance of the operators to machine precision. Finally, we illustrate the operators on fields measured on the Earth and on the celestial sky.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [The quantum geometry of the chemical bond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12746)
*Gabrielle Carrel,Nishchhal Verma,Fatmagül Katmer,Leslie M. Schoop,Raquel Queiroz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将化学键指标与量子几何张量（quantum geometric tensor, QGT）建立联系，揭示化学键如何决定能带在动量空间（momentum space）的织构，从而在化学描述与物理描述之间架起桥梁。其核心结果是：QGT可分解为三中心键指标的面积加权形式，并可据此区分离子键、共价键、超价键与金属价键；非共线三中心键被识别为固体中贝里曲率（Berry curvature）的来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料的电子性质同时取决于能带能量与电子态的量子几何，但化学键（chemical bonding）图像与量子几何描述长期处于相对分离的话语体系中。该研究旨在弥合化学键视角与固体物理视角，建立从键合模式出发预测物理性质的定量通道。

Method: 将化学键指标（bonding indices）与量子几何张量（QGT）相关联，考察键合如何塑造能带的动量空间结构；将QGT分解为三中心键指标（three center bond index）并按其有向面积加权；再以轨道对（orbital pair）为分辨单位解析各贡献，考察其供体—受体（donor–acceptor）特征。

Result: QGT可表示为中心键指标的面积加权分解，其轨道对分辨的贡献能够刻画离子键（ionic）、共价键（covalent）、超价键（hypervalent）与金属价键（metavalent bonding）之间的供体—受体差异。同时识别出材料中拓扑性的键合模式，即非共线三中心键（noncollinear three center bonds），其为固体中贝里曲率（Berry curvature）的来源；键涨落（bond fluctuation）的能量谱可解析并与电磁响应直接关联。

Conclusion: 化学键基元（bonding motifs）可通过量子几何张量定量映射到材料的物理性质，为从键合模式出发设计材料物理性质提供了定量路径，并统一了固体中化学键与量子几何/拓扑的表述框架。

Abstract: Electronic properties of materials depend both on band energies and quantum geometry of electronic states. We relate bonding indices to the quantum geometric tensor (QGT), showing how chemical bonding dictates the momentum-space texture of bands, bridging chemical and physical descriptions of solids. We show the QGT decomposed into the three center bond index, weighted by their oriented area. Resolving these contributions by orbital pair reveals the donor--acceptor character that distinguish ionic, covalent, hypervalent, and metavalent bonding. We identify the bonding pattern for topology in materials: noncollinear three center bonds, which are the source of Berry curvature in solids. Bond fluctuation can be directly linked to electromagnetic response by resolving their energy spectrum, opening a quantitative route towards the design of physical properties of materials from bonding motifs.

</details>


### [118] [Corrugated Niobene: Stability, Mechanical and Electronic Properties from First-Principles Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12749)
*Willian O. Santos,José A. S. Laranjeira,Kleuton A. L. Lima,Douglas S. Galvão,David L. Azevedo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种褶皱状单元素铌纳米片 c-Nb，并通过密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）系统研究其结构、能量、振动、力学、电子与光学性质，揭示其兼具强内聚、弹性柔顺与多带金属性，拓展了二维金属材料的结构多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料多样性不断增长，但褶皱结构（corrugated architectures）如何容纳金属键并影响单元素铌纳米片的稳定性与性质仍属未解方向。研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）模拟，对提出的褶皱铌纳米片 c-Nb 进行结构优化，并计算其结构、能量、振动（声子）、力学、电子及光学性质。

Result: 优化结构为中心对称 P-3m1 对称性，原胞含六个原子，几何厚度 4.279 Å；内聚能 -6.03 eV/atom，强于孤立原子结合，但比体相 BCC 铌高 2.49 eV/atom。无可虚频声子模式且面内刚度矩阵正定，表明动力学与力学稳定；尽管褶皱明显，仍呈现面内各向同性弹性，杨氏模量 43.66 N/m、剪切模量 13.66 N/m、泊松比 0.598。费米能级附近电子态由 Nb 4d 轨道主导，形成多带金属性（multiband metallicity）。

Conclusion: c-Nb 兼具强内聚、弹性柔顺与金属电子行为，扩展了单元素二维金属的结构多样性，并为铌纳米片在电子与紫外响应系统中的进一步探索提供基础。

Abstract: The growing diversity of two-dimensional (2D) materials has expanded opportunities in electronics, energy technologies, catalysis, and optical applications. Within this landscape, understanding how corrugated architectures accommodate metallic bonding and influence the stability and properties of monoelemental niobium sheets remains an open research direction. Here, we propose a corrugated niobene nanosheet, denoted c-Nb, and investigate its structural, energetic, vibrational, mechanical, electronic, and optical properties using density functional theory simulations. The optimized structure adopts a centrosymmetric P-3m1 symmetry, with six atoms per primitive cell and a geometrical thickness of 4.279 Angstrom. Its cohesive energy of -6.03 eV/atom indicates strong binding relative to isolated atoms, although bulk BCC Nb remains energetically favored by 2.49 eV/atom. The absence of imaginary phonon frequencies along the investigated high-symmetry path and the positive-definite in-plane stiffness matrix support dynamical and mechanical structural stability. Despite its pronounced corrugation, the nanosheet exhibits isotropic in-plane elasticity, with Young's and shear moduli of 43.66 N/m and 13.66 N/m, respectively, and a Poisson's ratio of 0.598. Electronic states near the Fermi level are dominated by Nb 4d orbitals, establishing multiband metallicity. These findings identify a corrugated structural candidate that combines strong cohesion, elastic compliance, and metallic electronic behavior, extending the structural diversity of monoelemental 2D metals and providing a basis for further exploration of niobium nanosheets in electronic and ultraviolet-responsive systems.

</details>


### [119] [Dynamic Chemical Changes of Ti3C2Tx MXenes in Halide Perovskite Precursor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12771)
*Kirill Sobolev,Nagaprasad Reddy Samala,Mariela J. Pavan,Sharon Hazan,Vladimir Ezersky,Shakked Schwartz,Omer Bartov,Adi Yakir,Michal Leskes,Tamar Goldzak Mizrachi,Yevgeny Rakita*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究系统考察了Ti3C2Tx MXene在前驱体墨水（precursor ink）中与MAPbI3各组分接触时的化学稳定性与表面演化；结合XPS与DFT揭示I2/多碘化物强氧化、PbI2缓氧化、MAI钝化稳定三种机制，并指出前驱体加入顺序可调控MXene表面状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将二维Ti3C2Tx MXene引入卤化物钙钛矿（Halide Perovskite, HaP）光电子器件虽具提升器件性能的潜力，但前驱体墨水阶段的基本化学相互作用尚未被充分研究，而该阶段决定最终材料状态。

Method: 以模型甲基铵碘化铅（MAPbI3）溶液为对象，将Ti3C2Tx MXene分别及同时暴露于其各组分中，结合X射线光电子能谱（X-ray Photoelectron Spectroscopy, XPS）分析与密度泛函理论（Density Functional Theory, DFT）计算，解耦MXene与墨水组分间的复杂相互作用。

Result: 元素碘（I2）与多碘化物（In-）作为强氧化剂，引发MXene片层快速降解与溶解；碘化铅（PbI2）因Pb2+在氧终止位点的强吸附而导致较慢的氧化过程；甲基铵碘化物（MAI）通过氢键吸附于MXene表面，有效钝化片层并延缓降解。此外，前驱体加入顺序决定MXene表面状态。

Conclusion: 该研究阐明MXene与HaP材料整合时应有的认识视角，指出前驱体顺序既需额外谨慎又可提供额外可调控性，为MXene/钙钛矿体系的理性工程设计提供基础。

Abstract: The integration of two-dimensional Ti3C2Tx MXenes into Halide Perovskite (HaP) optoelectronics has shown immense potential for enhancing device performance, yet the fundamental chemical interactions within the precursor ink (a critical stage determining the final material state) remain largely unexplored. This study presents a comprehensive investigation into the chemical stability and surface evolution of Ti3C2Tx MXenes when exposed to the individual and combined components of a model methylammonium lead iodide (MAPbI3) solution. By combining detailed X-ray Photoelectron Spectroscopy (XPS) analysis with Density Functional Theory (DFT) calculations, we decouple the complex interactions between MXenes and the ink constituents. We demonstrate that elemental iodine (I2) and polyiodides (In-) act as aggressive oxidizers, triggering rapid degradation and dissolution of the MXene flakes. Conversely, lead iodide (PbI2) induces a slower oxidation process driven by the strong adsorption of Pb2+ ions onto oxygen terminated sites. In stark contrast, methylammonium iodide (MAI) exhibits a stabilizing effect: it adsorbs onto the MXene surface via hydrogen bonding, effectively passivating the flakes and retarding degradation. In addition, our results reveal that the sequence of precursor addition governs the MXene surface state, requiring extra caution, but also enabling further tunability. These findings provide a critical view on how MXene integration with materials, in general, and HaP in particular, should be perceived, providing a fundamental for their rational engineering.

</details>


### [120] [Realizing Two-Dimensional Altermagnets with 100% Spin Polarization along the [110] Direction in Ambidentate-Ligand Based Square Organometallic Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12791)
*Qing-Bo Liu,Xin-Yi Chen,Xiangyang Li,Xingxing Li,Jinlong Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出利用双齿配体（ambidentate ligands）构建有机金属交替磁体（altermagnets）的化学策略，并在带边实现[110]方向依赖的100%自旋极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁体（altermagnetism）兼具零净磁矩与非相对论性自旋劈裂效应，但当前缺乏简单且实验可行的设计方法，且在价带或导带特定方向实现100%自旋极化仍具挑战。

Method: 基于紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）提出使用双齿配体构建有机金属交替磁体的化学策略；并通过第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）筛选90种二维双齿配体方形有机金属晶格。

Result: 筛选出三种交替磁材料：Fe(taz)2Cl2（taz = 1,2,4-三嗪）、Co(app)2Cl2与Fe(app)2Cl2（app = 1,4-氮杂膦杂苯）；均具价带[110]方向100%自旋极化及显著压磁行为（piezomagnetic behavior）。

Conclusion: 该研究为在交替磁材料中实现方向依赖的完全自旋极化开辟了途径。

Abstract: Altermagnetism, as a novel magnetic paradigm, not only possesses a zero net magnetic moment, but also exhibits a substantial nonrelativistic spin-splitting effect. Currently, developing a simple and experimentally feasible method for designing altermagnets is eagerly pursued. Moreover, achieving 100% spin polarization along certain directions in valence or conduction bands for altermagnets remains challenging. To address these issues, based on a tight-binding model, we propose a viable chemical strategy for constructing organometallic altermagnets by using ambidentate ligands. Furthermore, in these organometallic altermagnets, direction-dependent 100% spin polarization along the [110] direction in band edges can be induced via the coexistence of altermagnetically spin-split dispersive and flat bands, where the flat band suppresses electron transport in one spin channel and the dispersive band enables conduction in the other spin channel. Then, via first-principles calculations, a screening of 90 two-dimensional ambidentate-ligand based square organometallic lattices leads to the identification of three altermagnetic materials, namely Fe(taz)2Cl2 (taz = 1,2,4- triazine), Co(app)2Cl2, and Fe(app)2Cl2 (app = 1,4-azaphosphinine), all of which possess 100% spin polarization along the [110] direction in valence bands, as well as notable piezomagnetic behavior. This study paves the way for achieving direction-dependent fully spin-polarization in altermagnetic materials.

</details>


### [121] [Drude, Where's My Charge? End-to-End Learning of Polarizable Drude Force Fields for Polymer Electrolytes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12799)
*Artur Lyssenko,KyuJung Jun,Rafael Gomez-Bombarelli*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: AutoDrude 是一个可微框架，可直接从 DFT（density functional theory，密度泛函理论）能量、力与偶极数据参数化极化 Drude 力场，无需经验调参，计算成本仅为固定电荷模拟的 1.20 倍。对 PEO-LiTFSI 体系，其在离子电导率上与最佳电荷缩放模型精度相当，并能同时再现阳离子迁移数随浓度上升等电荷缩放模型无法捕捉的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体聚合物电解质（solid polymer electrolytes）的分子动力学模拟需要兼具高效率与高精度的力场：既要能在数百纳秒尺度上收敛离子输运，又要准确描述主导输运的离子-聚合物相互作用。固定电荷力场（fixed-charge force fields）满足效率要求，却忽略电子极化（electronic polarization），通常仅以经验性电荷缩放因子近似，因而可能缺失关键的极化效应。

Method: 提出 AutoDrude，一个可微（differentiable）框架，直接从 DFT 能量、力和偶极（dipoles）数据参数化可极化 Drude 力场，无需经验调参，计算开销仅为固定电荷模拟的 1.20 倍。框架包含偶极约束（dipole-constrained，硬约束，hard-constraint）与基于惩罚项（penalty-based，软约束，soft-constraint）两种变体，并与固定电荷模型及电荷缩放模型进行拟合对比。

Result: 针对 PEO-LiTFSI，偶极约束 AutoDrude 模型再现实验离子电导率（ionic conductivity）的精度与最佳电荷缩放模型相当（平均绝对百分比误差 17% 对 16%）。不同于所测试的电荷缩放模型，AutoDrude 还能再现稀限阳离子迁移数（dilute-limit cation transference number）、其随盐浓度的上升趋势，以及低浓度下不存在 Li-TFSI 接触离子对（contact ion pairs）的现象。软约束变体对 DFT 参考数据拟合更佳，却将电导率低估达一个数量级。

Conclusion: 结果表明，尽管电荷缩放（charge scaling）可再现离子电导率，但要在 PEO-LiTFSI 中捕捉阳离子输运对浓度的依赖关系，显式引入极化（polarization）是必要的。此外，与 DFT 训练数据更接近的拟合并不必然保证准确的离子输运预测。

Abstract: Molecular dynamics simulations of solid polymer electrolytes require force fields that are fast enough to converge ion transport over hundreds of nanoseconds, yet accurately describe the ion-polymer interactions that govern it. Fixed-charge force fields meet the first requirement but neglect electronic polarization, an effect often approximated by scaling ionic charges using empirically chosen factors. Here we present AutoDrude, a differentiable framework that parametrizes polarizable Drude force fields directly from DFT energies, forces, and dipoles, with no empirical tuning and at only 1.20 times the cost of a fixed-charge simulation. For PEO-LiTFSI, the dipole-constrained (hard-constraint) AutoDrude model reproduces the experimental ionic conductivity as accurately as the best charge-scaled model (17% versus 16% mean absolute percentage error). We compare the polarizable forcefield to fixed charge ones models by fitting a fixed charge model. Unlike the charge-scaled models tested here, however, AutoDrude also reproduces the dilute-limit cation transference number, its increase with salt concentration, and the absence of Li-TFSI contact ion pairs at low concentration. A penalty-based (soft-constraint) variant yields a closer fit to the DFT reference yet underestimates the conductivity by up to an order of magnitude, demonstrating that closer agreement with DFT training data alone does not guarantee accurate ion transport. These results indicate that although charge scaling can reproduce ionic conductivity the explicit inclusion of polarization is necessary to capture the concentration dependence of cation transport in PEO-LiTFSI.

</details>


### [122] [Room-temperature ferroelectric p-wave magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12892)
*Kaiwen Shen,Nicoly Hembeck,Warlley H. Campos,Jianting Dong,Bin Li,Yuhang Li,Gui Wang,Jan Priessnitz,Yifan Gao,Yingfen Wei,Hao Jiang,Xumeng Zhang,Ming Wang,Jia Zhang,Wu Shi,Qi Liu,Libor Šmejkal,Xianzhe Chen,Ming Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种无需Tt对称性的p波磁性（p-wave magnetism）新路径，基于非公度自旋螺旋序，在室温多铁性BiFeO3（BFO）中实现与铁电极化共存的p波磁性，并通过圆偏振光电效应（CPGE）验证其可电控手性翻转的自旋极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: p波磁体在动量空间具有奇宇称自旋极化且净磁化为零，是非相对论自旋电子学（nonrelativistic spintronics）的潜在平台；但此前基于时间反演与晶格平移组合对称性（Tt）的p波磁体有序温度低于液氮温区，限制了应用。因此需要探索不依赖Tt对称性的室温p波磁性实现途径。

Method: 采用对称性分析、密度泛函理论（density-functional-theory, DFT）计算与圆偏振光电效应（circular photogalvanic-effect, CPGE）测量相结合的手段，研究多铁性BiFeO3（BFO）的摆线（cycloidal）磁结构，并分析其p波自旋极化及CPGE响应。

Result: BFO的摆线磁结构本征地实现p波磁性，并与铁电极化在室温下共存；对称性分析与计算表明，摆线序伴随约100 meV的大的非相对论自旋劈裂（spin splitting）；该p波自旋极化产生特征性CPGE响应，其符号可通过电控摆线手性（chirality）实现可逆翻转。

Conclusion: 研究确立铁电BiFeO3为室温、可电控切换的p波磁性平台，并为基于宇称破缺磁序（parity-breaking magnetic order）的电压控制自旋电子器件提供了一条路径。

Abstract: P -wave magnets exhibit odd-parity spin polarization in momentum space while retaining zero net magnetization, offering a promising route toward nonrelativistic spintronics. Previously reported p-wave magnets based on combined time-reversal and lattice-translation symmetry, Tt, have thus far exhibited ordering temperatures below the liquid-nitrogen range. Here, we demonstrate a distinct route to a pristine p-wave magnetism that does not require T t symmetry, and instead is based on incommensurate spin-spiral order. Specifically, the cycloidal magnetic structure of the multiferroic BiFeO3 (BFO) intrinsically realizes p-wave magnetism coexisting with ferroelectric polarization at room temperature. Combining symmetry analysis, density-functional-theory calculations, and circular photogalvanic-effect (CPGE) measurements, we find a large nonrelativistic spin splitting of approximately 100 meV associated with the cycloidal order. The resulting p-wave spin polarization gives rise to a characteristic CPGE response whose sign can be reversibly switched by electrically controlling the cycloid chirality. Our results establish ferroelectric BiFeO3 as a room-temperature platform for electrically switchable p-wave magnetism and provide a route toward voltage-controlled spintronic devices based on parity-breaking magnetic order.

</details>


### [123] [Symmetry-Enforced Topology and Van Hove Saddle Points in Hexagonal TiAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12925)
*Azizur Rahman,Shashi B Mishra,Sheraz Khan,Khushnood Shah,Xun Ma,Xiaoyang Lin,Tomasz Sobol,Lei Zhang,Weisheng Zhao,Min Zhang,Zahir Muhammad*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 首次通过化学气相输运（chemical vapor transport）成功生长化学计量比六方 TiAs 单晶，并系统研究其声子、电子、拓扑与磁输运性质，确立其为研究非对称性带拓扑、Van Hove 奇异性与各向异性输运相互作用的平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六方 TiAs 单晶的制备此前尚未实现，限制了对该过渡金属单磷族化合物（transition-metal monopnictide）拓扑与输运性质的实验研究。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用化学气相输运（chemical vapor transport）生长化学计量比六方 TiAs 单晶（空间群 $P6_3/mmc$）；结合偏振拉曼光谱（polarized Raman spectroscopy）、第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）与磁输运（magnetotransport）及霍尔（Hall）测量。

Result: 偏振拉曼光谱确认全部四个拉曼活性声子模（$A_{1g} + E_{1g} + 2E_{2g}$），与六方对称性一致；计算揭示非平凡带拓扑（对称指示 $\mathbb{Z}_4 = 2$、强拓扑指数 $ν_0 = 1$）及非对称性布里渊区边界处对称性保护的四重简并。费米面沿 $k_z$ 呈现从准二维柱状到褶皱三维结构的轨道依赖交叉；$Γ\text{--}K$ 方向费米能级下约 0.5 eV 处存在 Van Hove 鞍点，关联颈断裂 Lifshitz 相变与态密度增强。磁输运显示显著二重角度磁阻，霍尔测量指示非补偿空穴主导输运。

Conclusion: 六方 TiAs 可作为探索非对称性带拓扑、Van Hove 奇异性与各向异性电子输运相互作用的材料平台。

Abstract: We report the first successful growth of stoichiometric hexagonal TiAs single crystals (space group $P6_3/mmc$) using chemical vapor transport and investigate their vibrational, electronic, topological, and magnetotransport properties. Polarized Raman spectroscopy identifies all four Raman-active phonon modes ($A_{1g} + E_{1g} + 2E_{2g}$), consistent with the hexagonal crystal symmetry. First-principles calculations reveal nontrivial band topology characterized by a symmetry indicator $\mathbb{Z}_4 = 2$ and a strong topological index $ν_0 = 1$, together with symmetry-protected fourfold band degeneracies at the nonsymmorphic Brillouin-zone boundary. The Fermi surface exhibits an orbital-dependent crossover from quasi-two-dimensional cylindrical sheets to a corrugated three-dimensional structure along $k_z$. A Van Hove saddle point approximately 0.5 eV below the Fermi level along $Γ\text{--}K$ is associated with a neck-disrupting Lifshitz transition and enhanced density of states. Magnetotransport measurements reveal a pronounced twofold angular magnetoresistance, consistent with anisotropic Fermi-surface geometry and open cyclotron orbits. Hall measurements indicate uncompensated, hole-dominated transport, accounting for the absence of extreme magnetoresistance and contributing to the field-orientation-dependent Hall response. These results establish hexagonal TiAs as a platform for exploring the interplay of nonsymmorphic band topology, Van Hove singularities, and anisotropic electronic transport in transition-metal monopnictides.

</details>


### [124] [High-Throughput Discovery of Chiral Carbon-Boron Semimetals with Weyl Topology and Phonon-Mediated Superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12965)
*Lizhou Yang,Wenneng Zhao,Ye Yang,Weiwei Chen,YongKeat Chan,Chen-Hao Lv,Qing-Bo Liu,Yujia Xiang,Xinming Qin,Zedong Li. Weiyi Wang,Jinlong Yang,Wei Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 结合基于Wyckoff位置的结构生成与高通量第一性原理计算，在六方手性空间群中筛选碳-硼（C-B）晶体，从972个候选结构中确定6个稳定材料，兼具拓扑半金属态与声子介导超导电性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性晶体（chiral crystals）为非常规拓扑费米子提供天然平台，而轻元素碳-硼骨架因强共价键与高声子频率有望实现声子介导超导（phonon-mediated superconductivity）；本文旨在将拓扑半金属与超导的材料范围拓展至化学设计的轻型手性结构。

Method: 采用基于Wyckoff位置（Wyckoff-position-based）的结构生成方法，结合高通量第一性原理计算（high-throughput first-principles calculations），在选定六方手性空间群中搜索C-B晶体；从972个生成的C_mB_n结构（m,n=1–4）出发，依次通过能量稳定性、动力学稳定性、电子金属性、拓扑能带特征与热稳定性的多重筛选。

Result: 筛选得到6个稳定候选材料：(C3B2)8、(CB2)3、(CB4)6、(C3B2)6、(C2B3)6与(CB2)6；这些材料在费米能级附近表现出多种拓扑半金属态，包括电荷数为1、2、3的Weyl点（Weyl points）、节线环（nodal rings）与节面态（nodal-surface states）。其中(C3B2)8具螺旋对称性保护的电荷数3的Weyl点和强电子-声子耦合（electron–phonon coupling），在库仑赝势参数μ*=0.10时给出McMillan转变温度31.1 K；(CB2)3具多个电荷数1的Weyl点，并由全各向异性Eliashberg计算揭示显著的各向异性多带超导（anisotropic multiband superconductivity）。

Conclusion: 该研究表明拓扑半金属与声子介导超导电性的材料范围可拓展至经化学设计的轻型手性结构，为相关材料探索提供了新的设计思路与候选体系。

Abstract: Chiral crystals provide a natural platform for unconventional topological fermions, while light-element carbon--boron frameworks are promising for phonon-mediated superconductivity owing to strong covalent bonding and high phonon frequencies. Here, we combine Wyckoff-position-based structure generation with high-throughput first-principles calculations to search for C--B crystals in selected hexagonal chiral space groups. Starting from 972 generated $\mathrm{C}_m\mathrm{B}_n$ structures ($m,n=1\text{--}4$), successive filters based on energetic stability, dynamical stability, electronic metallicity, topological band features, and thermal stability identify six stable candidates: $(\mathrm{C}_3\mathrm{B}_2)_8$, $(\mathrm{C}\mathrm{B}_2)_3$, $(\mathrm{C}\mathrm{B}_4)_6$, $(\mathrm{C}_3\mathrm{B}_2)_6$, $(\mathrm{C}_2\mathrm{B}_3)_6$, and $(\mathrm{C}\mathrm{B}_2)_6$. These materials host diverse topological semimetal states near the Fermi level, including charge-one, charge-two, and charge-three Weyl points, nodal rings, and nodal-surface states. Notably, $(\mathrm{C}_3\mathrm{B}_2)_8$ exhibits screw-symmetry-protected charge-three Weyl points and strong electron--phonon coupling, yielding a McMillan transition temperature of $31.1\,\mathrm{K}$ for the Coulomb pseudopotential parameter $μ^* = 0.10$. In addition, $(\mathrm{C}\mathrm{B}_2)_3$ hosts multiple charge-one Weyl points and pronounced anisotropic multiband superconductivity revealed by fully anisotropic Eliashberg calculations. These results extend the materials scope of topological semimetals with phonon-mediated superconductivity to chemically designed chiral light-element structures.

</details>


### [125] [Evolution and breakdown of chiral anomaly in Weyl semiconductor tellurium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13084)
*Zhifeng Xue,Jiaji Zhao,Yi Ji,Fuyang Chen,Nannan Tang,Gangjian Jin,Bing Shen,Bin Wang,Yang Wang,Mingxi Chi,Jin-Zhu Zhao,Ruiqiang Wang,Qizhong Zhu,He Wang,Huakun Zuo,Donghui Guo,Dingyong Zhong,Mingxun Deng,Huichao Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作以掺杂碲（doped tellurium）为平台，实现了可由费米能级与磁场调控的巨负纵向磁阻（negative longitudinal magnetoresistance），并揭示其手性异常（chiral anomaly）起源及极端量子极限下的磁阻反转行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性异常在拓扑材料（topological materials）中通常表现为负纵向磁阻，但其可调性与演化规律尚不明确。研究旨在寻找可调控该效应的材料平台并厘清其随费米能级和磁场的变化。

Method: 以掺杂碲为研究对象，通过调节费米能级（Fermi level）使其趋近外尔点（Weyl point），并在不同磁场强度（含超越量子极限（quantum limit）与极端量子极限）和温度条件下测量纵向磁阻/磁导行为。

Result: 费米能级趋近外尔点时负磁阻显著增强，该效应源于手性异常并可稳定持续至室温；超越量子极限后体系呈现正的线性磁导，被视为最低朗道能级（lowest Landau level）中手性异常的输运特征；在低温极端量子极限下磁阻由负急剧反转为正。

Conclusion: 磁阻反转暗示存在由增强的电子-电子相互作用（electron-electron interactions）驱动的场诱导相变，进入集体电子态（collective electronic state）。该结果有助于理解极端磁场下的非常规手性输运与涌现关联量子态。

Abstract: Chiral anomaly in topological materials manifests as negative longitudinal magnetoresistance, yet the tunability and evolution of this effect remain elusive. Here we show that doped tellurium serves as a versatile platform to realize a giant negative longitudinal magnetoresistance that is highly tunable via the Fermi level and magnetic field. By moving the Fermi level towards the Weyl point, we observe a pronounced enhancement of negative magnetoresistance, which arises from chiral anomaly and robustly persists up to ambient temperature. Beyond the quantum limit, the system exhibits positive linear magnetoconductance, serving as a compelling transport signature of the chiral anomaly in the lowest Landau level. Notably, in the extreme quantum limit at cryogenic temperatures, the magnetoresistance undergoes a dramatic reversal from negative to positive. This reversal points to a potential field-induced phase transition into a collective electronic state driven by enhanced electron-electron interactions. Our findings provide valuable insights into the unconventional chiral transport and emergent correlated quantum states under extreme magnetic fields.

</details>


### [126] [Role of composition-tuned disorder on magnetic exchange interactions in NiRuMnGa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13107)
*Manikantha Panda,Prabuddha Kant Mishra,Vinod Kumar,Wilfrid Prellier,Rahul Sharma,Tapas Paramanik*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究报告了四元 Heusler 合金（Heusler alloy）NiRuMnGa 的实验表征与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），揭示其存在显著 B2 型无序（B2-type disorder），并证明铁磁与反铁磁相互作用竞争导致磁阻挫（magnetic frustration），稳定了团簇玻璃态（cluster glass state）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探究四元 Heusler 合金 NiRuMnGa 的磁性基态性质，特别是其是否存在长程磁有序或呈现玻璃态行为。通过结构与磁性的联合表征，明确其因交换相互作用竞争而产生的磁阻挫现象。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算确定能量有利的晶体结构，并将该结构作为粉末 X 射线衍射（powder X-ray diffraction）数据精修的模型；同时进行直流磁化、交流磁化率（AC susceptibility）、磁弛豫（magnetic relaxation）、记忆效应及热容（heat-capacity）测量。

Result: 精修结果与实验图谱吻合良好，并揭示 Ni/Ru 与 Mn/Ga 位点互占所致的显著 B2 型无序。直流磁性在约 48 K 附近呈现明显 ZFC–FC 不可逆性，不可逆温度随外场增大向低温移动；等温磁化在 7 T 下仍不饱和，仅约 0.4 μB/f.u.，并伴有明显磁滞。交流磁化率、磁弛豫与记忆测量证实存在慢自旋动力学，热容在不可逆温度附近无明显异常。

Conclusion: NiRuMnGa 中不存在常规长程磁有序，其铁磁与反铁磁相互作用之间的竞争导致磁阻挫，并稳定了团簇玻璃态。该结果凸显结构无序与交换相互作用竞争在四元 Heusler 合金磁性基态中的关键作用。

Abstract: We report the experimental investigation of the quaternary Heusler alloy NiRuMnGa, complemented by first-principles calculations. The energetically favorable crystal structure was identified from first-principles calculations and subsequently used as the structural model for refinement of the powder X-ray diffraction data. The refinement shows good agreement with the experimental pattern and reveals substantial B2-type disorder involving Ni/Ru and Mn/Ga site intermixing. The dc magnetic response exhibits pronounced ZFC--FC irreversibility near $\sim 48$ K, with the irreversibility temperature shifting toward lower temperatures under increasing magnetic field, indicating a field-sensitive glassy state. The isothermal magnetization remains unsaturated up to 7 T, reaching only $\sim 0.4\,μ_{\mathrm{B}}$/f.u., accompanied by pronounced hysteresis, suggesting competing exchange interactions and frustrated magnetic moments. AC susceptibility, together with magnetic relaxation and memory measurements, confirms slow spin dynamics characteristic of a cluster glass state. The lack of a discernible heat-capacity anomaly near the irreversibility temperature further supports the absence of conventional long-range magnetic order. Collectively, these results demonstrate that competition between ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic interactions in NiRuMnGa leads to magnetic frustration and stabilizes a cluster glass state.

</details>


### [127] [Assessing quantitative phase reliability in electron ptychography using a twisted trilayer WS2 thickness staircase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13115)
*Hu Bao,Qilong Feng,Kun Ye,Tianyu Xue,Stephen J. Pennycook,Yihan Zhu,Anmin Nie,Shoucong Ning,Jing Tao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究以扭曲三层 WS2 厚度阶梯（含单层、双层、三层区域）为内参，在相同实验条件下测量电子叠层成像（electron ptychography）重建相位，揭示 W 原子柱相位随层数的非线性变化以及投影重叠的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子波相位对局域静电环境敏感，可反映原子尺度结构变化，但在电子叠层成像中，样品厚度、多重散射（multiple scattering）与投影原子重叠会使重建相位的定量解读复杂化，因而需要可控的参考体系进行校准。

Method: 采用含单层、双层与三层区域的扭曲三层 WS2 厚度阶梯作为内部参考，所有区域在完全相同的实验条件下进行测量，并考察 W–S 重叠与短程投影 W–W 重叠对 W 原子柱相位的影响。

Result: W 原子柱平均相位从单层到双层增加约 13.2%，而从双层到三层仅增加约 4.7%，呈现非线性厚度依赖；W–S 重叠对 W 原子柱相位影响很小，而短程投影 W–W 重叠使其增加约 8–9%。

Conclusion: 该结果为多层 WS2 中定量相位测量的评估提供基准，并有助于评估基于相位的局域静电势、电荷密度与三维原子尺度极化（polarization）成像。

Abstract: The phase of an electron wave is sensitive to the local electrostatic environment and can therefore provide information about atomic-scale structural variations. In electron ptychography, however, specimen thickness, multiple scattering and projected atomic overlap can complicate the quantitative interpretation of the reconstructed phase. Here, we use a twisted trilayer WS2 thickness staircase containing monolayer, bilayer and trilayer regions as an internal reference, with all regions measured under identical experimental conditions. The mean W-column phase increases by approximately 13.2% from the monolayer to the bilayer region, compared with only 4.7% from the bilayer to the trilayer region, revealing a nonlinear thickness dependence. W-S overlap has little effect on the W-column phase, whereas short-range projected W-W overlap increases it by approximately 8-9%. These results provide a benchmark for evaluating quantitative phase measurement in multilayer WS2 and for assessing phase-based mapping of local electrostatic potential, charge-density and three-dimensional atomic-scale polarization.

</details>


### [128] [Insights on the volume variation of LiCoxMn2-xO4 electrode upon cycling: a correlative operando XRD and XAS study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13189)
*Eunike Mahayoni,Louiza Larbi,Romain Batiot,Anatole Weill,Damien Brégiroux,Antonella Iadecola,Arnaud Perez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合原位 X 射线衍射（XRD）与原位 X 射线吸收谱（XAS），在 LiCoxMn2-xO4 尖晶石体系中建立了阳离子特异性氧化还原与体积演化之间的直接关联，证明 Co 取代可抑制电化学诱导的结构应变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压尖晶石正极的体积变化是导致机械降解与循环稳定性下降的关键因素，但将体积变化与特定阳离子的氧化还原过程相对应仍具挑战性。研究旨在通过关联局域结构与长程结构的演化，厘清这一联系。

Method: 合成五种组分（x = 0、0.25、0.5、0.75、1.0）的 LiCoxMn2-xO4 并完成电化学表征，其中 x = 0、0.5、1.0 在相同循环条件下进行详细研究；联合使用原位 XRD 与原位 XAS（Mn、Co K 边），并引入化学计量学（chemometric）工具分析 XAS 数据。

Result: XAS 显示 Mn4+/Mn3+ 与 Co4+/Co3+ 两套独立的氧化还原过程：Mn 的氧化还原活性在 4.7 V 以下占主导，Co 的氧化还原在更高电位被触发。XRD 与 XAS 虽探测不同的结构尺度，却给出一致的体积演化趋势；随 Co 含量增加，总体体积变化逐步减小，XAS 测得由 5.0 % 降至 1.6 %，XRD 测得由 5.8 % 降至 2.1 %。

Conclusion: 结果表明 Co 取代可抑制电化学诱导的结构应变，并在阳离子特异性氧化还原活性与体积演化之间建立了直接联系。该关联式原位 XRD/XAS 方法为研究新设计的低应变、高能量、循环稳定性更优的正极材料提供了分析框架。

Abstract: Understanding volume changes in high-voltage spinel cathodes is essential for mitigating mechanical degradation and improving cycling stability. However, linking these changes to cation-specific redox processes remains challenging. Here, we combine operando X-ray diffraction (XRD) and X-ray absorption spectroscopy (XAS) to investigate the LiCoxMn2-xO4 spinel system, which exhibits a cubic structure and isotropic lattice response, in order to correlate the evolution of the local and long-range structure. Five compositions (x = 0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, and 1.0) were synthesized and electrochemically characterized, with x = 0, 0.5, and 1.0 examined in detail under identical cycling conditions. XAS at the Mn and Co K-edges reveals distinct Mn4+/Mn3+ and Co4+/Co3+ redox processes, with Mn redox activity dominating below 4.7 V and Co redox triggered at higher potentials. Chemometric tools for XAS data analysis further resolve the evolution of local structure during cycling. Both XRD and XAS capture consistent trends in volume evolution, despite probing different structural length scales. Increasing Co content progressively decreases the overall volume change, from 5.0 % to 1.6 % by XAS and from 5.8 % to 2.1 % by XRD. These results demonstrate that Co substitution suppresses electrochemically induced structural strain and establish a direct link between cation-specific redox activity and volumetric evolution. The correlative operando XRD/XAS approach provides a framework to investigate newly designed low-strain, high-energy cathodes with improved cycling stability.

</details>


### [129] [Quantitative Eshelby stability: local and finite-shape theory through AI-assisted mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13400)
*Bo Ni*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究以源于Eshelby猜想（Eshelby conjecture）的定量稳定性问题为切入点，建立整体内部应变不均匀性与偏离椭球族（ellipsoid family）的纯几何距离之间的非线性双边关系，并提出互补的有限形状理论，给出可收敛的上下界。结果与独立的位移型有限元（finite element）计算一致，同时记录了人类主导、AI辅助的研究流程，并由此衍生出“Open Problems in Mechanics”这一版本化社区问题地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于探讨研究者如何在保持物理意义、数学严谨性与验证能力的前提下，利用人工智能（artificial intelligence, AI）推进困难力学问题。作者以Eshelby猜想相关的定量稳定性问题作为具体探索场景，并希望借此厘清人机协作的研究模式。

Method: 针对均匀各向同性固体中的均匀膨胀本征应变（uniform dilatational eigenstrain），建立整体内部应变不均匀性与“仅依赖几何”的椭球族距离之间的非线性双边关系，采用半阶Sobolev标度（half-order Sobolev scaling）以刻画形态长度尺度。进一步提出互补的有限形状理论：通过椭球扣除（ellipsoidal subtraction）得到精确的残余场收支，对给定几何获得系统收敛的上下界，并使用独立位移型有限元计算进行校验。

Result: 所得上下界在凸出（protruded）、凹陷（indented）与颈缩（necked）夹杂情形下与独立的位移型有限元计算结果一致。研究还记录了一个人类主导、AI辅助的流程：研究者负责选择重要问题、设定形状目标、审查论证、要求独立校验并解释力学含义，而AI拓展了解析探索并支持计算。

Conclusion: 研究给出了应变不均匀性与椭球族几何距离之间的可验证定量关系及可收敛的界估计框架，并表明在人类保留判断与控制权的前提下AI可有效辅助力学研究。该经验促成了“Open Problems in Mechanics”的建立——一个版本化、面向社区的力学问题地图，明确列出选定问题、已知/开放边界及进展判定标准。

Abstract: How can human researchers use artificial intelligence (AI) to make progress on difficult mechanics problems while retaining control of physical significance, mathematical rigor, and validation? We explore this opportunity through a quantitative stability problem rooted in the Eshelby conjecture. For a uniform dilatational eigenstrain in a homogeneous isotropic solid, we establish a nonlinear two-sided relation between whole-interior strain heterogeneity and a geometry-only distance from the ellipsoid family, with half-order Sobolev scaling that captures morphological length scale. We then develop a complementary finite-shape theory: ellipsoidal subtraction yields an exact residual-field budget from which systematically convergent upper and lower bounds are obtained for a prescribed geometry. Applied to protruded, indented, and necked inclusions, the bounds agree with independent displacement-based finite-element calculations. The study also documents a human-led, AI-assisted research process in which researchers select consequential problems, shape targets, audit arguments, require independent checks, and interpret mechanics, while AI broadens analytical exploration and supports computation. This experience also led us to develop Open Problems in Mechanics, a versioned community-facing map that makes selected mechanics questions, their known/open boundaries, and criteria for progress explicit.

</details>


### [130] [VN-GraphGG: von Neumann--Mullins--based graph grain growth simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13418)
*Yuntong Huang,Zhihao Li,Shuyang Dai,Chang Ma,Yang Xiang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出基于图神经网络（graph neural network）与 von Neumann–Mullins（VN）定律耦合的二维晶粒生长模拟方法 VN-GraphGG，实现动力学系数可调、无需重训练的快速晶粒生长模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多晶材料（polycrystalline materials）中工艺—结构—性能关系的研究依赖高效的晶粒生长模拟，而现有方法（如相场（phase-field）模拟）计算成本高。作者希望将晶粒尺度动力学（grain-scale kinetics）与微结构重构（microstructure reconstruction）解耦，以提升效率与灵活性。

Method: 将多晶微结构表示为包含晶粒节点与节点（junction nodes）的图（graph）；晶粒面积演化在图上的晶粒节点上由 von Neumann–Mullins（VN）定律控制；同时训练图神经网络（graph neural network）从图重构微结构。该分离设计使动力学系数（kinetic coefficients）可变而无需重新训练重构模型；拓扑变化（如晶粒消失、邻接关系改变）通过重构与后处理（postprocessing）直接处理。

Result: 模拟结果对 VN 动力学与粗化统计（coarsening statistics）给出准确预测，并相较相场（phase-field）模拟实现显著加速。文中还给出异常晶粒生长（abnormal grain growth）的应用示例，其中动力学异质性（kinetic heterogeneity）显著影响晶粒微结构演化。

Conclusion: 该方法为基于晶粒尺度动力学的高效晶粒生长模拟奠定基础，并为引入更广泛的物理机制（physical mechanisms）提供可能。

Abstract: Efficient simulations of grain growth are essential for investigating the process-structure-property relationships in polycrystalline materials. We introduce a two-dimensional von Neumann-Mullins-based graph grain growth simulation (VN-GraphGG), which couples the grain-scale kinetics with microstructure reconstruction. The polycrystalline microstructure is represented as a graph with grain and junction nodes. The grain-area evolution on the grain graph is governed by the von Neumann--Mullins (VN) law, while a graph neural network is trained to reconstruct the microstructure from the graph. This separation allows kinetic coefficients to be varied without retraining the neural reconstruction model. Topological changes during grain growth such as disappearance of grains and changes in neighbor relations are handled straightforwardly through reconstruction and postprocessing. Simulations demonstrate accurate predictions of VN kinetics and coarsening statistics, and significant speedup over the phase-field simulations. The proposed method provides a foundation for efficient grain growth simulation based on grain-scale kinetics, opening the way to incorporate a broader range of physical mechanisms. An application example is presented for abnormal grain growth, in which kinetic heterogeneity significantly influences the grain microstructure evolution.

</details>


### [131] [Predicting Nanofabrication Variance: A Conditional Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13420)
*Wenhan Zhang,Ben Mills,Oleksandr Buchnev,Kevin F. MacDonald*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出条件扩散模型（conditional diffusion model），学习以输入设计为条件的加工结果统计分布，从而预测聚焦离子束（FIB）铣削后结构的真实分布，而非单一确定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚焦离子束铣削制备的微纳结构因烧蚀速率波动（ablation-rate fluctuations）、再沉积（redeposition）等系统性与随机性效应而偏离设计，名义相同工艺仍产生不同结果，进而导致光学、力学等物理性质波动。现有模型多只预测单一确定性结果，忽略此变异性。

Method: 采用条件扩散模型，以输入设计为条件，学习加工结果的统计分布，用以预测可能输出样本结构的现实分布。

Result: 预测结果准确复现了制备样品中观察到的边缘形貌（edge morphology）、线宽变化（linewidth variations）与缺陷概率（defect probabilities），并支持铣削性能的统计分析及主导结构与工艺参数相关变形模式的识别。

Conclusion: 该工作为加工变异性分析（fabrication variability analysis）与面向加工的设计优化（fabrication-aware design optimization）建立了概率建模框架。

Abstract: Micro- and nanostructures fabricated by focused ion beam (FIB) milling invariably deviate from input designs due to systematic and stochastic effects such as ablation-rate fluctuations, and redeposition. As such, nominally identical iterations of a given process yield a distribution of non-identical fabrication outcomes, leading to variations in the physical (optical, mechanical) properties of fabricated structures. Existing models of the milling process typically predict a single deterministic fabrication result, neglecting this variability. Here we present a conditional diffusion model that learns the statistical distribution of process outcomes conditioned on input design, enabling it to predict a realistic distribution of possible output sample structures. These predictions accurately reproduce edge morphology, linewidth variations, and defect probabilities observed in fabricated samples, enabling statistical analysis of milling performance and the identification of dominant structural and process parameter-dependent deformation modes. The work establishes a probabilistic modelling framework for fabrication variability analysis and fabrication-aware design optimization.

</details>


### [132] [Magnetic-field-induced detwinning of orientational domains in altermagnet $α$-MnTe: A single-crystal neutron diffraction study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13444)
*Feihao Pan,Yishui Zhou,Sabreen Hammouda,Yinghao Zhu,Soohyeon Shin,Karin Schmalzl,Wolfgang Schmidt,Eric Ressouche,Oscar Fabelo,Yixi Su*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用单晶中子衍射（single-crystal neutron diffraction）证明，沿 $[-1,1,0]$ 方向施加面内磁场可在六角 $α$-MnTe 中使取向畴（orientational domain）近乎完全一致排列，饱和场约为 $μ_0H=2$ T，并给出磁矩方向与大小的定量精修结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Altermagnet（交替磁体）兼具补偿磁序与动量依赖的自旋劈裂（momentum-dependent spin splitting），但宏观响应易被磁畴平均削弱。六角 $α$-MnTe 存在三个对称相关的面内 Néel 矢量取向及其时间反演（time-reversal）对应态，共六个磁畴态，妨碍磁结构测定与手性磁振子（chiral magnon）响应测量，因此需要有效的磁畴控制手段。

Method: 采用单晶中子衍射，在面内沿 $[-1,1,0]$ 方向施加磁场；在 2 K、2 T 条件下收集 61 个核反射与磁反射并进行约束精修（constrained refinement），以确定磁矩方向、有序磁矩大小及取向畴布居。

Result: 沿 $[-1,1,0]$ 方向的面内磁场产生共同磁畴取向，并在 $μ_0H≈2$ T 附近饱和。约束精修给出 $\mathbf{M}\parallel[1,1,0]$，每个 Mn 的有序磁矩为 $4.62(8)~μ_\mathrm{B}$，取向畴布居 $r_A:r_B:r_C=0.97:0.03(5):0$，与取向畴近乎完全一致排列相符。另在 60–88 K 观察到所选反射温度依赖的异常行为，其微观起源在可能的内禀压磁（piezomagnetic）或磁弹性（magnetoelastic）效应框架下讨论。

Conclusion: 由于非极化衍射（unpolarized diffraction）既无法区分畴重新布居与 Néel 矢量的连续转动，也无法分辨时间反演反相畴，该场制备态应被视为近单一取向态而非真正的单畴态。该非接触式磁畴控制方法可作为应变与场冷（field-cooling）手段的实用补充，用于在块体 $α$-MnTe 中制备定义良好的磁态。

Abstract: Altermagnets combine compensated magnetic order with momentum-dependent spin splitting, which makes them promising for future spintronic and magnonic applications, but their macroscopic responses can be strongly reduced by domain averaging. In hexagonal $α$-MnTe, three symmetry-related in-plane Néel-vector orientations and their time-reversal counterparts form six magnetic domain states, complicating both magnetic structure determination and measurements of the chiral magnon response. Using single-crystal neutron diffraction, we show that an in-plane field applied along the $[-1,1,0]$ direction produces a common magnetic domain orientation that saturates near $μ_0H=2$ T. A constrained refinement of 61 nuclear and magnetic reflections collected at 2 K and 2 T yields $\mathbf{M}\parallel[1,1,0]$, an ordered moment of $4.62(8)~μ_\mathrm{B}$ per Mn, and an orientational domain population at $r_A:r_B:r_C=0.97:0.03(5):0$ that is compatible with a nearly complete alignment of orientational domains. Furthermore, an abnormal behaviour in the temperature dependence of the selected reflections is observed in 60-88 K, its microscopic origin is discussed within the context of possible intrinsic piezomagnetic or magnetoelastic effects. Because unpolarized diffraction neither distinguishes domain repopulation from continuous Néel-vector rotation nor resolves time-reversal antiphase domains, the field-prepared state is a near-single-orientation state rather than a truly single-domain state. This non-contact domain control provides a practical complement to strain and field-cooling approaches for preparing well-defined magnetic states in bulk $α$-MnTe.

</details>


### [133] [The effects of non-stoichiometric lattice structure and secondary phase α-Fe2O3 on modifying the magnetic, electrical, and magneto-electric properties of Eu doped BaFe12O19 hexaferrite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13499)
*Gara Kishor,R. N. Bhowmik,P. D. Babu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过以 Eu 替代 BaFe12O19 中的 Ba 位并调控 Eu/Ba 原子比（0–0.33），系统调节了 M 型钡六铁氧体（M-type Ba hexaferrite）中赤铁矿（hematite）次生相比例，并实现了磁性、光学及磁电子学（magneto-electronic）性质的宽幅调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索以 Eu/Ba 原子比作为调控手段，在化学计量与非化学计量晶格结构中调节 M 型钡六铁氧体的次生相（赤铁矿）与磁、光及磁电子学性质，以满足自旋电子器件（spintronic devices）的应用需求。

Method: 采用 Eu 原子替代 Ba 位制备 Ba1-xEuyFe12O19，Eu 占据 Fe 位点，Eu/Ba 原子比在 0–0.33 范围内变化；结合拉曼光谱（Raman spectra）、X 射线光电子能谱（X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy）表征物相与缺陷，并测量光学带隙、磁化强度、矫顽力、电导率、电流-电压特性及磁电阻与磁电耦合行为。

Result: Eu/Ba 比升高使赤铁矿相系统性增加；氧空位与表面缺陷存在，Ba、Fe/Eu 价态分别接近 +2 与 +3；材料呈直接光学跃迁，带隙为 2.25–3.09 eV。饱和磁化强度随 Eu/Ba 比升高而下降（3 K：84.44→57.68 emu g-1；300 K：67.09→43.42 emu g-1），而矫顽力显著增强（3 K：914→3656 Oe；300 K：1996→4862 Oe）。电导率由 30 K 的 10-11–10-10 Sm-1 升至 300 K 的 10-5–10-4 Sm-1；低温区符合 Efros-Shklovsky 型变程跳跃（variable-range hopping）机制，近室温符合 Mott 变程跳跃机制；电极-样品界面势垒高度为 0.27–0.74 eV。

Conclusion: 材料表现出新颖的磁电子学特性，包括双稳态电子开关（bi-stable electronic switching）及其温度尺度转变、磁电阻（magnetoresistance）与磁电耦合（magnetoelectric coupling），适用于自旋电子器件。研究表明，在化学计量与非化学计量晶格中控制 Eu/Ba 原子比可作为宽幅调控 M 型钡六铁氧体磁、光与磁电子学性质的有效策略。

Abstract: The Ba-atoms in BaFe12O19 were substituted by Eu-atoms to prepare the material of composition Ba1-xEuyFe12O19. The Eu atoms occupied at the sites of Fe atoms. The atomic ratio of Eu/Ba were varied from 0 to 0.33 to control the secondary phase (hematite) and M-type hexaferrite. Raman spectra provided complimentary support for a systematic increase of the hematite phase on increasing the Eu/Ba ratio. X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy informed the presence of oxygen deficiency and surface defects, and charge state of Ba and Fe/Eu ions close to +2 and +3, respectively. The material showed direct optical transitions with band gap in the range of 2.25-3.09 eV. The increase of Eu/Ba ratio systematically increased the saturation magnetization from 84.44 emu g-1 to 57.68 emu g-1 at 3 K and 67.09 emu g-1 to 43.42 emu g-1 at 300 K, along with remarkable enhancement of the coercivity (914- 3656 Oe at 3 K and 1996-4862 Oe at 300 K). Electrical conductivity increased from 10-11-10-10 Sm-1 at 30 K to 10-5 -10-4 Sm-1 at 300 K. The electrical conductivity analysis reveals the Efros-Shklovsky-type variable-range hopping mechanism at the low-temperature region, whereas the Mott variable range hopping mechanism is applicable near to room temperature. The analysis of current-voltage characteristics provided electrode to sample interfacial barrier height of 0.27-0.74 eV. The material exhibited novel magneto-electronic properties (bi-stable electronic switching and its transformation in the temperature scale, magnetoresistance and magnetoelectric coupling) which are highly useful for spintronic devices. This work demonstrates that controlled Eu/Ba atomic ratio in the stoichiometric and non-stoichiometric lattice structure could be an effective strategy to widely tune the magnetic, optical and magneto-electronic properties in M-type Ba hexaferrite material.

</details>


### [134] [Latin squares as a model of perfect mixing of high-entropy alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13519)
*P. S. Grinchuk*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出用拉丁方（Latin square）的数学语言描述高熵合金（high-entropy alloys）结构，构建离散模型以定量刻画理想混合，并在二维情形下借助平移对称性描述宏观样品性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵合金由5种及以上等摩尔组分构成，其结构描述与理想混合的定量刻画缺乏合适的数学框架，尤其是保证任一元素最近邻不出现同类元素这一条件难以形式化。

Method: 采用拉丁方作为基础数学工具构建离散模型；分析其平移对称性（translational symmetry）轴以实现单一方块铺展平面；结合群论（group theory）讨论超结构（superstructures）的识别与构造；并给出组分浓度可接受涨落的条件。

Result: 模型可定量评估特定原子构型对最大混合熵（mixing entropy）理想混合高熵合金的适用性；获得维持合金热力学稳定性（thermodynamic stability）的浓度涨落条件；估算五组分合金形成理想混合的概率不超过2.6×10^{-10}。

Conclusion: 拉丁方模型为高熵合金的理想混合结构提供了离散化、可定量的描述途径，并可借助群论识别超结构，同时表明理想混合在多元合金中实现的概率极低。

Abstract: The structure of high-entropy alloys, which include 5 or more components in equimolar proportions, is proposed to be described in the mathematical language of Latin squares. This model is fundamentally discrete and allows for a quantitative description of ideal mixing, in which any given alloy element is guaranteed not to have an identical element as its nearest neighbor. For the two-dimensional case, the model possesses four axes of translational symmetry, which makes it possible to tile the plane with a single Latin square and, based on the properties of this square, describe the properties of macroscopic material samples. The model makes it possible to make quantitative estimates of the suitability of a particular atomic structure (configuration) for implementation in a perfectly mixed high-entropy alloy with maximum mixing entropy. A condition has been obtained for acceptable fluctuations in the concentration of components at which the thermodynamic stability of the alloy is maintained. The possibility of identifying superstructures in high-entropy alloys based on group theory, as applied to the description and construction of Latin squares, is discussed. Based on the proposed model, the probability of forming a multicomponent alloy with ideal mixing is estimated. For a five-component alloy, this probability is no more than 2.6*10^{-10}.

</details>


### [135] [Ferroelectric Domain-Wall Motion, Stability, and Frequency Responses under Different Screening-Charge Conservation Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13619)
*Sergei V. Kalinin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作以极化（polarization）与屏蔽电荷（screening charge）耦合为核心，构建铁电畴壁（ferroelectric domain wall）运动、形状与内部结构的统一分析框架，按电学边界条件、电荷守恒与动力学、极化自由能进行分类，并给出一系列解析解、有限深度计算与宏观尺度模拟结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 畴壁行为通常依赖具体材料与器件构型，缺乏跨体系的通用判据；本文旨在梳理屏蔽电荷耦合下畴壁动力学与结构转变的普适情形，说明电学边界条件与电荷守恒如何决定畴壁能否稳态平移及其结构形态。

Method: 按边界条件分类考察裸露表面、介电层与顶电极下的界面、以及阻塞型混合离子-电子导体（mixed ionic-electronic conductor）界面；屏蔽机制区分局域交换、伴随横向输运的交换与严格守恒的重新分布。手段包括解析推导、有限深度计算、大场模拟，以及约化变量相图与小信号响应函数分析，并以BaTiO3和BiFeO3为例连接材料热力学。

Result: 建立了屏蔽拖曳（screening drag）、速度分支（velocity branches）与空间不稳定性条件；对于连接不同补偿畴的孤立壁，严格表面守恒与零远场电流排除屏蔽剖面的稳态平移，但允许瞬态位移、有限样品停滞（finite-sample arrest）与电荷平衡的结构变化，并约束相邻导体内运动结周围的长程输运。顶电极几何的大场模拟显示非线性褶皱化（corrugation）、向长波长转移及极化转动耦合。一阶极化能量学允许两个相间前沿（interphase fronts）包裹顺电核心（paraelectric core），多轴极化允许多种壁转动与竞争性中间铁电畴；驱动下的结构-屏蔽约化支持稳定周期行为。

Conclusion: 该框架以约化变量相图统一描述不同驱动与屏蔽条件下的畴壁运动与结构区制，并通过BaTiO3、BiFeO3实例与材料热力学对接；小信号分析可区分位移、极化与静电响应函数，为畴壁动力学与结构转变提供可推广的分析工具。

Abstract: We analyze ferroelectric domain wall motion, shape, and internal structure arising from coupling between polarization and screening charge. Universal cases are organized by electrical boundary conditions, charge conservation and dynamics, and polarization free energy. We consider exposed surfaces, interfaces beneath a dielectric layer and top electrode, and interfaces with a blocking mixed ionic-electronic conductor. Screening occurs through local exchange, exchange with lateral transport, or strictly conserved redistribution. Analytical derivations and finite-depth calculations establish screening drag, velocity branches, and conditions for spatial instability. For an isolated wall joining differently compensated domains, strict surface conservation and zero far-field current exclude steady translation of the screening profile, while allowing transient displacement, finite-sample arrest, and charge-balanced structural changes. Conservation also constrains long-distance transport around a moving junction in an adjoining conductor. Large-field simulations in the top-electrode geometry reveal nonlinear corrugation, transfer toward longer wavelengths, and coupled polarization rotation. First-order polarization energetics allow two interphase fronts enclosing a paraelectric core; multiaxial polarization permits wall rotation and competing intermediate ferroelectric domains. A driven structural-screening reduction supports stable periodic behavior. Reduced-variable diagrams summarize motion and structural regimes, while BaTiO3 and BiFeO3 examples connect them to material thermodynamics. Small-signal analysis distinguishes displacement, polarization, and electrostatic response functions. These results provide a framework for wall dynamics and structural transitions under different driving and screening conditions.

</details>


### [136] [Dislocation mediated twin boundary faceting in $β$-Sn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13714)
*John Israel,Ian Chesser,Khanh Dang,Vinamra Agrawal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该摘要报告了利用机器学习势（MLIP）驱动的分子动力学（MD）模拟，在β-Sn中揭示由〈c〉位错吸收沿(301)孪晶界（TB）介导的刻面化反应，并指出刻面长度需引入非对称孪晶刻面的向错（disclination）特征才能与各向同性弹性理论一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: β-Sn作为Sn基焊料的核心材料，其塑性与强化机制具有重要应用价值；近期实验观察到由孪晶-位错交互引起的形变诱导刻面化（faceting），但其原子尺度机制尚不明确，因此需求助高精度模拟加以阐明。

Method: 采用由新近发展的机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potential, MLIP）驱动的分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟，考察β-Sn中(301)孪晶界与〈c〉位错的交互。

Result: 模拟显示沿(301)孪晶界发生由〈c〉位错吸收介导的刻面化反应，刻面长度随被吸收位错数量增加而增长；预测的刻面长度仅在计入非对称孪晶刻面的向错（disclination）特征后才与各向同性弹性理论（isotropic elasticity theory）相符。

Conclusion: 研究指出该刻面化反应可与其他低对称性金属中的类似反应类比，并推测(301)孪晶在β-Sn中普遍存在的原因在于其能够穿越位错障碍林（forest of dislocation obstacles）快速生长。

Abstract: Grain boundaries (GBs), including twin boundaries (TBs), play a critical role in plastic deformation by interacting with dislocations. The deformation and strengthening mechanisms in $β$-Sn are of significant interest because Sn-based alloys are widely used as solder materials. Recent experiments have shown deformation-induced faceting in $β$-Sn arising from twin-dislocation interactions. Using molecular dynamics (MD) simulations informed by a recently developed machine-learned interatomic potential (MLIP), we demonstrate a $\langle c\rangle$ dislocation-absorption mediated faceting reaction along $(301)$ TBs in $β$-Sn in which facet length grows with the number of absorbed dislocations. We show that the predicted facet length is consistent with isotropic elasticity theory only when the disclination character of the asymmetric twin facet is taken into account. We discuss analogies to faceting reactions in other low symmetry metals and hypothesize that the ubiquity of $(301)$ twins in $β$-Sn stems from their ability to grow rapidly through a forest of dislocation obstacles.

</details>


### [137] [Improved screening of doped ZrO$_2$ catalysts for CO$_2$ hydrogenation from graph-based machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13795)
*Karsten Walz Jensen,Mie Andersen,Raffaele Cheula*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作改进基于图的机器学习方法 WWL-GPR，引入电子结构与局域环境特征，用于预测掺杂 ZrO$_2$(101) 表面的吸附能与过渡态（transition state, TS）能量，并在微动力学（microkinetics）层面验证其优于传统线性标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掺杂剂（dopants）可通过改变吸附物与过渡态能量学来调控氧化锆基 CO$_2$ 加氢催化剂活性，但当需筛选多种掺杂剂、反应中间体与过渡态时，密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算成本高昂，因此需要高效的数据驱动代理模型。

Method: 进一步发展 Wasserstein Weisfeiler-Lehman 高斯过程回归（WWL-GPR），为其引入与氧化物表面相关的额外电子结构和局域环境特征；将吸附物-表面结构表示为融合键连接性与特征的属性图（attributed graphs）。以 Al$^{3+}$、Cd$^{2+}$、Ga$^{3+}$、In$^{3+}$、Zn$^{2+}$ 掺杂的 ZrO$_2$(101) 表面为对象，与热力学标度关系（thermodynamic scaling relations, TSR）及 Brønsted-Evans-Polanyi 关系（BEP）进行基准对比，并将预测能量传播至微动力学 CH$_3$OH 产率估计。

Result: 相对于线性标度方法，WWL-GPR 持续改善反应中间体能量学预测，其过渡态估计与基于 BEP 的模型相当；当预测能量被传播通过反应网络时，这些改进尤为显著。微动力学计算表明，WWL-GPR 导出的能量给出的 CH$_3$OH 产率比线性关系工作流更接近 DFT 导出的产率。

Conclusion: 速率控制程度（degree-of-rate-control）分析将活性误差与掺杂剂依赖的速率控制步骤相联系，包括 HCOO* 生成、H$_2$COOH* 解离与 H$_3$CO* 生成。结果表明 WWL-GPR 可作为数据高效的代理模型（surrogate），将原子尺度能量学与掺杂氧化物表面的催化剂筛选可观测量相连接。

Abstract: Dopants can tune the activity of zirconia-based CO$_2$ hydrogenation catalysts through changes in adsorbate and transition-state (TS) energetics. Computing these quantities with density functional theory (DFT) becomes expensive when several dopants, reaction intermediates, and TSs must be screened. Here, we further develop the graph-based machine learning method Wasserstein Weisfeiler-Lehman Gaussian Process Regression (WWL-GPR): we introduce additional electronic-structure and local-environment features relevant for oxide surfaces and use the resulting model to predict adsorption and TS energies on ZrO$_2$(101) surfaces doped with Al$^{3+}$, Cd$^{2+}$, Ga$^{3+}$, In$^{3+}$, and Zn$^{2+}$. Adsorbate-surface structures are represented as attributed graphs that combine bonds connectivity and features. We benchmark WWL-GPR against thermodynamic scaling relations (TSR) for reaction intermediates and Bronsted-Evans-Polanyi relations (BEP) for TSs, and then propagate the predicted energies to microkinetic CH$_3$OH-yield estimates. WWL-GPR consistently improves the prediction of reaction intermediate energetics relative to linear scaling approaches and provides TS estimates that are competitive with BEP-based models. These improvements become especially relevant when the predicted energetics are propagated through the reaction network. Microkinetic calculations show that WWL-GPR-derived energies give CH$_3$OH yields closer to DFT-derived yields than the linear-relation workflow. Degree-of-rate-control analysis links the activity errors to dopant-dependent rate-controlling steps, including HCOO* formation, H$_2$COOH* dissociation, and H$_3$CO* formation. These results support WWL-GPR as a data-efficient surrogate for connecting atomistic energetics with catalyst-screening observables on doped oxide surfaces.

</details>


### [138] [Phononic Frequency Combs in Twisted Bilayer Transition Metal Dichalcogenide Heterostructures from First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13825)
*Naman Arora,Adarsh Ganesan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）从头获取声子频率、线宽与非线性耦合的完整参数集，预测了扭角 MoSSe 与 MoSMoSe 双层中的声子频率梳（phononic frequency comb）及其激发阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往研究中，控制能量转移的非谐模间耦合强度（anharmonic intermode coupling）多被当作自由参数、从器件中反演或沿用旧有计算，导致特定晶体能否支持声子频率梳以及所需驱动强度无法先验预测。本文旨在消除这一自由参数。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论获取完整参数集：二阶力常数给出模频，三阶力常数经玻尔兹曼输运方程（Boltzmann transport equation）给出线宽与品质因子，同一三阶张量投影到选定模对的声子本征矢上得到非谐耦合 kt 与 kss。研究对象为公度角 21.787° 处的 MoSSe 与 MoSMoSe 扭转双层，属空间群 P3，含 42 原子 √7 莫尔超胞。

Result: 两双层均存在接近 2:1 共振（ω1/ω2 = 1.9923（MoSSe）、1.9943（MoSMoSe），偏差 0.8% 以内）的模对。积分双模方程显示两模经非连续跃迁（subcritical）生成频率梳：MoSSe 中 701 fth 时包络方差由 2.1×10³ 升至 1.37×10⁴，模 1 齿数在 5 fth 增量内由 7 增至 16；谱熵峰值出现在该跃迁之前，为 341 fth。两化合物耦合强度相近（相差 1.2 倍），但阈值由信号模品质因子决定：Q₂ = 672（MoSSe）与 3844（MoSMoSe）。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，声子频率梳的可预测性可由第一性原理参数集实现，且其激发阈值并非由耦合强度主导，而由信号模品质因子所设定。

Abstract: Phononic frequency combs are equidistant spectral ladders generated when a driven vibrational mode transfers energy to an autoparametrically excited subharmonic mode through anharmonic intermode coupling. In previous studies, the coupling strength governing this energy transfer has typically been treated as a free parameter, extracted from a fabricated device, or adopted from an earlier calculation for a specific mode pair. As a result, whether a given crystal can support a phononic frequency comb, and the drive strength required to generate it, has not been predictable a priori. Here we obtain the complete parameter set from density functional theory. Twisted bilayers of \MoSSe{} and \MoSMoSe{} at the commensurate angle $21.787^{\circ}$ adopt space group $P3$ in a 42-atom \rootseven{} moiré supercell. The second-order force constants provide the mode frequencies; the third-order constants provide the linewidths through the Boltzmann transport equation and hence the quality factors; and the same third-order tensor projected onto the phonon eigenvectors of a chosen mode pair provides the anharmonic couplings $\kt$ and $\kss$. Both bilayers constitute a mode pair within $0.8\%$ of the $2\!:\!1$ resonance with $ω_1/ω_2 = 1.9923$ in \MoSSe{} and $1.9943$ in \MoSMoSe{}. By integrating the resulting two-mode equations, both modes generate combs through a discontinuous transition: at $701\,\fth$ in \MoSSe{} the envelope variance rises from $2.1\times10^{3}$ to $1.37\times10^{4}$ and the tooth count of mode 1 from 7 to 16 within a single $5\,\fth$ increment, identifying the onset as subcritical. The spectral entropy peaks \emph{before} this jump, at $341\,\fth$. The two compounds carry comparable coupling, differing by a factor of $1.2$. However, the comb excitation threshold is set by the quality-factors of signal modes: $Q_2 = 672$ for \MoSSe{} and $Q_2 = 3844$ for \MoSMoSe{}.

</details>


### [139] [Doubly Metastable Synthesis for Accelerated Quantum Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13859)
*Kyuho Lee,Berit H. Goodge,Soonhyun Kwon,Yash Anand,Theo Manev,Maximilien F. Debbas,Weiliang Luo,Takehito Suzuki,Heather J. Kulik,Joseph G. Checkelsky*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出“扩散激活外延（diffusion-activated epitaxy）”技术，将扩散与分子束外延两种亚稳合成方法结合，在窄合成窗口内稳定四种不同物相，其中三种无法与现有结构数据库匹配，表明双层亚稳合成（doubly metastable synthesis）有望拓展量子材料探索空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新物理源于新材料，而新材料依赖新合成技术的开发。作者将亚稳合成窗口（metastable synthesis window）的扩大视为实现新颖材料的关键，因此尝试通过方法组合来拓展可及的合成空间。

Method: 提出扩散激活外延（diffusion-activated epitaxy）技术，融合扩散（diffusion）与外延（epitaxy）两种亚稳合成方法，并在较窄的合成空间范围内开展实验合成与表征。

Result: 在所考察的窄合成窗口内实验上识别出四种不同物相的稳定化；其中三种物相的X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction, XRD）与扫描透射电子显微镜（scanning transmission electron microscopy, STEM）表征数据无法与实验及计算无机结构数据库中登记的结构相匹配。

Conclusion: 结果表明该合成路径可扩大亚稳合成窗口，凸显双层亚稳合成（doubly metastable synthesis）作为量子材料（quantum materials）探索手段的潜力。

Abstract: New physics emerges from new materials, which in turn rely on the development of new synthesis techniques. Identifying the enlargement of metastable synthesis window as enabling for realizing novel materials, we develop diffusion-activated epitaxy, a technique which combines two metastable synthesis methods of diffusion and epitaxy. We observe that within a narrow window of investigated synthesis space we can experimentally identify the stabilization of four distinct phases, three of which show X-ray diffraction and scanning transmission electron microscopy characterization data that are incompatible with the crystal structures registered in experimental and computational inorganic structure databases. These results suggest an enlarged metastable synthesis window is enabled by this synthesis approach, identifying doubly metastable synthesis as a promising method for exploration of quantum materials.

</details>


### [140] [Necessary conditions for benchmarking next-generation AI in materials discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13885)
*Daniel E Widdowson,Tatiana V Kurlina,Olga D Anosova,Vitaliy A Kurlin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文基于晶体结构的新数学定义与不变量描述子的渐近保证，构建晶体不变量层级（hierarchy of crystal invariants）以持续追踪并量化结构新颖性，揭示全球最大材料数据库中出乎意料的重复规模（以GNoME数据集为例），并提出新一代AI在宣称发现新材料前应自动强制执行的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI通过加速能量最小化与大规模数据库训练来预测材料，但数据生成的规模化使领域专家无法人工分析。若以化学组成、CIF（Crystallographic Information Files）中的传统表示或基于人工设定阈值的标准结构匹配器来评估新颖性，很容易生成数百万个"新"组成或文件，却未发现真正新颖的材料。

Method: 采用晶体不变量的层级体系（hierarchy of crystal invariants），其基础是晶体结构的新数学定义以及不变量描述子的渐近保证（asymptotic guarantees）；通过级联计算（cascade computations）在配置普通的计算机上进行基于不变量的分析。

Result: 基于不变量的分析在普通计算机上通过级联计算，暴露出全球最大材料数据库中出乎意料的重复（duplication）规模，文中以备受关注的GNoME数据集为典型案例予以展示。

Conclusion: 为防止当前"过度预测的窘境（embarrassment of over-prediction）"，论文提出新一代AI在宣称任何新材料之前应被自动强制执行的必要条件。

Abstract: Current AI predicts materials by speeding up energy minimisation and training on large databases. However, scaling data generation prevents manual analysis by domain experts. If novelty is evaluated by chemical compositions or traditional representations in Crystallographic Information Files, or standard structure matchers based on manually chosen thresholds, one can easily generate millions of ``new'' compositions or files without discovering truly novel materials.
  This paper uses a hierarchy of crystal invariants to continuously trace and quantify structural novelty based on the new mathematical definition of a crystal structure and asymptotic guarantees for invariant descriptors. The invariant-based analysis through cascade computations on a modest computer exposes the unexpected scale of duplication in the world's largest materials databases, shown here in the high-profile case of GNoME datasets. To prevent current ``embarrassment of over-prediction'', we propose necessary conditions for next-generation AI that should be automatically enforced before claiming any new materials.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Probing $τ$ lepton dipole moments at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12623)
*Dario Buttazzo,Gabriele Levati,Yang Ma,Fabio Maltoni,Paride Paradisi,ZeQiang Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该研究基于标准模型有效场论（Standard Model Effective Field Theory, SMEFT），评估未来高亮度正负电子对撞机（FCC-ee）对τ轻子电磁与弱偶极矩（dipole moments）的敏感度，并给出95%置信水平下的统计预期界限，较现有限值改善数个量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有τ轻子偶极矩限制来自LEP与LHC，精度有限；未来高亮度正负电子对撞机具备极高亮度与干净环境，有望显著提升探测能力，因此需量化其潜在统计灵敏度。

Method: 在SMEFT框架下分析FCC-ee各运行阶段的多个互补过程，包括e⁺e⁻→τ⁺τ⁻（借共振Z产生与Z极点能量扫描）、γγ→τ⁺τ⁻、辐射过程e⁺e⁻→τ⁺τ⁻γ与H→τ⁺τ⁻γ，并联合各道给出统计预期。

Result: e⁺e⁻→τ⁺τ⁻对弱偶极算符敏感度优异；γγ→τ⁺τ⁻因近阈区线性干涉主导，对反常磁矩（anomalous magnetic moment）给出最强约束；更高能阶段改善对τ电偶极矩（electric dipole moment）的敏感度。联合各道后95% CL统计预期为|Δa_τ|<2.8×10⁻⁵、|d_τ|<2.5×10⁻¹⁸ e cm、|Δa_τ^Z|<1.0×10⁻⁵、|d_τ^Z|<4.4×10⁻¹⁹ e cm。

Conclusion: 上述预期较当前界限改善数个量级，量化了FCC-ee项目的潜在统计覆盖范围，并推动纳入探测器效应、系统不确定度与高阶理论预测的专门研究。

Abstract: We study the sensitivity of future high-luminosity $e^+e^-$ colliders to the electromagnetic and weak dipole moments of the $τ$ lepton within the Standard Model Effective Field Theory. We analyse several complementary processes across the FCC-ee operating stages and show that the exceptional luminosity and clean environment of future $e^+e^-$ colliders allow for substantial improvements over current limits from LEP and the LHC. The $e^+e^- \to τ^+τ^-$ process provides excellent sensitivity to weak dipole operators through resonant $Z$ production and $Z$-pole energy scans, while $γγ\to τ^+τ^-$ yields the strongest constraint on the anomalous magnetic moment owing to the dominance of linear interference effects in the near-threshold region. Higher-energy stages improve the sensitivity to the $τ$ electric dipole moment. The radiative processes $e^+e^- \to τ^+τ^-γ$ and $H\toτ^+τ^-γ$ provide complementary, though subleading, probes of $τ$ dipole operators. Combining all channels, we estimate the statistical reach at the 95% CL to be $|Δa_τ|<2.8\times10^{-5}$, $|d_τ|<2.5\times10^{-18}\,e\,{\rm cm}$, $|Δa_τ^Z|<1.0\times10^{-5}$, and $|d_τ^Z|<4.4\times10^{-19}\,e\,{\rm cm}$, improving upon the current bounds by several orders of magnitude. These projections quantify the potential statistical reach of the FCC-ee program, motivating dedicated studies incorporating detector effects, systematic uncertainties, and higher-order theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [142] [Testing coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering N dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12628)
*Laura Duque,O. G. Miranda,G. Sanchez Garcia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出利用散裂中子源（Spallation Neutron Source, SNS）的相干弹性中微子-原子核散射（coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering, CEvNS）实验已成为稳健的精密测量，仅需引入一个额外参数（中子数 N）即可准确描述现有测量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CEvNS 探测虽性质难以捕捉（elusive），但正从不同中微子测量中发展为精密测量。作者希望说明 SNS 上的 CEvNS 实验已进入精密测量阶段，并检验其在核物理与基本粒子物理交织情形下的可描述性。

Method: 通过仅引入一个额外参数——中子数 N——对现有 CEvNS 测量进行描述与检验，并借此重构核靶中的中子数。

Result: 仅用一个额外参数 N 即可准确描述当前的 CEvNS 测量；这表明现有 CEvNS 精度足以重构核靶中的中子数。

Conclusion: SNS 上的 CEvNS 实验是进入精密时代的稳健测量；靶材料中核子数的确定可作为此类探测器精度与准确度的基准（benchmark）。

Abstract: Despite its elusive character, coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering (CEvNS) detection is evolving as a precision measurement from different neutrino measurements. In this note we show how CEvNS experiments using the Spallation Neutron Source (SNS) are robust measurements that enter into a precision era. Despite the interplay of nuclear and elementary particle physics, we demonstrate that CEvNS current measurements are accurately described by using only one extra parameter, the number of neutrons, $N$. By making this test, we illustrate that current CEvNS are precise enough to reconstruct the number of neutrons in the nuclear target. At the same time, determining the number of nucleons in the target material can be used as a benchmark for the precision and accuracy of this kind of detectors.

</details>


### [143] [Direct detection constraints on EFT operators for inelastic dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12889)
*X. G. Wang,A. W. Thomas*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 将已有的弹性自旋无关（spin-independent, SI）暗物质-质子散射截面排除限转化为事件率（event rate）上限，并据此约束 GeV 质量区轻暗物质非弹性散射（inelastic scattering）的其他有效算符，覆盖等标量、亲质子及亲质子自旋相关三类模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有排除限通常针对弹性自旋无关散射，难以直接用于其他有效算符描述的非弹性散射情形，因此需要一种可迁移的约束手段以扩展到 GeV 质量区的轻暗物质模型。

Method: 把已有弹性 SI 暗物质-质子截面排除限转换为事件率上限，再将此上限应用于约束非弹性散射的其他有效算符，考察三个代表性情景：等标量（isoscalar）、亲质子（proton-philic）以及亲质子自旋相关（proton-philic spin-dependent）非弹性暗物质模型。

Result: 在 GeV 质量区，上述三类非弹性暗物质模型均可由转换所得的事件率上限给出约束（依据摘要，未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: 该转换方法可将弹性 SI 排除限复用为非弹性散射的通用约束工具，适用于现象学研究中动机充分的多种轻暗物质探测情景。

Abstract: We convert existing exclusion limits on the elastic spin-independent (SI) dark matter--proton cross sections into upper bounds on the event rate, subsequently applying them to constrain other effective operators for inelastic scattering of light dark matter in the GeV mass region. We focus on three representative scenarios including the isoscalar, proton-philic, and the proton-philic spin-dependent inelastic dark matter model, which are well-motivated in phenomenological studies of dark matter detection.

</details>


### [144] [nCTEQ26 nuclear parton distribution functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13633)
*nCTEQ Collaboration,K. Greve,T. Ježo,A. Kusina,J. Wissmann,M. Klasen,K. Kovařík,C. Léger,J. G. Morfín,F. I. Olness,P. Risse,I. Schienbein,J. Y. Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: nCTEQ 合作组发布新一代核子部分子分布函数（nuclear parton distribution functions, nPDFs）nCTEQ26，基于迄今最大规模数据选择，采用新参数化与部分数据驱动方法，并通过 Hessian 误差分析给出不确定度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在整合此前更具选择性的各项实验测量研究，在迄今最广泛的数据基础上构建一套现代化的 nPDFs，以覆盖显著扩展的运动学范围。

Method: 全局分析（global analysis）纳入中性流与带电流深度非弹性散射（deep-inelastic scattering, DIS）、固定靶 Drell-Yan 以及 RHIC 与 LHC 第一、二期运行的大量对撞机数据；理论上采用结合 x 依赖的灵活函数形式与二次对数核 A 依赖的新 nPDF 参数化，并引入靶质量（target-mass）与氘核修正；延续此前工作，部分采用数据驱动（data-driven）方法纳入开重夸克（open heavy-quark）与夸克偶素（quarkonium）数据，并与仅基于开重夸克数据微扰预言的替代拟合进行一致性比较。

Result: 通过 Hessian 误差分析确定了 nCTEQ26 nPDFs 的不确定度；将结果与其他近期类似分析进行比较以检验可靠性；并对未纳入分析的当前及未来实验测量给出预言。

Conclusion: nCTEQ26 提供了基于最广数据选择、覆盖扩展运动学范围的现代 nPDFs 集合，其数据驱动方法在纳入开重夸克与夸克偶素数据方面通过替代拟合验证了自洽性，并具备可检验的不确定度与可外推的实验预言。

Abstract: We present the new release of nuclear parton distribution functions (nPDFs) from the nCTEQ collaboration: nCTEQ26. In this global analysis, we combine our recent, more selective studies of different experimental measurements and extend them to determine a modern set of nPDFs based on the largest selection of data considered to date. This selection includes neutral- and charged-current deep-inelastic scattering (DIS), fixed-target Drell-Yan, and a large variety of collider data from RHIC and LHC Runs I and II. On the theoretical side, we match this considerably extended kinematic range with a new nPDF parametrization that combines a flexible functional form of the x-dependence with a quadratic logarithmic nuclear A-dependence as well as target-mass and deuteron corrections for the reliable inclusion of high-x DIS data. Following our previous successful work, we partially employ a data-driven approach that allows us to not only include open heavy-quark data, but also quarkonium data. In addition, we establish the consistency of this approach by comparing it to an alternative fit based on perturbative predictions of open heavy-quark data alone. We determine the uncertainties of our nCTEQ26 nPDFs with a Hessian error analysis, test their reliability by comparing our nPDFs to other, similar recent analyses, and make predictions for current and future experimental measurements that have not been included in the analysis.

</details>


### [145] [Precision Tokenization for Multi-Modal Data in High Energy Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13876)
*Jeffrey Krupa,Samuel Klein,Nilotpal Kakati,Daniel Murnane,Baran Hashemi,Eilam Gross,Lukas Heinrich,Michael Kagan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文针对高能物理（high energy physics, HEP）基础模型（foundation models）中分词器（tokenizer）研究不足的问题，学习 HEP 数据的离散压缩分词表示，并提出四条跨模态的设计准则与开源库 heptokens。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在 HEP 等领域受到广泛关注，但将探测器（detector）数据转换为离散输入的分词器相对缺乏探索。论文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将分词（tokenization）建模为率失真问题（rate-distortion problem），在覆盖两种复杂度不同的模拟探测器、共四种模态的数据上进行系统性超参数扫描（hyperparameter scan）。

Result: 得到适用于所有基于集合（set-based）模态的四条分词设计准则；在约 4–11× 压缩因子（compression factor）下仍保留任务所需的大部分信息，且 jet $p_T$ 分辨率保持在 1% 以下，评估任务包括 jet $p_T$ 回归（regression）、粒子流重建（particle flow reconstruction）与味鉴别（flavor tagging）。

Conclusion: 分词在较高压缩率下仍能保留 HEP 下游任务所需信息，并发布开源分词库 heptokens。

Abstract: Foundation models have recently gained significant attention in fields such as high energy physics (HEP). The tokenizers that convert detector data into discrete inputs for such models, however, are comparatively unexplored. This paper addresses this gap by learning discrete and compressed tokenized representations of HEP datasets. We demonstrate the approach on four modalities covering two different simulated detectors that vary in complexity. Treating tokenization as a rate-distortion problem, a systematic hyperparameter scan yields four design rules for tokenizing HEP data that hold across all set-based modalities studied. The cost of tokenization is evaluated on tasks including jet $p_T$ regression, particle flow reconstruction, and flavor tagging. We find that tokenization preserves much of the information needed for these tasks at compression factors of approximately 4-11$\times$ while keeping the jet $p_T$ resolution below 1%. We also present heptokens, an open-source library for tokenizing HEP data.

</details>


### [146] [Stochastic gravitational waves from narrow-band cosmological magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12719)
*Abdias Aires,Tatsuya Daniel*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 窄带原初磁场产生的引力波谱在共动波数 2k_c 处峰值并具陡峭截断；将其应用于复合时期附近的超轻暗物质磁成因模型，发现虽可进入脉冲星计时频段，但振幅低于实验灵敏度，并据此对其他时期模型给出温度下限 T ≳ 1-10 MeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初磁场可能作为引力波源，其谱形与可探测性需要定量刻画；同时脉冲星计时阵列（pulsar timing arrays, PTA）暗示的随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational wave background）是否可由磁成因解释尚待检验。

Method: 解析计算窄带（narrow-band）峰值于共动波数 k_c 的磁场功率谱所生成的引力波谱；并将其结果应用到复合时期（recombination）附近、由轴子-光子耦合（axion-photon coupling）经参量共振（parametric resonance）产生螺旋磁场（helical magnetic field）的超轻暗物质（ultralight dark matter）磁成因模型。

Result: 引力波信号在 2k_c 处峰值并在该尺度出现尖锐截断；在复合时期附近的模型中，足够大的耦合可使频率进入脉冲星计时实验频段，但在物理允许的磁场能量密度下振幅低于实验灵敏度；推广至其他时期的磁成因模型，得到产生 PTA 所暗示随机引力波背景的磁成因过程温度约为 T ≃ 160 MeV，并给出可行模型下限 T ≳ 1-10 MeV。

Conclusion: 窄带磁场产生的引力波具有 2k_c 峰值与陡峭截断的特征谱；该机制在复合时期附近的超轻暗物质磁成因实现中难以达到脉冲星计时实验灵敏度，据此对更一般磁成因模型施加了温度下限约束（T ≳ 1-10 MeV）。

Abstract: We calculate the spectrum of gravitational waves generated by a cosmological magnetic field with a narrow-band power spectrum. For a spectrum narrowly peaked at a comoving wavenumber $k_c$, we find that the gravitational wave signal peaks at $2k_c$ and has a sharp cutoff at this scale. We apply this result to an ultralight dark matter model of magnetogenesis operating around recombination, in which an axion-photon coupling generates a helical magnetic field by parametric resonance. We determine the conditions for the resulting gravitational waves to enter the frequency range of pulsar timing experiments. Although the required frequency can be reached for sufficiently large coupling, the corresponding amplitude is below the experimental sensitivity for physically allowed energy densities of the magnetic field. We generalize this conclusion to models of magnetogenesis operating at other epochs, estimating as $T \simeq 160 \mathrm{MeV}$ the temperature of a magnetogenesis process assumed to be the source of the stochastic gravitational wave background suggested by pulsar timing arrays. We place the lower bound $T \gtrsim 1$-$10 \mathrm{MeV}$ on such viable models.

</details>
