### [147] [Effective field theory for strong phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10692)
*Oliver Gould,Farbod-Sayyed Rassouli,Paul M. Saffin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种适用于强相变（strong phase transition）的微扰有效场论（effective field theory, EFT）构造方法，突破传统高温量纲约化（dimensional reduction）工具的适用限制。该方法在三维空间中给出具有非多项式场依赖的 EFT，并在双标量模型及含费米子与规范场的扩展中验证其系统可改进性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高温量纲约化方法在强相变情形下失效，缺乏可控的微扰处理框架。本文旨在为这类标准工具不适用的区域建立受控的微扰展开。

Method: 通过识别耦合、质量与背景场之间的幂次计数关系（power counting relations），构造适配该区域的受控微扰展开；指出序参量场在相变时保持轻质量，从而存在受控的导数展开（derivative expansion），但大外场需被重求和（resummation）。所得 EFT 位于三维且具有非多项式场依赖。

Result: 在双标量模型（two-scalar model）中，验证了到次次领头阶（next-to-next-to-leading order, NNLO）的重整化标度抵消（renormalisation scale cancellation），并发现随微扰阶数升高，潜热（latent heat）的修正逐渐减小。扩展至含费米子与规范场后，识别出一大类可系统改进强相变热力学描述的模型。

Conclusion: 所构造的微扰 EFT 框架为强相变提供受控且可系统改进的热力学计算途径，适用于一大类包含标量、费米子与规范场的模型。

Abstract: We show how to construct perturbative effective field theories for strong phase transitions, where the standard tools of high-temperature dimensional reduction fail. By identifying the relevant power counting relations for the couplings, masses and background fields, we construct a controlled perturbative expansion adapted to this regime. We find that the order parameter field remains light at the transition, so that there is a controlled derivative expansion, even though large external fields need to be resummed. The resulting effective field theories for strong phase transitions live in three dimensions and have nonpolynomial field dependence. Applying our framework to a two-scalar model, we verify renormalisation scale cancellation through next-to-next-to-leading order and find decreasing corrections to the latent heat as the perturbative order is increased. Extending to include fermions and gauge fields, we identify a broad class of models in which strong-transition thermodynamics are systematically improvable.

</details>


### [148] [Chiral Symmetries in the Standard Model of Particle Physics and its Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13332)
*Sergio Ferrando Solera*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本论文围绕手征规范理论（chiral gauge theory）的精确重整化与唯象学展开：一方面在 BMHV 方案下通过代数重整化（algebraic renormalization）消除虚假反常并推导 νSMEFT 四费米算符的单圈对称性恢复有限抵消项；另一方面将左右对称模型（LRM）视为有效场论，从对撞机观测量导出新规范玻色子质量与 LR 能标的稳健下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型中宇称对称性破缺使手征规范理论成为理论粒子物理的核心课题，而 LRM 通过自发对称性破缺机制解释宇称破缺。LHC 未直接探测到 BSM 物理，促使研究转向精确观测量与有效场论（特别是 SMEFT 框架），但手征理论中采用维度正规化时需引入 D 维 γ_5 方案，由此产生破坏规范对称性的项，阻碍 Slavnov-Taylor 恒等式。

Method: 采用数学上自洽的 Breitenlohner-Maison-'t Hooft-Veltman（BMHV）方案处理 D 维 γ_5；运用代数重整化方法系统消除由该方案引入的虚假反常（spurious anomalies）；推导重整化 νSMEFT 四费米算符所需的一圈对称性恢复有限抵消项。此外，将嵌入更大 BSM 理论（如大统一理论）的 LRM 作为 EFT 处理，仅保留实现自发对称性破缺所需的最小标量内容，并基于对撞机观测量进行分析。

Result: 推导出用于重整化 νSMEFT 四费米算符的一圈对称性恢复有限抵消项，并给出通过代数重整化消除虚假反常的教学式介绍；同时针对有效版本与传统版本 LRM，导出了新规范玻色子质量与 LR 能标的稳健下界，且结果独立于理论的其他参数。

Conclusion: BMHV 方案下引入的规范对称性破坏项可通过代数重整化予以消除，并可由有限抵消项实现 νSMEFT 四费米算符的单圈重整化；将 LRM 作为 EFT（源于 GUT 等 BSM 嵌入，TeV 能标无剩余离散对称性）进行处理，可基于对撞机观测量获得不依赖其他参数的规范玻色子质量与 LR 能标下界。

Abstract: Chiral gauge theories are a central focus of research in theoretical particle physics due to the parity symmetry breaking observed in the Standard Model. Left-Right Symmetric Models (LRM) explain the breaking of parity through a Spontaneous Symmetry Breaking Mechanism, predicting new $W_R$ and $Z_R$ gauge bosons. The absence of direct BSM physics detection at the LHC has driven research towards precision observables and Effective Field Theories (EFTs), particularly the SMEFT framework. Precision radiative calculations in chiral theories using dimensional regularization require a $D$-dimensional $γ_5$ prescription, such as the mathematically consistent Breitenlohner-Maison-'t Hooft-Veltman (BMHV) scheme. However, this scheme introduces gauge symmetry-breaking terms that obstruct Slavnov-Taylor identities. A primary objective of this thesis is to provide a pedagogical introduction to cancelling these spurious anomalies via algebraic renormalization, and to derive the one-loop symmetry-restoring finite counterterms required to renormalize the four-fermion operators of the $ν$SMEFT. Furthermore, the thesis explores UV completions like LRMs embedded in other BSM theories like the Grand Unified Theories. This embedding implies that no discrete symmetries remain at the TeV scale and allows LRMs to be treated as EFTs with the minimal scalar content needed to perform the Spontaneous Symmetry Breaking Mechanism. The second main objective is to derive robust lower bounds on the masses of the new gauge bosons and the LR scale using collider observables. This analysis is performed for both the effective and traditional LRMs, ensuring that the results are independent of other parameters of the theory.

</details>


### [149] [Utilizing Neural Networks for Solving Functional Renormalization Group Flow Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13754)
*J. Gurzó,G. Fejős*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于物理信息神经网络（physics-informed neural networks, PINNs）的方法求解泛函重整化群（functional renormalization group, FRG）流方程，在 $O(N)$ 模型有限温度相变研究中摆脱大 $N$ 近似（large-$N$ approximation），并通过与网格 Runge-Kutta 方法对比验证了网络学习的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前基于类似方法的尝试依赖大 $N$ 近似，且作者认为这些既有解难以推广到更复杂的对称性（symmetry）情形。为此，需要在更一般设定下求解 FRG 流方程，以获得新的认识。

Method: 采用 PINNs 求解 FRG 流方程，其技术核心是一系列拟设（ansätze），将小场近似与大场近似方案（small- and large-field approximation schemes）同神经网络结合，从而在整个场范围内获得高精度近似。

Result: 方法在局部势近似（local potential approximation, LPA）下成功学习有效势（effective potential）对温度、场与尺度（scale）的依赖关系；与传统的基于网格的 Runge-Kutta 结果进行比较，表明网络能够实现准确近似。

Conclusion: PINNs 方法可无需大 $N$ 近似地处理 $O(N)$ 模型有限温度相变，并具备推广至更复杂对称性问题的潜力，为 FRG 流方程的求解提供了新途径。

Abstract: A novel physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) based approach for solving functional renormalization group flow equations is presented. We investigate the finite temperature phase transition in $O(N)$ models without relying on the large-$N$ approximation, which was used in earlier attempts with similar methods. We argue that these previous solutions cannot easily be generalized for more complex symmetries, and hence our approach can provide new insight. Our technique relies on a series of ansätze that combines the small- and large-field approximation schemes with a neural network, leading to a highly accurate approximation over the entire field range. We compare the PINNs approach with traditional grid-based Runge-Kutta results and show that the network can successfully learn the temperature, field, and scale dependencies of the effective potential in the local potential approximation.

</details>


### [150] [The LZ inelastic splitting as a probe of anomaly mediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13930)
*Seung J. Lee,Taewook Youn*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文将 LZ 248 keV 反冲候选事件解释为赝狄拉克暗物质（pseudo-Dirac dark matter），其非弹性 Z 相互作用受混合压低。指出 MSSM 中 Higgsino 极限与太阳中微子界限冲突，而 gaugino 实现需要未受保护的质量简并。在 MRSSM 中通过 R 对称狄拉克 bino（R-symmetric Dirac bino）产生所需 Z 跃迁，并用 wino 共湮灭（coannihilation）给出观测丰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ 248 keV 反冲候选事件需要一个能同时满足太阳中微子界限与暗物质丰度的理论解释。MSSM 中 Higgsino 极限与太阳中微子界限冲突，gaugino 实现则依赖未受保护的质量简并，因此需寻找更自然的谱结构实现。

Method: 在 MRSSM 框架下，利用 R 对称狄拉克 bino 产生所需 Z 跃迁，由 wino 共湮灭提供观测到的 relic abundance（遗迹丰度）。在高能标施加 N=2 超对称（N=2 supersymmetry）的 Higgs 与规范耦合关系并演化至低能；采用异常介导（anomaly mediation）处理 Majorana 劈裂，并借助五维翘曲构造（five-dimensional warped construction）经 Goldberger-Wise 稳定化与 no-scale Kähler 势的圈修正生成该能标。

Result: 在其余模型参数固定的情况下，得到约 300–850 GeV 的谱，满足所采用的约束且具有较小的对角轴流（diagonal axial current）。若异常介导主导 Majorana 劈裂，对于几百 keV 的劈裂，其局域能标为数十 MeV。

Conclusion: MRSSM 中的赝狄拉克暗物质框架可同时满足 LZ 候选事件、太阳中微子界限与遗迹丰度约束，并通过弹性散射（elastic scattering）、太阳中微子与长寿命带电态（long-lived charged states）保持可检验性。

Abstract: The LZ 248 keV recoil candidate admits an interpretation in terms of pseudo-Dirac dark matter with a mixing-suppressed inelastic $Z$ interaction. In the MSSM, the Higgsino limit conflicts with the adopted solar-neutrino bound, while a gaugino realization requires an unprotected mass degeneracy. We realize such spectra in the MRSSM, where an $R$-symmetric Dirac bino produces the required $Z$ transition and wino coannihilation supplies the observed relic abundance. We impose the Higgs and gauge coupling relations of $N=2$ supersymmetry at a high scale and evolve them to low energy. With the remaining model parameters held fixed, we obtain spectra spanning roughly 300-850 GeV that satisfy the adopted constraints and have a small diagonal axial current. If anomaly mediation dominates the Majorana splitting, its local scale is tens of MeV for splittings of a few hundred keV. A five-dimensional warped construction generates this scale through Goldberger-Wise stabilization and a loop correction to the no-scale Kähler potential. The spectra remain testable through elastic scattering, solar neutrinos and long-lived charged states.

</details>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Evidence of Taste-Symmetry restoration for Kähler-Dirac fermions on Euclidean Dynamical Triangulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13791)
*Mingwei Dai,Walter Freeman,Mason Grieb,Jack Laiho,Marc Schiffer,Judah Unmuth-Yockey*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该研究在欧几里得动力学三角剖分（EDT）中考察通过引力耦合的Kähler-Dirac费米子双粒子束缚态谱，采用淬火近似，发现受U(1)对称性保护的束缚态质量在手征极限下消失，而未受保护的质量标度随连续极限趋于零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究引力背景下Kähler-Dirac费米子束缚态的谱性质，并检验其U(1)对称性与连续统手征对称性的关联如何影响束缚态质量在手征极限下的行为。同时旨在判断未受保护质量的标度是否对应普朗克质量或涌现de Sitter背景曲率尺度等物理标度。

Method: 在欧几里得动力学三角剖分（Euclidean Dynamical Triangulations, EDT）中将Kähler-Dirac费米子通过引力耦合，并采用淬火近似（quenched approximation）：几何可涨落，但物质对几何无反馈（back-reaction）。所用量子作用量保持精确的U(1)对称性，该对称性与连续统手征对称性（chiral symmetry）相关。

Result: 受U(1)对称性保护的束缚态质量在费米子裸质量趋于零的手征极限（chiral limit）下消失。未受U(1)对称性保护的束缚态质量在手征极限下不消失，但其对称性破缺相关标度并非普朗克质量或涌现de Sitter背景的曲率尺度，而似乎表现为晶格间距的正幂次，随连续极限（continuum limit）逼近而减小。

Conclusion: 结果表明，EDT中的Kähler-Dirac费米子可被解释为平空间晶格场论中交错费米子（staggered fermions）的引力类比，二者具有相似行为。

Abstract: We study the spectrum of two-particle bound states of Kähler-Dirac fermions coupled via gravity in Euclidean Dynamical Triangulations (EDT). We work in the quenched approximation, where the geometry is allowed to fluctuate, but there is no back-reaction from the matter on the geometry. The Kähler-Dirac action respects an exact $U(1)$ symmetry that is related to continuum chiral symmetry, and this $U(1)$ symmetry ensures that some of the bound-state masses vanish in the chiral limit of vanishing fermion bare mass. The bound-state masses that are not protected by the $U(1)$ symmetry do not vanish in the chiral limit, but the scale associated with the symmetry breaking does not appear to be a physical scale like the Planck mass or the curvature scale of the emergent de Sitter background. Rather, the mass scale associated with this non-degeneracy appears to be a positive power of the lattice spacing, decreasing as the continuum limit is approached. This suggests that Kähler-Dirac fermions in EDT can be interpreted as the gravity analog of staggered fermions in flat-space lattice field theories, with similar behavior.

</details>

### [152] [Recovering a Riemannian manifold from minimal surface areas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13405)
*Catalin Carstea,Matti Lassas,Tony Liimatainen,Leo Tzou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文研究由极小曲面（minimal surfaces）的面积数据恢复三维黎曼流形（Riemannian manifold）的度量，证明了在极小区分叶流形上面积可决定度量（至边界固定等距），并给出紧致带边三维流形上爱因斯坦度量（Einstein metric）的全局刚性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于解决外部形式下最小面积数据（least-area data）的边界刚性（boundary rigidity）猜想，并探讨在无需对极小曲面施加拓扑限制的条件下，面积数据能否唯一确定度量。

Method: 方法基于对由极小曲面分叶（foliated）的三维黎曼流形，利用极小曲面面积作为数据，分析其与度量之间的对应关系；对爱因斯坦度量情形，则附加基本群条件 $π_1(M,\partial M)=0$ 与非捕获条件（non-trapping condition）。

Result: 结果表明极小曲面的面积可确定分叶流形上的度量，且等距映射在边界上固定；由此解决了外部形式下最小面积数据的边界刚性猜想，且不要求极小曲面的拓扑限制。同时在 $π_1(M,\partial M)=0$ 与非捕获条件下，证明了紧致带边三维流形上爱因斯坦度量的全局刚性。

Conclusion: 结论为极小曲面面积数据在三维情形下具有强刚性，可恢复度量并给出爱因斯坦度量的全局刚性，验证了相关边界刚性猜想；摘要末尾内容不完整，未提供该爱因斯坦结果的进一步推论。

Abstract: We study the recovery of a three-dimensional Riemannian manifold from the areas of minimal surfaces. We show that areas of minimal surfaces determine the metric on manifolds foliated by minimal surfaces, up to boundary-fixing isometry. This resolves the conjecture on boundary rigidity for least-area data in an exterior formulation, even without topological restrictions on the minimal surfaces. We also prove global rigidity for Einstein metrics on compact three-manifolds with boundary, assuming $\(π_1(M,\partial M)=0\)$ and a non-trapping condition. The Einstein result includes the

</details>


### [153] [Compactly supported instability of Riemannian Schwarzschild--Tangherlini metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13507)
*Erwann Delay*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明在高维黎曼 Schwarzschild--Tangherlini 度量（Riemannian Schwarzschild--Tangherlini metric，n≥4）上，Ozuch 的不稳定 TT 张量（TT tensor）属于"TT 张量机器"（TT-tensor machine）的像，从而可用光滑紧支 TT 张量逼近，证明其通常意义下的不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前即使 4 维情形也未见到精确的光滑紧支不稳定 TT 张量，故非紧流形上通常意义的不稳定性证明存在缺口。

Method: 在黎曼 Schwarzschild--Tangherlini 度量上，将 Ozuch 的不稳定 TT 张量表示为"TT 张量机器"的像，并借助连续性在适当加权 Sobolev 空间（weighted Sobolev spaces）中用光滑紧支 TT 张量逼近。

Result: 所得光滑紧支 TT 张量上稳定性二次型（stability quadratic form）仍为负，由此证明非紧流形通常意义下的不稳定性。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，此前尚无已知的精确光滑紧支不稳定 TT 张量，即使在 4 维情形亦如此。

Abstract: On the Riemannian Schwarzschild--Tangherlini metric of dimension $n\geq 4$, we show that the destabilizing TT tensor of Ozuch lies in the image of the ``TT-tensor machine''. By continuity, it can then be approximated, in suitable weighted Sobolev spaces, by smooth compactly supported TT tensors, on which the stability quadratic form is still negative. This proves instability in the usual sense for noncompact manifolds. To our knowledge, no exact smooth compactly supported destabilizing TT tensor was known before, even in 4D.

</details>


<div id='physics.bio-ph'></div>

# physics.bio-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Efficient Transport Networks through Edge Pruning by Relative Resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13582)
*Mathieu Le Verge-Serandour,Viktor Vasilev,Lara Szeimies,Karen Alim*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种仅依赖局部规则、无需全局目标或特定边界条件的输运网络（transport network）自适应方法：按相对电阻（relative resistance）依次剪除冗余边，从而得到稀疏、富含环路且低耗散的网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输运网络对生命系统与工程系统均至关重要。核心问题是：网络能否仅凭局部规则高效适应，而不依赖全局目标或特定边界条件？生物输运网络中的观察结果为此提供了启发。

Method: 受生物输运网络观察启发，对网络进行逐步剪枝：以相对电阻为判据，移除那些电阻高于“经由网络其余部分传输流量”所需电阻的边。该冗余度量是局部的、无边界条件依赖的，且仅由拓扑（topology）决定。

Result: 按此局部冗余度量进行适应后，网络演化为稀疏（sparse）、富含环路（loop-rich）且低耗散（low-dissipation）的结构。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅凭由拓扑设定的局部、无边界条件的冗余度量，即可实现输运网络的有效适应，并自发产生兼具稀疏性、环路丰富性与低耗散特性的网络形态。

Abstract: Transport networks are key to living and engineered systems. How can transport networks adapt efficiently using only local rules, without a global objective or specific boundary conditions? Inspired by observations in biological transport networks, we adapt networks by subsequently pruning edges by relative resistance, thus removing edges whose resistance is higher than the resistance for routing flow through the rest of the network. We find that adaptation by such a local, boundary-condition-free measure of redundancy, set by topology alone, yields sparse, loop-rich, low-dissipation networks.

</details>

### [155] [Entropic-ordered superfluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12635)
*Isabella Zane,Andrew Lucas*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一个极简的经典晶格模型，通过带“软”转子的三维 XY 模型（three-dimensional XY model），在任意高温下实现超流性（superfluidity），其 U(1) 对称性因熵序（entropic order）在正临界温度之上自发破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索超流性是否可存在于任意高温，突破常规认为高温涨落会破坏有序相的直觉。

Method: 方法采用带“软”转子（soft rotors）的修改版三维 XY 模型构建经典晶格模型，并在该高温区间研究流体动力学（hydrodynamics），检验其与涨落耗散定理（fluctuation-dissipation theorem）的一致性。

Result: 结果显示，全局 U(1) 对称性在正临界温度之上因熵序而自发破缺，高温下虽噪声方差很大，但不足以破坏超流性或戈德斯通模（Goldstone modes）的长寿命。

Conclusion: 结论表明该极简模型可支持任意高温下的超流性，且其高温流体动力学与涨落耗散定理自洽，高温噪声不必然摧毁超流有序与戈德斯通模寿命。

Abstract: We present a minimal classical lattice model of superfluidity at arbitrarily high temperatures, using a modified three-dimensional XY model with ``soft" rotors. The global U(1) symmetry is spontaneously broken above a positive critical temperature due to entropic order. We study hydrodynamics in this high-temperature regime and verify consistency with the fluctuation-dissipation theorem, explaining how the large noise variance at very high temperatures is not sufficient to destroy superfluidity or the long lifetime of Goldstone modes.

</details>


### [156] [Spectral optimization of spherical lattices and applications to classical and quantum interacting spin systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12663)
*Jonas Völler,Grigorios Makris,Fabian Hassler,Stefan Wessel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于离散拉普拉斯算子（Laplace operator）谱的球面格点离散优化算法，通过梯度下降最小化连续 SO(3) 对称性多重态的分裂，从而获得近均匀三角格点，并验证其对相互作用模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面格点离散常因离散几何破坏连续旋转对称性（rotational symmetry），导致谱学与临界行为出现偏差。作者希望以自由粒子谱为出发点，构造能更好保持连续对称性的球面格点。

Method: 从精细化二十面体格点（refined icosahedral lattices）出发，以离散拉普拉斯算子谱为判据，用梯度下降（gradient descent）最小化 $(2\ell+1)$ 维连续 SO(3) 多重态（multiplets）的分裂，同时使三角形单元的外接圆半径（circumradii）与周长趋于均匀。

Result: 优化仅使用自由粒子谱，但所得格点可迁移至相互作用模型：在临界 Ising 与 Potts 模型（Potts models）中旋转对称性得以恢复，三维 Ising 共形场论（conformal field theory）的主标度维数（primary scaling dimensions）被准确重现（需采用适当的几何相关相互作用强度）。高角动量后代态（descendant states）显示，在二十面体对称性保护之外仍存在微弱残余对称性破缺。

Conclusion: 基于自由粒子谱的格点优化可有效恢复连续旋转对称性并准确再现临界标度维数，为球面格点上的相互作用场论与共形场论数值研究提供可行方案；残余对称性破缺主要体现在高角动量后代态中。

Abstract: We develop an optimization algorithm for spherical lattice discretizations based on the spectrum of the discretized Laplace operator. Starting from refined icosahedral lattices, we minimize the splitting of the $(2\ell+1)$, $\ell=0,1,2,\dots$, continuum multiplets expected from $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ symmetry via gradient descent. This approach simultaneously produces lattices based on triangles with nearly uniform circumradii and perimeters. Although the optimization uses only the free-particle spectrum, we show that the resulting lattices carry over to interacting models. On the optimized lattices, rotational symmetry is restored for the critical Ising and Potts models and primary scaling dimensions are accurately reproduced for the three-dimensional Ising conformal field theory, when appropriate geometry-dependent interaction strengths are used. Higher-angular-momentum descendant states reveal weak residual symmetry breaking beyond the sectors protected by the underlying icosahedral symmetry.

</details>


### [157] [Scalene Yang-Baxter triples realize all nearest-neighbour Hamiltonians with global additive symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13782)
*Pramod Padmanabhan,Somnath Maity,Akash Sinha,Vladimir Korepin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文建立了一维最近邻哈密顿量守恒一站点算符均匀或交错求和的充要条件：其键哈密顿量密度（至局域差分项）须位于键算符 $w_j \pm w_{j+1}$ 的对易子（commutant）中。由此可用标量Yang-Baxter三元组的monodromy矩阵构造所有此类哈密顿量及其守恒荷，且不要求平移不变性与Yang-Baxter可积性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在刻画具有可加守恒荷（additive conserved charge）的一维最近邻哈密顿量，建立键算符对易子条件与守恒荷存在性之间的充要关系，并给出统一的构造框架。其动机还包括在不依赖传统Yang-Baxter可积性和平移不变性的更一般条件下，系统地生成和分类此类模型。

Method: 方法以键算符 $w_j \pm w_{j+1}$ 的对易子（commutant）为核心：证明键哈密顿量密度在其内（至在周期边界条件下求和的局域差分项）是守恒均匀/交错求和 $w_j$ 的充要条件。随后从这些局域对易子出发，利用标量Yang-Baxter三元组（scalene Yang-Baxter triples）的规则与非规则元素构造monodromy矩阵来生成相应哈密顿量与守恒荷。构造与表示无关，适用于任意有限局域维度，且不要求平移不变性。

Result: 结果给出了一维最近邻哈密顿量守恒均匀或交错 $w_j$ 求和的充要条件，并表明此类哈密顿量及其守恒荷均可由标量Yang-Baxter三元组的monodromy矩阵构造得到。结论适用于长度 $L \geq 3$ 的周期链，交错情形需偶数长度；每条键上的相互作用可独立地从对应完整对易子中选取。文中以自旋-$\tfrac12$和自旋-1系统作了说明，并讨论了与双对易子定理（double-commutant theorem）的关系。

Conclusion: 该构造在任意有限局域维度下穷尽了具有指定可加对称性的哈密顿量，无需通常的Yang-Baxter可积性要求。对一站点生成元 $w$ 及其关联键对易子的分类，为分类具有可加守恒荷的此类哈密顿量提供了路径。

Abstract: We establish that bond Hamiltonian densities, up to local difference terms whose sum vanishes under periodic boundary conditions, lying in the commutant of bond operators, $w_j\pm w_{j+1}$, is a necessary and sufficient condition for a one-dimensional nearest-neighbour Hamiltonian to conserve a uniform or staggered sum of the one-site operators $w_j$. Starting from these local commutants, we show that every such Hamiltonian and its conserved charge can be obtained from monodromy matrices constructed using regular and non-regular members of scalene Yang-Baxter triples. The results hold on periodic chains of length $L\geq3$, with even length required in the staggered case. The results do not require translation invariance, and hence the interaction on each bond can be chosen independently from the full corresponding commutant. Our representation-independent construction applies in arbitrary finite local dimension and exhausts the Hamiltonians with either prescribed additive symmetry, without requiring ordinary Yang-Baxter integrability. We illustrate the construction with spin-$\tfrac12$ and spin-1 systems and discuss its relation to the double-commutant theorem. Classifying the one-site generators $w$ and their associated bond commutants provides a route to classifying such Hamiltonians with additive conserved charges.

</details>


### [158] [Graph-theoretic framework for semi-Markov networks: transient and stationary occupation probabilities, hitting times and weak ergodicity breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13505)
*Julian Voits*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文证明有限半马尔可夫网络（semi-Markov networks）的拉普拉斯变换占据概率与首达时间分布可用增广图上的加权生成树与森林的显式组合表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半马尔可夫网络能自然刻画具有非指数停留时间的粗粒化状态间随机转迁，广泛用于生物物理、神经科学、流行病学与金融等领域，但其组合结构与渐近性质尚缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 通过在增广图上构建加权生成树与森林的组合表示，对拉普拉斯变换后的占据概率和首达时间分布进行展开，并据此推导渐近结果。

Result: 得到马尔可夫链树定理（Markov Chain Tree Theorem）向半马尔可夫网络的推广：率常数由伪率（pseudo-rates）替代，即经该通道的退出概率加权的平均逗留时间倒数；给出无穷均值逗留时间下首达时间存活概率的长时间标度；将首达时间矩的组合公式扩展至半马尔可夫情形，且平均首达时间公式与具相应伪率的马尔可夫网络一致；据此给出有限均值半马尔可夫网络中Kemeny常数恒定性的简短证明。

Conclusion: 在重尾半马尔可夫网络中，对极限分布支撑集上非常数的时间可加状态可观测量，弱遍历性破缺（weak ergodicity breaking）具有一般性。

Abstract: Semi-Markov networks provide a natural framework for describing stochastic transitions between coarse-grained states with non-exponential dwell times, with applications ranging from biophysics and neuroscience to epidemiology and finance. Here, it is shown that finite semi-Markov networks admit explicit combinatorial representations of their Laplace-transformed occupation probabilities and hitting-time distributions as sums over weighted spanning trees and forests of an augmented graph. These representations yield asymptotic results, including a generalization of the Markov Chain Tree Theorem to semi-Markov networks, in which the rate constants replaced by pseudo-rates given by the inverse mean sojourn time weighted by the exit probability through this channel, as well as the long-time scaling of hitting-time survival probabilities in the presence of infinite-mean sojourn times. They also extend recently derived combinatorial formulas for hitting-time moments to the semi-Markov setting. In particular, the mean hitting-time formula coincides with that of a Markov network with the corresponding pseudo-rates, which also yields a short proof of the constancy of Kemeny's constant for finite-mean semi-Markov networks. Finally, we use the same framework to show that weak ergodicity breaking occurs generically for time-additive state observables that are nonconstant on the support of the limiting distribution in heavy-tailed semi-Markov networks.

</details>


### [159] [Replica Transition at Measurement-Altered Ising Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13802)
*Kabir Khanna,Sara Murciano,Romain Vasseur*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究监测下的临界 Ising 链（monitored critical Ising chain）中测量后态集合的复制品（replica）结构，指出其相变恰好发生在物理相关的复制品极限 Q→1，从而阻碍常规复制品处理，并用边界共形场论（boundary conformal field theory, BCFT）解释该机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对无隙量子基态（gapless quantum ground states）进行测量可产生新的普适现象，但其起源即使在典型 Ising 临界点处仍理解不足。复制品方法（replica methods）是研究此类测量后态集合的自然框架，其中 Q→1 描述物理的 Born 平均观测量，因此需要厘清复制品极限处的行为。

Method: 论文针对监测临界 Ising 链，考察其复制品化测量后态集合，分析其在 Q→1 处的相变行为；并采用边界共形场论（BCFT）方法刻画该相变的底层机制，进而对测量记录（measurement record）的 Shannon 熵中的普适 O(1) 常数进行微扰解析计算，并将工具应用于测量平均的自旋关联函数（spin correlators）。

Result: 研究发现，监测临界 Ising 链的复制品化测量后态集合恰在物理相关的复制品极限 Q→1 处发生相变，从而阻碍标准复制品处理。所提方法给出了测量记录 Shannon 熵中普适 O(1) 常数的微扰解析计算，该常数此前仅通过数值方法获得；同时对测量平均自旋关联函数给出描述。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了复制品极限处相变对标准复制品方法的阻碍，并借助 BCFT 提供了测量记录 Shannon 熵普适常数的解析结果；其方法为临界 Ising 链中测量结果与测量后态的普适性质提供了统一描述。

Abstract: Performing measurements on gapless quantum ground states can give rise to new universal phenomena, whose origins remain poorly understood even for the paradigmatic Ising critical point. Replica methods offer a natural framework to study such post-measurement ensembles, with the replica limit $Q\to 1$ describing physical Born-averaged observables. Here we show that, for the monitored critical Ising chain, the replicated post-measurement ensemble of states itself undergoes a phase transition precisely at the physically relevant replica limit $Q \to 1$, preventing standard replica treatments. We use boundary conformal field theory (BCFT) methods to explain the underlying mechanism of this transition. Our methods yield a perturbative analytic calculation of the universal $O(1)$ constant in the Shannon entropy of the measurement record, previously known only numerically. We also apply our tools to study measurement-averaged spin correlators, providing a unified description of the universal properties of measurement outcomes and post-measurement states in the critical Ising chain.

</details>


### [160] [Robust Golden Ratio universality as a consequence of a circulant symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13842)
*Luka Paljk,Johannes Schmidt,Vladislav Popkov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究指出，黄金比例（Golden Ratio, GR）动力学普适类可由通量雅可比矩阵（flux Jacobian）的全局循环对称性（circulant symmetry）自然产生，而无需精细调节参数。作者提出一个极简的多车道排他过程（multilane exclusion process）作为无参数模型，其所有守恒模式（多数为二重简并）均呈现GR普适性，并用推广的模式耦合理论（mode-coupling theory）与大尺度蒙特卡洛模拟相互验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前GR普适类（动力学指数 z ≡ φ）的出现需要系统参数的精细调节（fine-tuning），其普适性来源缺乏一般性解释。该工作旨在寻找无需调节、可广泛出现的GR普适类生成机制。

Method: 从广类马尔可夫过程（Markov processes）出发，利用通量雅可比矩阵的全局循环对称性推导GR普适类的出现条件，即守恒律数目为奇数且大于三。构建含伯努利稳态分布（Bernoulli stationary measure）的多车道排他过程作为最小无参数模型；采用推广至简并守恒模式的模式耦合理论进行解析预测，并以大规模蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟加以检验。

Result: 在满足守恒律数目为奇数且大于三的条件下，GR普适类作为循环对称性的结果普遍出现（generically）。所提出的多车道排他过程对所有守恒模式均表现出GR普适性，其中多数模式为二重简并（doubly degenerate）。理论预测与大规模蒙特卡洛模拟结果一致。

Conclusion: GR动力学普适类的出现可由通量雅可比矩阵的循环对称性稳健解释，无需参数微调，从而将其从特殊调节情形推广为一大类马尔可夫过程的普遍现象；多车道排他过程提供了可检验的最小实现。

Abstract: We find a robust scenario for the emergence of the Golden Ratio (GR) dynamical universality class, characterized by the dynamical exponent $z \equiv \varphi=\frac{1}{2}(1+\sqrt{5})$. Previously, its emergence required fine-tuning of system parameters. Here, the GR universality class appears generically in a wide class of Markov processes as a consequence of a global, circulant symmetry of the flux Jacobian. This symmetry leads to GR universality in systems where the total number of conservation laws is odd and larger than three. We introduce a multilane exclusion process with Bernoulli stationary measure as a minimal, parameter-free model, which features GR universality for all conserved modes, most of which are doubly degenerate. Our predictions, based on mode-coupling theory generalized to treat degenerate conserved modes, are corroborated by large-scale Monte Carlo simulations.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.HE'></div>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [Simulation Study of Reconstruction of Cosmic Ray Extensive Air Shower with Thermal Neutron Detected by ENDA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13183)
*Fan-Ping Li,Xin-Hua Ma,Hu Liu,Tian-Lu Chen,Shu-Wang Cui,Danzengluobu,Xin-Rui Gao,Wei Gao,Wen-Chao Gao,Hai-Bing Hu,Denis Kuleshov,Kirill Kurinov,Bing-Bing Li,Mao-Yuan Liu,Ye Liu,Igor Maliy,Da-Yu Peng,Yao-Hui Qi,Jia-Bo Qin,Dong Qu,Oleg Shchegolev,Yuri Stenkin,Yu-Sen Wang,Tian-Shuang Yang,Li-Qiao Yin,Hui-Qian Zhang,Liang-Wei Zhang,Shi-Yuan Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该工作通过模拟电子-中子探测器阵列（ENDA）对广延大气簇射（EAS）热中子的响应，研究其横向分布特征，验证双指数函数（double-exponential function）优于NKG函数，并揭示10 m处热中子密度与高能强子能量/数量的对数线性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在宇宙线能谱"膝"区（knee region）研究中，利用不同EAS次级粒子进行簇射重建对能量测定与成分区分至关重要。ENDA被设计用于探测高能强子在地面产生的热中子，这些热中子携带原初宇宙线的关键信息。

Method: 当前ENDA-64（64个探测器）部署于高海拔宇宙线观测站（LHAASO）内。本文对ENDA响应进行模拟，并利用蒙特卡洛（MC）模拟数据研究热中子横向分布，比较NKG函数与双指数函数的拟合效果。

Result: 双指数函数比NKG函数更准确地描述热中子横向分布，拟合与测量的热中子密度平均差异约在6%以内。距簇射轴10 m处的热中子密度ρ_n主要依赖于全部强子能量之和或高能强子数量，且ρ_n与EAS强子能量或高能强子数量的关系在对数尺度上可被线性函数良好拟合。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来利用ENDA进行混合探测以实现能量测定与成分区分奠定了基础。

Abstract: In the investigation of the "knee" region within the cosmic ray energy spectrum, extensive air shower (EAS) reconstruction by using different EAS secondary particles are of paramount importance for energy determination and composition separation and finally measurement of energy spectrum of different cosmic ray composition. The Electron-Neutron Detector Array (ENDA) is designed to detect thermal neutrons generated in the ground by high energy hadrons which constitute the "backbone" of EAS and carry vital information regarding the primary cosmic rays. At present, ENDA-64, consisting of 64 detectors, is employed inside the Large High Altitude Air Shower Observatory (LHAASO). This work simulates the response of ENDA and investigates the lateral distributions of thermal neutrons. A comparison of the NKG and double-exponential functions for describing this distribution using MC simulation data shows that the double-exponential function is more accurate. The mean difference between fitted and measured thermal neutron density is within ~ 6%. Meanwhile, the thermal neutron density at 10 m from the shower axis, $ρ_n$, depends mainly on the sum of all hadrons energy or the number of high-energy hadrons. The relationship between $ρ_n$ and EAS hadron energy or the number of high-energy hadrons can be well fitted by a linear function on a log scale. This work is fundamental for energy determination and composition separation with hybrid detection by using ENDA in the near future.

</details>


### [162] [Flip-Flop Shocks and Tori in Bondi-Hoyle-Lyttleton Accretion onto Rotating Kerr-Weyl Black Holes and Their Quasi-Periodic Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13384)
*Orhan Donmez,Imtiaz Khan,G. Mustafa*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该工作通过广义相对论流体动力学（GRHD）模拟，研究Weyl共形引力（Weyl conformal gravity）中Kerr-Weyl度规下旋转黑洞的Bondi-Hoyle-Lyttleton吸积，发现吸积流态由单一无量纲量eta主导，可依次呈现稳定激波锥、flip-flop振荡与振荡环面三种区域，并产生与test-particle频率无关的低频QPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Kerr黑洞之外的引力修正（Weyl共形引力）是否会改变吸积流结构与观测特征，尤其关注曲率项在吸积半径处的作用及其可观测性。

Method: 采用广义相对论流体动力学（general relativistic hydrodynamics, GRHD）模拟赤道面Bondi-Hoyle-Lyttleton吸积，使用Kerr-Weyl度规，自旋取a=0.9M与a=0.5M，扫描多个Weyl系数（经有效曲率参数k引入），并与相同设置的Kerr算例对比。

Result: 对所用k，视界、ISCO与test-particle频率与Kerr差异不超过约2%；但曲率项按kr^2增长并在吸积半径R_acc起作用。流动由eta=|k|R_acc^3/M控制：eta<~0.03为与Kerr不可区分的稳定激波锥；eta~0.2-0.3出现反de Sitter型项将激波气体困于黑洞后方并驱动持续flip-flop振荡；eta>~1.6锥体被破坏并形成振荡环面。强偏离模型中平均吸积率为Kerr的1.7-3倍，rms变化20-60%（Kerr-like为0.1-1%）；功率谱显示10 Msun黑洞基频2.9-4.8 Hz的全局QPO及其谐波阶梯，3:2与2:1来自单一非正弦振荡而非共振，且与test-particle频率（约70-200 Hz）无关，遵循f_0=xi f_K(R_acc)，稳定锥xi~0.7-1.1，flip-flop与环面xi~0.23-0.38。

Conclusion: 强振荡对明亮银河源可在数秒内被探测；所需|k|比星系旋转曲线值高18-36个数量级，因此探测到激波驱动的QPO即意味着测得对Kerr的局域偏离，而其缺失可对|k|给出限制。

Abstract: We present GRHD simulations of Bondi-Hoyle-Lyttleton accretion onto a rotating black hole in Weyl conformal gravity, described by the Kerr-Weyl metric. The equatorial flow is evolved for spins a=0.9M and a=0.5M and several Weyl coefficients, which enter through an effective curvature parameter k, and is compared with Kerr runs sharing the same setup. For the k considered, the horizon, ISCO and test-particle frequencies differ from Kerr by at most ~2%. However, the curvature term grows as kr^2 and acts at the accretion radius R_acc. The flow is controlled by one quantity, eta=|k|R_acc^3/M, the ratio of curvature-induced to Newtonian acceleration at R_acc. For eta<~0.03 a stable shock cone forms, indistinguishable from Kerr. For eta~0.2-0.3 the anti-de Sitter-like term traps shocked gas behind the black hole and drives persistent flip-flop oscillations. For eta>~1.6 the cone is destroyed and an oscillating torus forms. Spin modifies only the inner few gravitational radii and does not change the regime. In strongly deviating models the mean accretion rate is 1.7-3 times the Kerr value and varies by 20-60% rms, versus 0.1-1% in Kerr-like flows. The power spectra show global QPOs with fundamentals at 2.9-4.8 Hz for a 10 Msun black hole, with harmonic ladders whose 3:2 and 2:1 ratios come from one non-sinusoidal oscillation, not resonances. These lie in the low-frequency QPO band and are unrelated to test-particle frequencies (~70-200 Hz). They scale as f_0=xi f_K(R_acc), with xi~0.7-1.1 for stable cones and xi~0.23-0.38 for flip-flop and torus regimes. Strong oscillations would be detectable within seconds for a bright Galactic source. The required |k| exceeds galactic-rotation-curve values by 18-36 orders of magnitude, so a detected shock-driven QPO would measure a local deviation from Kerr, and its absence would constrain |k|.

</details>


### [163] [Resistive alignment of a black-hole magnetosphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13288)
*Ruben Lier,Oliver Porth*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文在电阻性相对论磁流体力学框架下建立解析理论，阐明黑洞磁层（black-hole magnetospheres）中磁轴与自旋轴加速对齐的物理机制，即 Blandford-Znajek 解的二阶自旋效应与显式电阻耗散共同驱动该过程，并以数值模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期数值模拟显示黑洞磁层会快速向磁轴与自旋轴对齐演化，但该过程的物理起源此前尚未明确，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 在电阻性相对论磁流体力学（resistive relativistic magnetohydrodynamics）中构建解析理论：以慢自旋（slow-spin）的 Blandford-Znajek 单极子解（monopole solution）作为外部磁场，将感应方程（induction equation）约化为描述电流片倾角（current-sheet inclination angle）的松弛方程（relaxation equation）。

Result: 分析表明对齐源自 Blandford-Znajek 解中的二阶自旋效应（second-order spin effects）与显式电阻耗散（explicit resistive dissipation）的联合作用；在多个自旋与电阻率参数下进行的电阻性 GRMHD 模拟中，存在一个参数区间，其测得的晚时长期衰减与解析预测在定量上一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为黑洞磁层磁轴—自旋轴对齐提供了可定量检验的解析解释，并得到数值模拟支持，确立了电阻耗散在该过程中的关键作用。

Abstract: Recent numerical simulations have shown that black-hole magnetospheres evolve rapidly toward alignment between the magnetic axis and spin axis but the physical origin of this process has remained unclear. We develop an analytic theory of this alignment within resistive relativistic magnetohydrodynamics. Taking the slow-spin Blandford-Znajek monopole solution as the exterior magnetic field, we reduce the induction equation to a relaxation equation for the current-sheet inclination angle. Our analysis shows that alignment results from second-order spin effects in the Blandford-Znajek solution combined with explicit resistive dissipation. We test this prediction using resistive-GRMHD simulations at several spins and resistivities and find a regime in which the measured late-time secular decay agrees quantitatively with the analytic prediction.

</details>

### [164] [The Redshift-Space Galaxy Power Spectrum at Two-Loop Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13867)
*Oliver H. E. Philcox*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文在有效场论（Effective Field Theory of Large Scale Structure）框架下构建了红移空间星系功率谱（galaxy power spectrum）的完整两圈（two-loop）模型，参数化包含49个自由系数，可将功率谱建模尺度范围从 $k_{\rm max}=0.2h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$ 提升至 $0.34h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$，但两圈项并未显著增强 $σ_8$ 约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大尺度结构（large-scale structure）分析以星系功率谱为核心，采用一圈微扰论（one-loop perturbation theory）建模，但在高波数处失效，无法从小尺度聚类中提取宇宙学信息。因此需要更高阶的建模方法扩展可用尺度范围。

Method: 基于大尺度结构有效场论，推导红移空间星系功率谱的两圈模型，涵盖五阶星系偏置（fifth-order galaxy bias）、红移空间畸变（redshift-space distortions）、小尺度非线性、空间非局域性、长波体流（bulk flows）、坐标畸变与随机性，共49个自由系数；同时编码一圈双谱（bispectrum）与三谱（trispectrum）及树图四谱、五谱的确定性贡献。数值实现采用 \textsc{vegas} 算法预计算圈积分。

Result: 模型通过高分辨率模拟（high-resolution simulations）测得的功率谱进行验证，涵盖暗物质与真实星系样本。两圈扩展显著提升建模尺度范围：对 $z=0.5$ 的亮红星系（luminous red galaxy）样本，$k_{\rm max}$ 从 $0.2h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$ 升至 $0.34h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$。完整似然（likelihood）可在毫秒量级完成评估。但新增信息多被五阶偏置与反项吸收，$σ_8$ 约束未被显著改善。

Conclusion: 两圈模型大幅扩展了功率谱可建模的尺度范围并支持毫秒级似然计算，但对 $σ_8$ 的约束提升有限。后续工作将评估两圈模型单独及与高阶统计量联合用于宇宙学参数推断的完整意义。

Abstract: Contemporary large-scale structure analyses center around the galaxy power spectrum, modeled with one-loop perturbation theory. This description naturally breaks down at large wavenumbers, prohibiting the extraction of cosmological information from small-scale clustering. In this work, we present a complete model for the redshift-space galaxy power spectrum up to two-loop order, derived in the framework of the Effective Field Theory of Large Scale Structure. This accounts for a wide variety of effects, including fifth-order galaxy bias, redshift-space distortions, small-scale non-linearities, spatial non-locality, long-wavelength bulk flows, coordinate distortions, and stochasticity, involving a total of 49 free coefficients. Our model additionally encodes all deterministic contributions to the one-loop bispectrum and trispectrum (as well as the tree-level quadspectrum and pentaspectrum), which depend on many of the same parameters. We implement the model numerically, using the \textsc{vegas} algorithm to precompute the loop integrals; this allows the full likelihood to be evaluated in milliseconds. The approach is validated using power spectra measured from high-resolution simulations, considering both dark matter and realistic galaxy samples. The extension to two-loop order greatly increases the range of scales over which the power spectrum can be modeled, with $k_{\rm max}$ rising from $0.2h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$ to $0.34h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$ for a luminous red galaxy sample at $z=0.5$. Much of the additional information is absorbed by the numerous fifth-order biases and counterterms; for this reason, constraints on $σ_8$ are not significantly enhanced by the inclusion of two-loop terms. Future work will assess the full implications of the two-loop model for cosmological parameter inference, both alone and in combination with higher-order statistics.

</details>


### [165] [Galaxy Clustering Can Reveal Host-Galaxy Properties of Binary Black Hole Mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11045)
*Daiki Watarai,Hayato Imafuku,Fumihiro Chuman*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种利用星系成团（galaxy clustering）信息推断双黑洞（binary black hole, BBH）并合宿主星系族群占比的统计框架，无需识别单个宿主星系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个 BBH 事件的宿主星系通常难以识别，但宿主星系族群会通过邻近星系的统计分布影响成团特征，因此希望在不依赖宿主识别的前提下推断并合的宿主族群构成。

Method: 在引力波定位区域（localization regions）内构造以宿主为条件的星系计数分布，据此建立事件级宿主类型似然（event-level host-type likelihoods），再将其组合以推断不同星系族群宿主 BBH 并合的比例；该框架不预设形成效率（formation efficiencies）或并合延迟时间分布（merger delay-time distributions）。

Result: 基于实测的恒星形成星系（star-forming galaxies）与淬灭星系（quenched galaxies）成团数据，在假设所有并合均发生于淬灭星系的理想化一年预测下，淬灭宿主占比的单侧 90% 置信下限中位数分别为 O5 的 0.19、post-O5 的 0.48 和第三代探测网络（third-generation networks）的 0.92。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在第三代探测器时代到来之前，即可通过星系成团对 BBH 并合的宿主星系族群进行推断。

Abstract: Galaxy clustering encodes information about the host galaxies of binary black hole (BBH) mergers even when individual hosts remain unidentified, because the distribution of neighboring galaxies depends statistically on the host-galaxy population. We construct event-level host-type likelihoods from host-conditioned galaxy-count distributions within gravitational-wave localization regions and combine them to infer the fractions of BBH mergers hosted by different galaxy populations. The framework applies to common and rare galaxy populations without prescribing formation efficiencies or merger delay-time distributions. Using measured star-forming and quenched galaxy clustering, idealized one-year forecasts assuming all mergers occur in quenched galaxies yield median one-sided 90% lower credible limits of 0.19, 0.48, and 0.92 on the quenched-host fraction for O5, post-O5, and third-generation networks. These forecasts illustrate the potential for host-galaxy population inference before the third-generation detector era.

</details>


### [166] [Relaxation Dark Energy: Compositional Dynamics and the Future of Cosmic Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13739)
*Gianmassimo Tasinato*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 论文提出将宇宙加速视为暗部门（dark sector）向组分平衡（compositional equilibrium）弛豫的瞬态阶段，而非永久 de Sitter 状态。通过线性响应理论（linear response theory）与 Onsager 近平衡回归假设，导出暗能量状态方程 w = w_0 a^{-n}，并获得 2.6–3.6σ 的非零弛豫偏好。同时证明分别守恒的物质与暗能量在点式本构关系下无法维持全域规则、梯度稳定的负压分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 若宇宙加速由正的宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）驱动，宇宙将趋于永久加速的 de Sitter 态，其量子引力（quantum gravity）解释仍不明确。论文试图以暗部门弛豫的瞬态图像替代这一永久加速图景，并为线性扰动提供一致的组分框架。

Method: 以线性响应理论（linear response theory）和 Onsager 近平衡回归假设（Onsager's near-equilibrium regression hypothesis）为指导，引入度量偏离平衡的组分变量（composition variable）；将常量分数弛豫（constant fractional relaxation）推广到完整背景演化，得到暗能量状态方程 w = w_0 a^{-n}。数据方面使用 DESI DR2 BAO 与 Planck 距离先验（distance priors），并搭配三种替代超新星（supernova）汇编。扰动层面提出在 phantom crossing 处保持正则的本构假设（constitutive prescription）。

Result: 该演化历史将早期暗能量抑制（early dark-energy suppression）、向上的 phantom crossing 与密度极大值，同未来的无压标度（pressureless scaling）未来相联系。观测上得到非零弛豫相对于常量 w 的名义 2.6–3.6σ 偏好。近平衡下，规则长波组分扰动以背景速率弛豫至领头阶，小尺度涨落以阻尼声波（damped sound waves）传播。并证明对分别守恒的物质与暗能量，点式本构关系无法在全过去演化中维持规则、梯度稳定的负压分支。

Conclusion: 研究探索了压力响应的修正方案及其与邻近均匀历史的相容性，并指出将近平衡描述延拓至完整宇宙演化所需的条件。总体而言，暗部门弛豫框架可作为瞬态加速的候选解释，但其本构关系在全演化范围内的自洽性仍需进一步扩展。

Abstract: If cosmic acceleration is driven by a positive cosmological constant, the universe approaches an eternally accelerating de Sitter state, whose interpretation in quantum gravity remains elusive. We develop a framework in which acceleration is instead a transient stage of dark-sector relaxation towards compositional equilibrium: matter and dark energy approach a stationary density ratio, eventually diluting like pressureless matter. Guided by linear response theory and Onsager's near-equilibrium regression hypothesis, we describe this evolution through a composition variable measuring departure from equilibrium. Extending constant fractional relaxation to the full background evolution yields the dark-energy equation of state $w=w_0a^{-n}$, where $n$ is the relaxation rate per e-fold of expansion. This history connects early dark-energy suppression, an upward phantom crossing and a density maximum to a pressureless scaling future. Using DESI DR2 BAO and Planck distance priors, with and without three alternative supernova compilations, we find a nominal $2.6$--$3.6σ$ preference for nonzero relaxation over constant $w$. The composition framework also motivates a constitutive prescription for linear perturbations that remains regular at phantom crossing. Near equilibrium, regular long-wavelength composition disturbances relax at the background rate to leading order, while small-scale fluctuations propagate as damped sound waves. We prove, however, that for separately conserved matter and dark energy, the pointwise constitutive relation cannot sustain a regular, gradient-stable negative-pressure branch throughout its full past evolution. We explore modifications of the pressure response and their consistency with neighbouring homogeneous histories, identifying requirements for extending the near-equilibrium description to the full cosmological evolution.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [BKT transition for $\mathsf{Sine}_β$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13484)
*Theodoros Assiotis*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文证明 $\mathsf{Sine}_\beta$ 点过程两点关联函数在长距离下发生 Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless（BKT）相变，对所有 $\beta>0$ 成立，并验证了 Forrester 预测的渐近展开首项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在确立 $\mathsf{Sine}_\beta$ 点过程长程关联中 BKT 相变的存在性，并检验 Forrester 提出的渐近展开预测，该预测此前缺乏严格证明。

Method: 基于对 $\mathsf{Sine}_\beta$ 点过程两点关联函数大距离渐近行为的严格分析，对所有 $\beta>0$ 进行证明。

Result: 证明了对所有 $\beta>0$，$\mathsf{Sine}_\beta$ 点过程两点关联函数在长距离下确实呈现 BKT 相变，且渐近展开首项与 Forrester 的预测一致。

Conclusion: 该结果严格确认了 $\mathsf{Sine}_\beta$ 点过程中 BKT 相变的存在性，并证实了 Forrester 渐近展开预测的首项。

Abstract: We prove the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) phase transition in the large-distance behaviour of the two-point correlation function of the $\mathsf{Sine}_β$ point process for all $β>0$, confirming the first term of an asymptotic expansion predicted by Forrester.

</details>


### [168] [TAP variational formula for 2-spin models with general interaction matrix and marginality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13670)
*David Belius,Simon Briend,Volodymyr Fomichov*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文为紧支撑极限谱分布的球面二自旋模型（spherical 2-spin models）建立了Thouless–Anderson–Palmer（TAP）自由能变分公式，适用于任意逆温度，并将此前球面Sherrington–Kirkpatrick模型的结果加以推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有TAP表示主要针对球面Sherrington–Kirkpatrick模型，其相互作用矩阵极限谱为Marchenko–Pastur型。本文旨在将TAP变分公式推广到极限谱分布仅具紧支撑的更一般球面二自旋模型，并处理临界情形下的marginality问题。

Method: 提出一种新的微正则（microcanonical）证明。按携带指定范数比例的最小顶部特征方向集合对球面进行划分；在每个划分元素上，剩余自由度处于有效高温，其贡献通过球面积分渐近（spherical-integral asymptotics）计算。所得辅助变分问题将宏观低温贡献与高温涨落项分离。

Result: 该辅助变分问题的最大化可恢复通常的TAP泛函与Plefka条件；同时表明仅TAP极大点邻域不足以捕捉Gibbs质量，所有与大特征值相关的特征方向在同一指数尺度上均有贡献，体现球面二自旋态的marginality。

Conclusion: 在紧支撑极限谱分布条件下，球面二自旋模型的自由能可由TAP变分公式刻画，且在任意逆温度下成立。该分解揭示了marginality的机制：Gibbs质量不能仅由TAP极大点邻域描述，而须计入全部大特征值方向的贡献。

Abstract: We establish a Thouless--Anderson--Palmer (TAP) variational formula for the free energy of spherical $2$-spin models whose interaction matrices have a compactly supported limiting spectral distribution. The formula holds at every inverse temperature and, in particular, extends the TAP representation previously obtained for the spherical Sherrington--Kirkpatrick model.
  Our main contribution is a new microcanonical proof designed to handle marginality. We partition the sphere according to the smallest collection of top eigendirections carrying a prescribed fraction of the norm. On every element of this partition, the remaining degrees of freedom are effectively at high temperature and their contribution can be computed through spherical-integral asymptotics. The resulting auxiliary variational problem separates the macroscopic low-temperature contribution from the high-temperature fluctuation term; its maximization recovers the usual TAP functional and Plefka condition. This decomposition also shows why neighborhoods of the TAP maximizers alone do not capture the Gibbs mass: all eigendirections associated with large eigenvalues contribute on the same exponential scale, a manifestation of the marginality of spherical $2$-spin states.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Separating Field--Camp Alignment from Structural Balance in a Frustrated Random-Field Ising Model of Social Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12578)
*Natalia Romero*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 在共享同一图结构、场赋值与边权重幅度的受阻随机场Ising模型（frustrated random-field Ising model）上，比较三种符号放置条件；结果表明驱动能量隙分布变化的是场-阵营对齐（field-camp alignment），而非单纯的三元平衡（triadic balance）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实符号网络（Epinions、Slashdot、Wikipedia）中平衡三元组（balanced triads）过量，而完全平衡的放置与未受阻铁磁体（unfrustrated ferromagnet）规范等价，因此既有模型将对抗性边独立按固定比例放置的做法可能遗漏关键机制。论文旨在厘清三元平衡本身与场-阵营对齐各自的作用。

Method: 在极化群体的受阻随机场Ising模型上设定三种条件：（1）独立放置基线；（2）阵营成员身份与选民场符号一致的平衡结构化条件；（3）同条件但随机置换阵营标签，使三元平衡在构造上相同而阵营-场相关性被移除。采用混淆受控的Kolmogorov-Smirnov检验比较采样所得能量隙分布（能量隙分布，energy-gap distribution；使用模拟退火，simulated annealing），并检验其对并行回火采样（parallel tempering）、系统规模（至N = 300）与结构噪声的稳健性。

Result: 对齐条件在所有测试密度下均与基线存在差异（p = 7.3e-8）；置换条件无显著差异（配对平均差0.003，95% CI [-0.041, 0.046]）。单纯三元平衡在N = 90时无可检测效应，在N = 180–300时至多产生小效应（KS距离配对变化约0.04，95% CI上界低于0.09）。

Conclusion: 效应由场-阵营对齐而非三元平衡单独驱动；其定性方向可由已知映射推出，本文贡献在于对该极限之外效应大小的定量、混淆受控测量，且结论对采样方法、系统规模与校准至真实网络的结构噪声均稳健。

Abstract: Frustrated Ising models of social networks mix ferromagnetic (agreement-seeking) and antiferromagnetic (antagonistic) couplings, and the antagonistic bonds are almost always placed independently at a fixed rate, yielding a +/-J spin glass. Real signed networks (Epinions, Slashdot, Wikipedia) instead show an excess of balanced triads, and perfectly balanced placement is gauge-equivalent to an unfrustrated ferromagnet. We compare three sign-placement conditions on a frustrated random-field Ising model of a polarised population, sharing an identical graph, field assignment, and edge-weight magnitudes: an independent baseline; a balance-structured condition whose camp membership coincides with each voter's field sign; and the same condition with camp labels randomly permuted, so triadic balance is identical by construction while the camp-field correlation is removed. On a confound-controlled Kolmogorov-Smirnov test of the sampled energy-gap distribution (simulated annealing), the aligned condition differs from the baseline at every density tested (p = 7.3e-8), while the permuted condition shows no significant difference (paired mean difference 0.003, 95% CI [-0.041, 0.046]). Field-camp alignment, not triadic balance alone, drives the effect. The qualitative direction follows from a known mapping; the contribution is a quantitative, confound-controlled measurement of the effect's size away from that limit, robust to parallel-tempering sampling, system sizes up to N = 300, and structural noise calibrated to real networks, while triadic balance alone produces no detectable effect at N = 90 and at most a small one at N = 180-300 (paired change in KS distance about 0.04, 95% CI upper bound below 0.09).

</details>

### [170] [Odd-parity surface magnetism in a Kondo insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12573)
*Maxim Dzero,Brian M. Andersen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出拓扑近藤绝缘体 SmB$_6$ 纳米线针尖与双共线反铁磁体 Fe$_{1+x}$Te 之间的自旋极化隧穿理论，指出时间反演对称性必须在针尖表面破缺，且偏压反转时磁对比度变号可作为表面时间反演破缺的光谱学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验观察到磁对比度随偏压反转变号，需理论解释其来源。作者首先论证任何时间反演对称的针尖（无论自旋-动量锁定多强）都无法产生实验观测的磁对比度，从而表明针尖表面必然存在时间反演破缺。

Method: 在 SmB$_6$ 表面理论的平均场（mean-field）处理框架下，分析传导 d 电子与 f 电子之间的相互作用，研究近藤杂化（Kondo hybridization）振幅中时间反演奇分量的凝聚及其对称性通道。

Result: 相互作用驱动约 T$_c$ ~10 K 的表面相变，其中时间反演奇分量在面外表面磁化的同一对称性通道中凝聚，使狄拉克表面态（Dirac surface states）打开能隙。相变以下，每个打开能隙的表面狄拉克锥的自旋分辨谱函数对其节点呈能量奇对称。对电中性针尖，费米能级位于表面 Γ 与 X 狄拉克节点之间，故自旋极化电导在零偏压附近变号。

Conclusion: 该变号现象是 SmB$_6$ 表面时间反演对称性破缺的光谱学特征（spectroscopic signature）。

Abstract: We formulate a theory of spin-polarized tunneling between a nanowire tip of a topological Kondo insulator SmB$_6$ and the bi-collinear antiferromagnet Fe$_{1+x}$Te in which the tunneling conductance which measures magnetic contrast changes sign when the bias is reversed. First we show that no time reversal symmetric tip, however strong its spin-momentum locking is, can produce experimentally observed magnetic contrast, which implies that time reversal must be broken at the tip surface. Within a mean-field treatment of the surface theory of SmB$_6$ we find that the interaction between conduction d-and f-electrons drives a surface phase transition near T$_c$ ~10 K in which the time reversal odd component of the Kondo hybridization amplitude condenses in the same symmetry channel as an out-of-plane surface magnetization, gapping out the Dirac surface states. Below the transition the spin-resolved spectral function of each gapped surface Dirac cone is odd in energy about its node. For a charge-neutral apex the Fermi level lies between the surface $Γ$ and $X$ Dirac nodes, so the spin-polarized conductance, which is determined by this function at the bias energy, reverses its sign close to zero bias. This sign change is thus a spectroscopic signature of time reversal symmetry breaking on the surface of SmB6.

</details>


### [171] [Search for free non-Abelian anyons in paired composite-fermion states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12645)
*Mytraya Gattu,J. K. Jain*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 基于部分子理论（parton theory）的微观计算表明，多数实验观测到的配对复合费米子（paired composite-fermion）态中元激发会束缚为阿贝尔分子（Abelian molecules），从而掩盖非阿贝尔编织统计（non-Abelian braid statistics）；宽量子阱中的 ν=1/2 态是例外，具有自由准粒子，是研究该统计的理想候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶数分母分数量子霍尔（fractional quantum Hall, FQH）态被理解为复合费米子的拓扑超导体（topological superconductor），理论上应支持服从非阿贝尔编织统计的元准粒子（quasiparticles, QPs）或准空穴（quasiholes, QHs）。然而实验观测到的多种配对态是否真能体现该统计尚不明确，本文旨在检验其元激发结构。

Method: 采用部分子理论（parton theory）的微观框架，对多个实验观测到的配对复合费米子态进行计算，考察其元准粒子与准空穴是否发生束缚。

Result: 多数配对态的元准粒子与准空穴普遍束缚形成阿贝尔分子，遮蔽了非阿贝尔编织统计；例外是宽量子阱（wide quantum wells）中的 ν=1/2 态，计算显示其存在自由的准粒子或准空穴。

Conclusion: 宽量子阱中的 ν=1/2 态被认定为实现与研究非阿贝尔编织统计的特别有前景的候选体系；此外，对这类束缚态的研究有助于揭示该态的拓扑结构（topological structure）。

Abstract: The even-denominator fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states, understood as topological superconductors of composite fermions, are predicted to support elementary quasiparticles (QPs) or quasiholes (QHs) obeying non-Abelian braid statistics. Using the microscopic framework of parton theory, we investigate many experimentally observed paired composite-fermion states and find that their elementary QPs and QHs in general bind to form Abelian molecules, thus obscuring non-Abelian braid statistics. An exception is the $ν=1/2$ state in wide quantum wells, where our calculations show free QPs or QHs; we thus identify this state as an especially promising candidate for realizing and studying non-Abelian braid statistics. We also indicate how the study of such bound states can help reveal the topological structure of the state.

</details>


### [172] [Improving the lower bound on the Laughlin gap: An SDP search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12700)
*Qiang Gao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文改进了 OpenAI 关于球面费米子 Laughlin 能隙猜想（Laughlin spectral-gap conjecture）的证明：通过更精细的三体尾部乘积扭结界（product-twirl bound）与四体配额的阶梯价格（staircase price）估计，将 Fock 空间不等式中的常数从 0.0461673… 提升至 0.0601 以上；进一步将辅助算符视为半定规划（semidefinite program, SDP）变量并扩大轨道模式集合，得到 γ_*=0.2190037845063196…，从而证明 Laughlin 态上方能隙至少为 1/5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenAI 的预印本以 Fock 空间不等式 H_Q^2 ≥ γH_Q 解决了完整 V_1 相互作用下的球面费米子 Laughlin 谱隙猜想，但其原始常数偏小（γ 仅低于 0.0461673…，能隙下界仅 1/25）。本文旨在不改变原有证明证书的前提下，尽可能放大该常数，使能隙下界更接近真实值，并检验此类辅助算符形式的极限能力。

Method: 沿用原证明确立的 Fock 空间不等式框架，引入两项更精细估计——三体尾部的乘积扭结界（product-twirl bound）与四体配额的阶梯价格（staircase price）；随后将辅助算符视作半定规划（SDP）的变量，扩大其作用的有限轨道模式集合，并以精确有理算术（exact rational arithmetic）对所得常数进行认证。

Result: 两项精细估计将常数提高到 0.0601 以上；SDP 方法给出对任意 γ < γ_*=0.2190037845063196… 成立的不等式 H_Q^2 ≥ γH_Q。由此，Laughlin 态上方能隙对所有足够大的球面至少为 1/5；未截断的平面 V_1 哈密顿量具有正谱，其下界为 γ_*，且该下界在粒子数与角动量上一致成立。启发式外推表明现有形式的辅助算符至多给出约 0.222，而有限系统精确对角化（exact diagonalization）给出的真实能隙接近 0.4。

Conclusion: 原 Fock 空间不等式论证可承载远大于原始值的常数，且 SDP 表述使候选辅助算符族易于检验；更大的辅助算符族有望将下界进一步推向真实能隙。改进证明与 SDP 搜索由 AI 模型 Claude Opus 5.5 在作者指导下完成。

Abstract: A recent preprint by OpenAI resolved the spherical fermionic Laughlin spectral-gap conjecture for the full $V_1$ interaction by a Fock-space inequality: $H_Q^2\ge γH_Q$ on the lowest-Landau-level Fock space for all large flux $Q$ and every $γ$ below $0.0461673\ldots$, so that the gap above the Laughlin state at filling $1/3$ is at least $1/25$. That proof, with its original constant, has also been formalized in Lean. We show that the same argument supports a much larger constant. Two sharper estimates, a product-twirl bound for the three-body tail and a staircase price for the four-body allowance, raise the constant above $0.0601$ without changing the original certificate. Treating the auxiliary operators as the variables of a semidefinite program (SDP) and enlarging the finite set of orbital modes on which they act then gives $H_Q^2\geγH_Q$ for every $γ<γ_*=0.2190037845063196\ldots$, certified by exact rational arithmetic. Consequently the gap above the Laughlin state is at least $1/5$ for all sufficiently large spheres, and the untruncated planar $V_1$ Hamiltonian has positive spectrum bounded below by $γ_*$, uniformly in particle number and angular momentum. A heuristic extrapolation of the SDP optima suggests that auxiliary operators of the present form can give at most about $0.222$, while exact diagonalization of finite systems places the true gap near $0.4$. A larger family of auxiliary operators could push the bound further towards the true gap, and the SDP formulation makes such families easy to test. The proof of the improvement and the SDP search were completed by the AI model Claude Opus 5.5 (Anthropic) under the author's direction.

</details>


### [173] [Homogeneous to multi-scale inhomogeneous complex magnetism in Ba3LnRu2O9 (Ln = Gd, Ho), Revealed by muSR and Machine-Learned Muon-Site Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12726)
*S. Ghosh,K. Sharma,E. Kushwaha,M. Kumar,G. Roy,A. Pandey,F. L. Pratt,S. Cottrell,D. T. Adroja,T. Basu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过 μSR（muon spin rotation and relaxation）研究 6H 钙钛矿（perovskite）Ba3LnRu2O9（Ln = Gd, Ho），揭示稀土各向异性（rare-earth anisotropy）在 4d-4f 耦合磁基态中的调控作用：Ho 体系呈多尺度非均匀磁态，Gd 体系近均匀反铁磁基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究稀土各向异性在 4d-4f 耦合体系磁基态中的作用，利用结构相似的 Ba3HoRu2O9 与 Ba3GdRu2O9 对比，以厘清稀土离子对磁基态的调控机制。

Method: 采用零场（ZF）与纵向场（LF）μSR 测量并结合 Redfield 分析，同时使用机器学习辅助的 μ 子占位（muon-site）与偶极场（dipolar-field）计算，对不同结构位点上的局域磁场与自旋涨落进行解析。

Result: Ba3HoRu2O9 在 TN1 = 46 K 与 TN2 = 10 K 发生两次连续磁序，表现为双组分指数弛豫、宽局域场分布、LF 解耦不完全及持续自旋动力学，存在约 46 K 与 10 K 两个磁能标；Ba3GdRu2O9 在 TN = 14.5 K 主要为单一磁序，呈高斯弛豫，符合窄静场分布。

Conclusion: 稀土离子是 Ba3LnRu2O9 中有效的调控参数，通过竞争的 4d-4f 磁相互作用，使基态由均匀磁态转变为复杂、多尺度的非均匀磁行为。

Abstract: We report a comprehensive muon spin rotation and relaxation (muSR) study of the hexagonal 6H-perovskites Ba3LnRu2O9 (Ln = Gd, Ho) to investigate the role of rare-earth anisotropy in the magnetic ground state of 4d-4f coupled systems. Ba3HoRu2O9 exhibits two successive magnetic orderings at TN1 = 46 K and TN2 = 10 K, leading to a complex magnetic ground state, whereas Ba3GdRu2O9, with Gd3+ (L = 0) S-state ions, shows primarily single magnetic ordering at TN = 14.5 K. Zero-field (ZF) and longitudinal-field (LF) muSR measurements reveal markedly different magnetic behavior despite their structural similarity. Ba3HoRu2O9 displays a multi-scale inhomogeneous magnetic state characterized by two-component exponential relaxation, broad local-field distributions, incomplete LF decoupling, and persistent spin dynamics. The muSR results identify two characteristic magnetic energy scales near 46 and 10 K, associated with correlated Ru-Ho interactions and quasistatic magnetic ordering, respectively. Redfield analysis further demonstrates strong local magnetic fields and distributed spin fluctuation timescales, confirming magnetic inhomogeneity. Machine-learning-assisted muon-site and dipolar-field calculations show that the broad field distribution naturally arises from muons sensing distinct coexisting magnetic domains at different structural sites. In contrast, Ba3GdRu2O9 exhibits a comparatively homogeneous antiferromagnetic ground state below 14.5 K, with Gaussian relaxation consistent with a narrow static field distribution. These results establish that the rare-earth ion in Ba3LnRu2O9 is a powerful tuning parameter, driving the ground state from homogeneous to complex, multi-scale inhomogeneous behavior through competing 4d-4f magnetic interactions.

</details>


### [174] [Quadratic XY-anisotropy correction to the spontaneous staggered magnetization of the gapped XYZ spin-$1/2$ chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12798)
*Felipe Taha Sant'Ana*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于八顶点解（eight-vertex solution）推导带隙 XYZ 自旋-1/2 链交错序参量对 XY 各向异性（XY anisotropy）主导二次响应的系数闭式，并验证其渐近与数值行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 八顶点解给出的交错序参量以椭圆变量表示时，哈密顿量耦合仅被隐式固定，缺乏对 XY 各向异性响应的显式闭式描述，故需在固定纵向各向异性下导出主导二次响应系数。

Method: 从 Baxter–Kelland–Jimbo–Miwa–Nakayashiki 极化公式（polarization formula）出发，追踪模数 nome 与交叉参数变量随 XY 各向异性的变化，并结合自旋链四阶微扰计算（无八顶点解输入）与无限 DMRG（infinite-DMRG）数值计算。

Result: 该系数为有理项加 Lambert 级数（Lambert series），严格为负且被纵向各向异性的有理函数上界约束，并在 Ising 极限下渐近于此上界；在 isotropic point 处按到该点距离的负五分之二次幂发散，次主导项亦可闭式给出；当 XY 各向异性按距离的四分之五次幂趋于零，微扰与未微扰序参量之比在标度变量下趋于高斯，且变量较小时二次截断可控。

Conclusion: 四阶微扰计算重现了强耦合主导行为，四个各向异性下的 infinite-DMRG 结果与闭式一致，支持所导出系数闭式的正确性。

Abstract: The staggered order parameter of the gapped XYZ spin-$1/2$ chain follows from the eight-vertex solution, but in elliptic variables that fix the Hamiltonian couplings only implicitly. We derive a closed form for the coefficient governing the leading quadratic response of the spontaneous staggered magnetization to the XY anisotropy at fixed longitudinal anisotropy in the gapped antiferromagnetic regime. Starting from the Baxter--Kelland--Jimbo--Miwa--Nakayashiki polarization formula, we show that at fixed longitudinal anisotropy both the modular nome and the crossing-parameter variable of the formula move with the XY anisotropy, and that the motion of the crossing-parameter variable reverses the sign of the expansion at fixed elliptic variables. The coefficient is a rational term plus a Lambert series. It is strictly negative and bounded above by a rational function of the longitudinal anisotropy, to which it is asymptotic in the Ising limit. At the isotropic point it diverges as the inverse five-halves power of the distance to that point, and since the Lambert sum has an asymptotic expansion with exponentially small remainder, the subleading terms of the divergence also follow in closed form. When the XY anisotropy vanishes as the five-fourths power of the distance to the isotropic point, the ratio of the perturbed to the unperturbed order parameter tends to a Gaussian in the scaling variable, and the quadratic truncation is controlled when that variable is small. A fourth-order perturbative calculation in the spin chain, with no input from the eight-vertex solution, reproduces the leading strong-coupling behaviour, and infinite-DMRG calculations at four anisotropies in the gapped regime are consistent with the closed form.

</details>


### [175] [Ground-State Phase Diagram of the Effective Model with Antiferromagnetic Nearest-Neighbor and Ferromagnetic Next-Nearest-Neighbor Interactions in 1/1 Periodic Approximants to Icosahedral Quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12840)
*Shinji Watanabe*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过完全枚举磁矩构型，确定了二十面体准晶1/1周期近似相上含反铁磁最近邻相互作用$J_1$与铁磁次近邻相互作用$J_2$的有效模型的基态相图，并识别出多种非共线亚铁磁与反铁磁序及其磁空间群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解稀土基1/1近似相中实验观测到的磁结构与磁畴，需要在包含竞争交换相互作用与磁各向异性的有效模型上确定其基态相图。

Method: 方法为对二十面体准晶1/1周期近似相（periodic approximant to icosahedral quasicrystal）上所有磁矩构型进行完全枚举（complete enumeration），考察反铁磁最近邻相互作用（antiferromagnetic nearest-neighbor interactions）$J_1$、铁磁次近邻相互作用（ferromagnetic next-nearest-neighbor interactions）$J_2$之比以及源于晶体电场（crystalline electric field）的磁各向异性（magnetic anisotropy）的影响。

Result: 结果显示，随$J_2/J_1$比值与磁各向异性变化，多种非共线亚铁磁序（non-collinear ferrimagnetic orders）与反铁磁序（antiferromagnetic orders）被稳定化，其磁空间群（magnetic space groups）被识别为$R\bar{3}$、$C2'/m'$、$Pm'm'2$与$I_{\rm P}m'\bar{3}'$；并揭示了各基态在简并（degeneracy）下的拓扑与磁学性质。

Conclusion: 结论表明，所获得的基态相图及简并基态的拓扑与磁学性质有助于理解稀土基1/1近似相中磁结构与磁畴的实验观测。

Abstract: By performing complete enumeration of all configurations of magnetic moments, we have determined the ground-state phase diagram of the effective model with antiferromagnetic nearest-neighbor interactions $J_1$ and ferromagnetic next-nearest-neighbor interactions $J_2$ on the 1/1 periodic approximant to icosahedral quasicrystal. Depending on the ratio of $J_2/J_1$ and the magnetic anisotropy arising from the crystalline electric field, various non-collinear ferrimagnetic orders and antiferromagnetic orders are shown to be stabilized, whose magnetic space groups are identified as $R\bar{3}$, $C2'/m'$, $Pm'm'2$ and $I_{\rm P}m'\bar{3}'$. We have revealed the topological as well as magnetic property of each ground state with degeneracy, which is useful to understand experimental observations of magnetic structures and domains in the rare-earth-based 1/1 approximants.

</details>


### [176] [Real-Time Relaxation Dynamics of Dark Excitons in Mirror-Symmetric Monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12996)
*Rishabh Saraswat,Mantasha Ansari,Rekha Verma,Sitangshu Bhattacharya*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作结合群论（group theory）与从头算多体微扰理论（ab initio many-body perturbation theory），推导平面 D₃h 单层中有限动量激子的镜面宇称 σ_h，并建立覆盖整个布里渊区（Brillouin zone）的精确激子-声子（exciton-phonon, exc-ph）选择定则，据此计算激子线宽并求解实时玻尔兹曼输运方程（Boltzmann transport equation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性只能决定哪些激子-声子散射通道"可以"存在，无法说明弛豫中的激子"实际"使用哪些通道。该工作旨在以宇称为切入点，将对称性约束转化为可跨整个布里渊区适用的精确选择定则，从而弥合这一缺口。

Method: 将群论与从头算多体微扰理论相结合，推导平面 D₃h 单层中有限动量激子的镜面宇称 σ_h，并构建在整个布里渊区严格成立的选择定则；随后应用这些定则计算激子线宽，并以单层 GaN 为测试体系求解实时玻尔兹曼输运方程，同时以单层 hBN 作为基准对照。

Result: 最低激子带的宇称沿一条闭合轮廓发生改变。偶宇称声子连接宇称相同的态，奇宇称声子连接宇称相反的态。基于这些定则得到激子线宽，并在单层 GaN 中给出实时玻尔兹曼输运方程的解，且与单层 hBN 进行了基准比较。

Conclusion: 激子-声子耦合的精确选择定则由激子镜面宇称 σ_h 决定，并在整个布里渊区成立；该框架可支撑激子线宽与实时输运的定量计算，并已在 GaN 与 hBN 单层的对比中得到验证。

Abstract: Symmetry governs which exciton-phonon (\textit{exc-ph}) scattering channels \textit{can} exist, but not which a relaxing exciton actually uses. Combining group theory with \textit{ab initio} many-body perturbation theory, we derive the mirror parity $σ_h$, of finite-momentum excitons in planar $D_{3h}$ monolayers, and form exact \textit{exc-ph} selection rules that hold across the whole Brillouin zone. The parity of the lowest exciton band changes across a closed contour. The even phonons connect states of the same parity, while odd phonons connect states of opposite parity. We use these rules to compute exciton linewidths and we solve the real-time Boltzmann transport equation for monolayer GaN as the test case and benchmark it against monolayer \textit{h}BN.

</details>


### [177] [Odd-Parity Crystalline Electric Field and Multipoles in Icosahedral Quasicrystal and Approximant: Magnetic Toroidal and Electric Dipoles in Ce-Based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13009)
*Shinji Watanabe,Syunta Furuya,Mitsuaki Kawamura*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作在点电荷模型基础上构建了Ce基二十面体准晶（quasicrystal, QC）及其近似晶体（approximant crystal, AC）中偶宇称与奇宇称的晶体电场（crystalline electric field, CEF），揭示4f-5d杂化波函数产生奇宇称多极（如电偶极与磁环偶极），并解释了1/1近似晶体中涡旋序与团簇型磁环矩、电磁效应及非共线磁结构的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准晶缺乏周期性原子排列，却具有区别于周期晶体与随机系统的独特结构序，其电子性质理解远未完备；同时Ce位点的局域反演对称性破缺可能引入常被忽略的奇宇称效应，本文旨在从奇宇称CEF角度揭示相关电子序与磁结构。

Method: 基于点电荷模型（point charge model），在Ce基二十面体准晶与近似晶体中推导偶宇称与奇宇称晶体电场；考虑Ce位点局域反演对称性破缺下4f与5d态的杂化波函数，分析其间产生的奇宇称多极；通过调控4f与5d能级使其接近，考察团簇磁环矩与各二十面体内磁环偶极的共振关系。

Result: 奇宇称CEF下的4f-5d杂化导致奇宇称多极（电偶极、磁环偶极）出现；1/1近似晶体中磁矩在各二十面体上旋流的涡旋序由团簇型磁环矩（cluster-type MT moment）表征，其铁性排列（ferroic alignment）产生电磁效应；调谐4f与5d能级接近时团簇磁环矩与各二十面体磁环偶极发生共振；二十面体准晶团簇基态稳定出非共线磁结构，其背后机制倾向于各二十面体间净磁环偶极的铁性相互作用。

Conclusion: 研究指出4f与5d能级可在价态转变（valence transition）或价态交叉（valence crossover）处趋于接近，而该现象在稀土基近似晶体与准晶中普遍存在，为理解准晶电子性质提供了奇宇称CEF与磁环多极的新视角。

Abstract: Quasicrystals (QCs) lack periodic arrangement of atoms but possess unique structural order distinct from periodic crystals and random systems. The electronic property is far from complete understanding. Here we have formulated not only even-parity but also odd-parity crystalline electric field (CEF) in the Ce-based icosahedral QC and approximant crystal (AC) on the basis of the point charge model. Under the odd-parity CEF due to local-inversion symmetry breaking at the Ce site, the hybrid wavefunction of 4f and 5d states at Ce is shown to give rise to the odd-parity multipoles such as electric dipole and magnetic toroidal (MT) dipole. We have found that the vortex order in the 1/1 AC where the magnetic moments on each icosahedron swirl is characterized by the cluster-type MT moment. The ferroic alignment of the cluster MT moments in the vortex order gives rise to the electro-magnetic effect. The cluster-MT moment is shown to be resonated with the MT dipole moments in each icosahedron by tuning the 4f energy level and 5d energy level so as to approach. We have revealed a noncollinear magnetic structure is stabilized in the ground state of the icosahedral QC cluster, behind which there exists underlying mechanism to prefer the ferroic interaction for the net MT dipole moments between the icosahedrons. We point out that the 4f and 5d energy levels can be made approach at the valence transition or the valence crossover, which is ubiquitous in the rare-earth based ACs and QCs.

</details>


### [178] [X-ray absorption spectroscopic studies of oxygen-deficient double perovskite oxide NdBaCo$_2$O$_{5+δ}$ ($δ\sim 0.65$)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13155)
*Himanshu Pant,S. Khalid,R. Bindu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作利用 XANES 光谱结合多重散射与 DFT+U 计算，研究层状双钙钛矿 NdBaCo₂O₅₊δ 中氧空位分布，发现基面（basal site）空位的存在，并将其与 Griffiths 相及变程跳跃导电机制相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A 位掺杂层状双钙钛矿氧化物中氧空位程度可导致阴离子空位有序（anion vacancy ordering）并形成不同超结构，因此需厘清氧空位分布与磁性、电荷输运之间的关联。

Method: 采用 X 射线吸收近边结构（XANES）光谱，并结合多重散射（multiple scattering）与基于密度泛函理论（DFT+U）的第一性原理计算，测量并分析 Co K 边、Ba 与 Nd L_III 边谱。

Result: 近边谱特征的数量与形状表明 δ 值更接近 0.75；Ba L_III 边确认 Ba 为 +2 价；Co K 边提示除顶端位点（apical site）外，基面位点（basal site）亦可能产生氧空位，与传统认知相反，该分布可解释 Griffiths 相（Griffith phase）与变程跳跃（variable range hopping）导电机制。

Conclusion: 氧空位在最佳水平时可提升氧离子迁移率（oxygen ion mobility），并有助于获得更灵敏、更快的氧传感器；研究结果有助于调控氧离子迁移与优化氧传感性能。

Abstract: The degree of oxygen vacancy in A-site-doped layered double perovskite oxides can lead to anion vacancy ordering, which subsequently results in distinct superstructures. We have studied oxygen deficient double perovskite oxide, NdBaCo$2$O$_{5+δ}$ ($δ\sim$ 0.65), utilising X-ray absorption near edge structure (XANES) spectroscopy complemented by multiple scattering and first-principles calculations based on density functional theory (DFT+U). The present results establish a direct correlation between oxygen-vacancy distribution, magnetic inhomogeneity, and charge transport in layered cobaltates. Our investigations on the Co K, Ba, and Nd $L_{\mathrm{III}}$ edges suggest that the near-edge spectra, based on the number and shape of the spectral features, indicate the value of $δ$ for the compound under study lies closer to 0.75. The analysis of the Ba $L_{\mathrm{III}}$ edge spectrum confirms the +2 oxidation state for Ba ions. The Co K-edge near-edge spectra suggest the possibility of oxygen vacancies arising at the basal sites in addition to the apical sites. This is counterintuitive to the common understanding that oxygen vacancies usually occur at the apical sites, i.e., in the lanthanide (Nd) layer in these compounds. Such a distribution of oxygen vacancies naturally explains the Griffith phase and the conduction mechanism of variable range hopping observed in the compound. The vacancy distribution, when at the optimum level, can also bring about easier oxygen ion mobility and a more sensitive and faster oxygen sensor. We believe that our results will help in tailoring oxygen-ion mobility and enhancing oxygen-sensing performance.

</details>


### [179] [Interplay of disorder and spin liquid physics in the triangular-lattice antiferromagnet YbZn2GaO5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13346)
*Hafsa Zeroual,Shams Sohel Islam,Toni Shiroka,Thomas James Hicken,Rabindranath Bag,Sara Haravifard,Hubertus Luetkens,Philippe Mendels,Edwin Kermarrec,Fabrice Bert*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究利用核磁共振（NMR）与缪子自旋弛豫（muSR）对三角晶格反铁磁体 YbZn2GaO5 进行微观探测，确认其存在 Ga/Zn 位点混合与局域晶体电场随机性，同时自旋动力学可一直持续至 30 mK 而无冻结迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: YbZn2GaO5 被视为结构理想的三角晶格反铁磁体及 U(1) Dirac 量子自旋液体（quantum spin-liquid, QSL）候选基态，但展宽的晶体电场（crystal electric field, CEF）激发暗示可能存在位点无序，其对本征自旋动力学的影响尚不明确。

Method: 采用 71Ga 核磁共振（nuclear magnetic resonance, NMR）与缪子自旋弛豫（muon spin relaxation, muSR）对 YbZn2GaO5 的结构与磁性进行微观表征。

Result: NMR 谱显示两个不等价 71Ga 位点以及宽的四极频率分布，表明 Ga3+/Zn2+ 位点混合与局域 CEF 随机性；自旋-晶格弛豫率在低至 1.3 K 呈幂律温度依赖，符合阻挫磁体中交换能标以下持续的 spin dynamics。muSR 显示自旋涨落持续至 30 mK，无自旋冻结迹象。

Conclusion: YbZn2GaO5 存在显著结构无序，但极低温下动态自旋涨落的持续可能表明 QSL 类激发在此无序下仍可存续。

Abstract: YbZn2GaO5 has recently emerged as a structurally ideal triangular-lattice antiferromagnet and a promising candidate for hosting a U(1) Dirac quantum spin-liquid (QSL) ground state. However, signatures of broadened crystal electric field (CEF) excitations have raised questions about potential site disorder and its impact on the intrinsic spin dynamics. In this study, we employ 71Ga nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) and muon spin relaxation (muSR) to microscopically probe both the structural and magnetic properties of YbZn2GaO5. NMR spectra reveal two inequivalent 71Ga sites and a broad distribution of quadrupolar frequencies, providing clear evidence of Ga$^{3+}$/Zn$^{2+}$ site mixing and local CEF randomness. The NMR spin-lattice relaxation rate exhibits, down to 1.3~K, a power-law temperature dependence, consistent with persisting spin dynamics below the exchange energy scale in a frustrated magnet. Complementary muSR measurements further show that the spin dynamics persist down to 30mK, with no signs of spin freezing. Together, these results establish that YbZn2GaO5 presents substantial structural disorder, while the persistence of dynamic spin fluctuations down to the lowest temperatures may indicate the survival of QSL-like excitations despite such disorder.

</details>


### [180] [Temperature evolution of spin dynamics in extended Kitaev models within an Abrikosov fermion theory beyond mean field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13401)
*Daiki Sasamoto,Joji Nasu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作建立有限温部分子平均场理论（parton mean-field theory），结合随机相近似（random phase approximation, RPA），用于计算 Kitaev 磁体的动力学自旋结构因子（dynamical spin structure factor）。方法在纯 Kitaev 模型中重现与连续时间量子蒙特卡洛（continuous-time quantum Monte Carlo）定性一致的谱特征，并将其推广至非 Kitaev 相互作用与磁场诱导的均匀铁磁态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $S=1/2$ Kitaev 模型虽可精确求解并体现自旋分数化（spin fractionalization），但真实 Kitaev 磁体中的非 Kitaev 相互作用破坏可积性（integrability），使其有限温动力学难以计算。因此需要一个能够处理交换相互作用并计算有限温动力学响应的近似理论框架。

Method: 基于 Abrikosov 费米子表示（Abrikosov-fermion representation）构建有限温部分子平均场理论，并通过随机相近似（RPA）纳入交换相互作用效应，进而计算动力学自旋结构因子。

Result: 对纯 Kitaev 模型，方法重现有限低能处强且近乎无色散的峰及高能连续谱（continuum），并再现动力学自旋结构因子的动量依赖与温度演化（含低能谱权下移），与连续时间量子蒙特卡洛结果定性一致。连续谱收缩被归因于温度诱导的键振幅（bond amplitudes）减小，使部分子能带变窄并压缩双部分子相空间。对铁磁 Kitaev 耦合，低能峰能量在铁磁 Heisenberg 相互作用下于 $Γ$ 点最小，在反铁磁相互作用下于该点最大。在铁磁 Kitaev-$Γ$ 模型中，静态响应出现有限动量软化（finite-momentum softening），表明向数值研究报道的周期三磁序（period-three magnetic order）的不稳定性。对 $[111]$ 磁场选择的均匀铁磁态，出现宽部分子连续谱与类磁振子（magnonlike）色散结构共存，谱特征与自旋子到磁振子（spinon-to-magnon）渡越（crossover）一致。

Conclusion: 该有限温部分子平均场加 RPA 框架可在定性层面再现 Kitaev 模型动力学的关键特征，并为非可积 Kitaev 磁体的有限温谱学、相互作用诱导不稳定性及自旋子-磁振子渡越提供统一描述。

Abstract: The $S=1/2$ Kitaev model provides an exactly solvable setting in which spin fractionalization produces itinerant Majorana fermions coupled to an emergent $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge field. In realistic Kitaev magnets, however, non-Kitaev interactions break integrability and make their finite-temperature dynamics difficult to calculate. We develop a finite-temperature parton mean-field theory based on the Abrikosov-fermion representation and incorporate the effects of exchange interactions through the random phase approximation to calculate the dynamical spin structure factor. For the pure Kitaev model, the method reproduces a strong, nearly dispersionless peak at a finite low energy together with a broad continuum at higher energies. It also reproduces the momentum dependence of the dynamical spin structure factor and its temperature evolution, including the downward shift of the low-energy spectral weight, in qualitative agreement with continuous-time quantum Monte Carlo calculations. Within the present theory, the contraction of the continuum is traced to the temperature-induced reduction of the bond amplitudes, which narrows the parton bands and compresses the two-parton phase space. For ferromagnetic Kitaev coupling, the energy of the low-energy peak is minimized at the $Γ$ point for a ferromagnetic Heisenberg interaction and maximized there for an antiferromagnetic interaction. In the ferromagnetic Kitaev-$Γ$ model, the static response develops a finite-momentum softening, indicating an instability toward the period-three magnetic order reported in numerical studies. For uniform-ferromagnetic states selected by a $[111]$ magnetic field, we find that a broad parton continuum coexists with magnonlike dispersive structures, producing spectral features consistent with a spinon-to-magnon crossover.

</details>


### [181] [A new approach to the Kondo effect: sophomore's dream function and Lee-Yang zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13666)
*V. Yu. Irkhin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于推广“大二梦想积分”的有效配分函数（effective partition function）解析处理近藤问题（Kondo problem），将近藤交叉（Kondo crossover）描述为复平面上近似等距 Lee-Yang 零点阶梯的穿越，求和给出以 Gamma 函数为核心的普适交叉骨架，并经 Euler-Maclaurin 展开与 Fermi 液体理论衔接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上近藤交叉被视为实轴上的奇异性，该文旨在提供一种不依赖实轴奇异性、可解析处理的替代图像，并用统一的普适函数刻画完整交叉过程。

Method: 构建推广 sophomore's dream 积分的有效配分函数；将交叉表示为复平面中近似等距的 Lee-Yang 零点阶梯；对该阶梯求和得到 Gamma 函数 Γ(α + T_K/(2πT))；用 Euler-Maclaurin 展开通过 Bernoulli 数联系 Nozières Fermi 液体理论；给出双截断（two-cutoff）公式及温度空间 Fano 线型描述，并处理漂移修正。

Result: 得到 Γ(α + T_K/(2πT)) 作为普适交叉骨架，偏移量 α = 1/2 由 Friedel 求和规则固定；双截断公式以基本单参数形式描述完整交叉，其形式为温度空间 Fano 线型，即 STM 所观测能量空间 Fano 共振的热平均。

Conclusion: 该方法可推广至 SU(N) Coqblin-Schrieffer 模型、欠屏蔽（underscreened）与 Anderson 杂质模型以及近藤晶格（Kondo lattices），表明该解析框架具有一定的普适性。

Abstract: We develop an analytical approach to the Kondo problem based on an effective partition function that generalizes the sophomore's dream integral. The Kondo crossover is represented as a passage through an approximately equidistant ladder of its Lee-Yang zeros in the complex plane, rather than as a singularity on the real axis. Summation over this ladder yields the Gamma function $Γ(α+ T_K/(2 πT))$ as the universal crossover backbone, where the offset $α=1/2$ is fixed by the Friedel sum rule. The Euler-Maclaurin expansion connects the discrete zero structure to the Nozieres Fermi-liquid theory through Bernoulli numbers. A two-cutoff formula provides a compact, essentially one-parameter description of the full crossover in terms of a temperature-space Fano lineshape which is a thermal average of the energy-space Fano resonance observed in STM. Drift corrections are treated. The approach extends to $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ Coqblin-Schrieffer, underscreened and Anderson impurity models, and Kondo lattices.

</details>
